### [1] [The conditional superiority of fast silicon sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14079)
*Nickolas Hock Yuen Lam,Ji Xuan Voo,Xiangyu Ma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了快速与慢速两种硅采样（silicon sampling）模式在算法保真度上的表现，基于新加坡全国代表性调查数据。结果显示，快速硅采样在计算效率上显著更优，同时在算法保真度上单调优于慢速模式，但硅采样整体仍处于早期发展阶段，仅能适度估计总体均值，对意见方差和潜在语境空间的刻画仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅采样有时能产生出人意料地好的总体估计，但尚不清楚快速执行是否会削弱这种保真度。作者希望在既有工作的基础上系统比较快速与慢速硅采样模式之间的算法保真度差异。

Method: 研究基于新加坡全国代表性调查受访者样本，扩展并评估了正在进行中的硅采样研究，通过对比"快速"与"慢速"两种硅采样模式的算法保真度（algorithmic fidelity）来进行实证分析。

Result: 研究发现，使用当代前沿模型的硅采样仍属早期发展阶段，应谨慎使用；硅样本仅能适度忠实地估计总体均值，但会低估意见方差并扭曲人类意见背后的潜在语境空间。在此限制条件下，快速硅采样模式在计算资源和运行时间上显著更高效，且在算法保真度上单调优于传统慢速模式。

Conclusion: 快速硅采样是相对更优的选择，在效率与保真度两个维度上均优于慢速模式；但鉴于硅采样整体仍存在明显局限，该方法目前只应在充分认识其约束的前提下谨慎使用。

Abstract: Silicon sampling can produce surprisingly good population estimates at times. Does doing it fast attenuate such fidelity? In this study, we extend and assess ongoing work in silicon sampling by comparing the algorithmic fidelity of "fast" and "slow" modes of silicon sampling among a nationally representative sample of Singaporean survey respondents. We find that silicon sampling with contemporary frontier models remains a method in early development to be used only with great caution. While silicon samples are able to produce moderately faithful estimates of population means, they continue to understate opinion variance and distort the latent contextual space behind human opinions. Conditional on such limitations, we find "fast" modes of silicon sampling to be relatively superior to traditional "slow" modes of silicon sampling. Fast silicon sampling is significantly more efficient in compute resources and run-time while being monotonically superior to slower modes of sampling in algorithmic fidelity.

</details>

### [2] [Thickness-dependent secondary-electron emission from suspended MoS$_2$ membranes in the helium ion microscope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13688)
*Cyan Kim,David Lister,Philip Jackle,Karen L. Kavanagh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了氦离子显微镜中的二次电子发射对悬浮纳米厚MoS2薄膜厚度的敏感性，通过电子能量损失谱独立测量厚度并与之关联。研究发现总二次电子信号在40-55 nm厚度处出现峰值，正反面信号比达4.7，并提出了非对称逸出模型以解释这一响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在氦离子显微镜（HIM）中，当样品薄到足以让氦离子穿透且底表面发射的二次电子被收集时，二次电子发射对膜厚变得敏感。作者旨在建立二次电子信号与膜厚之间的定量关联，为悬浮薄膜提供快速厚度筛选手段，并验证低能离子-固体相互作用模型。

Method: 将悬浮在lacey碳上的纳米厚MoS2薄片的二次电子总强度与通过电子能量损失谱（EELS）独立测量的厚度进行关联；应用SRIM电离分布模拟，建立非对称二次电子逸出模型（后端逸出深度大于前端），以复现实验结果。

Result: 二次电子响应在40-55 nm厚度处出现峰值，正反面信号比可达4.7。模型拟合得到的有效逸出侧逸出深度约为10 nm，约为假定的入射侧逸出深度（2 nm）的五倍，表明沉积能量到达出射表面的效率远高于入射表面，或SRIM模型中的能量沉积分布受到通道效应等因素影响。

Conclusion: 该相关性为悬浮薄膜提供了一种快速厚度筛选方法，并为在薄材料中测试低能离子-固体相互作用模型提供了途径。

Abstract: Secondary-electron (SE) emission in the helium ion microscope (HIM) becomes sensitive to membrane thickness when the sample is thin enough for He-ion transmission and when the SEs emitted from the bottom surface are collected. We correlated the total SE intensity of suspended, nanometer-thick MoS$_2$ flakes on lacey carbon with thickness measured independently by electron energy-loss spectroscopy. The response peaks at 40-55 nm, with an apparent back-to-front SE signal ratio reaching 4.7. The peaked, thickness-dependent component is attributed primarily to SE emission at the bottom surface of the flake, rather than to transmitted ions striking instrument surfaces. Applying SRIM ionization profiles, an asymmetric SE-escape model with a longer escape depth on the exit side reproduces the response. We find an effective exit-side escape depth of approximately 10 nm, five times the assumed 2 nm entrance value, suggesting that deposited energy reaches the exit surface far more efficiently than the entrance surface or that the SRIM model's energy deposition profile is shifted by an effect such as channeling. The correlation provides a rapid thickness screen for suspended membranes and a route to testing low-energy ion-solid interaction models in thin materials.

</details>


### [3] [Stochastic twinning in confined volumes of Mg: Insights from in-situ micromechanical testing and atomistic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13703)
*Hexin Wang,Fatim Zahra Mouhib,Chunhua Tian,Sang-Hyeok Lee,Henry Ovri,Julien Guénolé,Sandra Korte-Kerzel,Talal Al-Samman,Zhuocheng Xie*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究结合定点微柱压缩实验与原子尺度模拟，揭示了镁中{10-12}孪生在小尺度下表现出随机性的原子级起源：孪生被分解为高应力、shuffle辅助的形核与纵向传播机制，以及低应力、由位错滑动（disconnection glide）控制的横向增厚机制；测量的屈服应力离散性源于局部缺陷环境（缺陷的存在、分布与形态）驱动的竞争性孪生路径的随机选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块体镁中孪生通常表现为低临界应力的确定性响应，但在受限体积（confined volumes）下表现出显著的离散性，这严重阻碍了小尺度力学行为的预测。因此，理解这种随机性的物理起源成为实现小尺度镁力学行为可靠预测的关键前提。

Method: 将定点微柱压缩实验（site-specific micropillar compression）与原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）相结合。实验中沿<a>轴压缩观察应力-应变曲线中的离散应力降与{10-12}孪生活动对应；模拟进一步将孪生分解为两种机制性过程，并通过变形后表征（post-mortem characterization）将机制认知与实验测量的屈服应力离散性联系起来。

Result: 实验表明<a>轴压缩下塑性变形由{10-12}孪生主导，每次离散应力降对应一个孪晶的激活与快速扩展。原子模拟将孪生分为两种机制：高应力、shuffle辅助的形核与纵向传播机制，以及低应力、由位错滑动控制的横向增厚机制。测量屈服应力的离散性源于在竞争性孪生路径间的随机选择，该选择受局部缺陷景观（缺陷的存在、分布与形态）控制。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了镁小尺度随机孪生的原子级基础，并为塑性受离散激活事件控制的材料提供了一个通用分析框架。

Abstract: Tensile twinning plays a central role in accommodating <c>-axis plasticity in Mg. In bulk Mg, twinning typically shows a relatively deterministic response with a low critical stress, whereas in confined volumes it exhibits pronounced scatter, complicating the prediction of small-scale mechanical behavior. In this study, we investigate the origin of this stochasticity by combining site-specific micropillar compression with atomistic simulations. Experiments show that under <a>-axis compression, plastic deformation is dominated by {10-12} twinning, with each discrete stress drop in the stress-strain response marking the activation and rapid advance of a twin. Atomistic simulations further separate twinning into two mechanistic regimes: nucleation and longitudinal propagation occur in a high-stress, shuffle-assisted regime, whereas lateral thickening proceeds in a low-stress regime controlled by disconnection glide. Linking these mechanistic insights with post-mortem characterization of deformed pillars demonstrates that the scatter in measured yield stresses arises from stochastic selection among competing twinning pathways, governed by the local defect landscape (presence, distribution, and morphology of pre-existing defects). Overall, this work identifies an atomistic basis for size-dependent stochastic twinning in Mg and provides a general framework for materials whose plasticity is controlled by discrete activation events.

</details>


### [4] [Multiscale Correlation of Morphological, Chemical, and Optical Properties in p-Type Porous Silicon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13802)
*Arturo Ramírez-Porras,Isaac Prado-Bermúdez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了电化学加工条件（电流密度、蚀刻时间、氢氟酸浓度）对多孔硅（porous silicon）形态学、化学及光学性质的影响，利用二十个样品进行表征，发现单一描述符无法充分刻画多孔硅的多尺度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多孔硅的电化学加工参数与其最终物理化学性质之间的关联尚缺乏系统性理解，作者旨在通过控制变量实验建立加工条件与材料特性之间的对应关系，并检验是否存在单一描述符可以全面刻画其行为。

Method: 从硼掺杂(100)晶向晶体硅（crystalline silicon）制备二十个多孔硅样品，调控电流密度、蚀刻时间及氢氟酸浓度三个加工参数；采用扫描电子显微镜（scanning electron microscopy）、傅里叶变换红外光谱（Fourier-transform infrared spectroscopy）和光致发光（photoluminescence）对样品进行表征；使用包含局域表面态跃迁的四组分量子线/量子点（quantum-wire/quantum-dot）模型对校正后的光致发光光谱进行拟合。

Result: 二维孔隙率仅中等程度变化，而多孔层厚度随蚀刻时间和电流密度增加而增大；氧化硅与氢化硅比值变化相对较小；提取的量子线和量子点尺寸随电流密度增加而系统性减小，但375 nm激发下的光致发光峰位对加工条件无明确的单调依赖关系。

Conclusion: 结果表明没有任何单一描述符能够充分刻画多孔硅的多尺度行为，需综合考虑多个加工参数与多个表征维度才能理解其结构与光学性质。

Abstract: We report a systematic study of the correlations among electrochemical processing conditions and the morphological, chemical, and optical properties of porous silicon (pSi). Twenty pSi samples were fabricated from boron-doped (100) crystalline silicon by varying current density, etching time, and hydrofluoric-acid concentration. Scanning electron microscopy, Fourier-transform infrared spectroscopy, and photoluminescence were used to characterize the resulting structures. The two-dimensional porosity varied only moderately, whereas porous-layer thickness increased with etching time and current density. The silicon oxide to silicon hydride ratio showed comparatively small variations. Corrected photoluminescence spectra were fitted with a four-component quantum-wire/quantum-dot model including localized surface-state transitions. The extracted quantum-wire and quantum-dot dimensions decreased systematically with increasing current density, while the photoluminescence maximum under 375 nm excitation showed no clear monotonic dependence on processing conditions. The results demonstrate that no single descriptor adequately captures the multiscale behavior of porous silicon.

</details>


### [5] [SPEAR: Structure Property Explainability with Attention Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13826)
*Aditya Raghavan,Utkarsh Pratiush,Dalton A. Pearl,Jade Holliman,Katharine Page,Philip D Rack,Sergei V Kalinin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出SPEAR（结构-性能可解释性与注意力正则化）框架，通过在学习过程中约束注意力分布来提升基于光谱和衍射数据回归模型的稳定性、选择性和物理可解释性，在合成基准和实验X射线衍射数据上均验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习虽被广泛用于从光谱和衍射数据学习结构-性能关系，但其在材料发现中的应用常受限于模型预测的可解释性差。尽管注意力机制通常被视为天然可解释的，但未正则化的注意力可能产生不稳定、碎片化或强度驱动的归因模式，模糊了关系的物理本源。

Method: SPEAR通过在训练过程中约束注意力分布来提升其稳定性、选择性和物理可解释性。具体而言，SPEAR在基于注意力的回归模型中引入一个可学习的温度参数（learnable temperature）以控制注意力集中度，并施加一个平滑性惩罚（smoothness penalty）以强制相邻光谱位置之间的连贯性，将注意力视为一个可学习的解释性对象而非事后可视化工具。

Result: 在具有已知生成结构的合成光谱基准上，注意力正则化产生了平滑、连续且与因果特征对齐的归因轮廓，同时保持预测精度。在实验应用方面，对组合稀土锆酸盐薄膜库的X射线衍射数据进行分析时，正则化模型选择性地强调物理相关的衍射特征，并将特征重要性从原始峰强度中解耦。识别的反射促使重新评估早期结构分析，揭示了220峰位置、容纳阳离子尺寸无序的四方畸变以及局部热导率之间的相关性。

Conclusion: 注意力正则化为可解释的结构-性能回归提供了一种原则性的训练约束，能够在不牺牲预测性能的前提下产生具有机理意义的解释。

Abstract: Machine learning is increasingly used to learn structure property relationships from spectroscopic and diffraction data, yet its adoption in materials discovery is often limited by poor interpretability of model predictions. Although attention mechanisms are frequently treated as inherently explainable, unregularized attention can yield unstable, fragmented, or intensity driven attribution patterns that obscure the physical origin of these relationships. Here we introduce SPEAR (Structure Property Explainability with Attention Regularization), a framework that constrains attention distributions during training to improve their stability, selectivity, and physical interpretability. SPEAR augments attention based regression with a learnable temperature that controls attention concentration and a smoothness penalty that enforces coherence across neighboring spectral positions, treating attention as a learnable explanatory object rather than a post hoc visualization. Using synthetic spectral benchmarks with known generative structure, we show that attention regularization produces smooth, contiguous attribution profiles aligned with causal features while preserving predictive accuracy. Applied to experimental X ray diffraction data from a combinatorial rare earth zirconate thin film library, the regularized model selectively emphasizes physically relevant diffraction features and decouples feature importance from raw peak intensity. The reflection it identified prompted a reassessment of our earlier structural analysis, revealing a correlation between the 220 peak position, the tetragonal distortion that accommodates cation size disorder, and the local thermal conductivity. Attention regularization therefore provides a principled training constraint for explainable structure property regression, yielding mechanistically meaningful explanations without sacrificing predictive performance.

</details>


### [6] [Realizing record-high transverse thermoelectric figure of merit at room temperature in artificially tilted multilayers based on high power factor NiFe alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13830)
*Yebin Lee,Fuyuki Ando,Takamasa Hirai,Keisuke Hirata,Kota Hasegawa,Ken-ichi Uchida*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道了在Ni50Fe50/Bi0.2Sb1.8Te3基人工倾斜多层膜（ATML）中实现室温外加磁场下的横向热电优值（figure of merit）z_yx·T=0.36，为横向热电材料的实际应用提供了可行路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纵向热电材料受结构限制，而横向热电转换器件结构灵活，但此前在无外磁场条件下难以实现具有竞争力的室温热电性能。

Method: 利用Ni50Fe50合金的高纵向功率因子（power factor）以及n型Ni50Fe50与p型Bi0.2Sb1.8Te3之间在电学和热输运性质上的显著差异，构建各向异性结构，同时实现高电导率、大横向热电势和低热导率，并通过直接测量各热电输运参数验证z_yx·T。

Result: 在室温下测得Ni50Fe50/Bi0.2Sb1.8Te3基ATML的z_yx·T为0.36，与解析预测值完全一致，归因于界面处极低的接触电阻和热阻。

Conclusion: 这些结果为实现室温横向热电材料的实际应用铺平了道路。

Abstract: Transverse thermoelectric conversion using artificially tilted multilayers (ATMLs) offers a versatile device architecture that circumvents the structural limitations of conventional longitudinal thermoelectrics. However, achieving competitive room-temperature thermoelectric performance without an external magnetic field remains a critical challenge. Here, we report a record-high transverse thermoelectric figure of merit $z_{yx}T$ of 0.36 in Ni$_{50}$Fe$_{50}$/Bi$_{0.2}$Sb$_{1.8}$Te$_{3}$-based ATML at room temperature without an external magnetic field. Leveraging the longitudinal high power factor in a Ni$_{50}$Fe$_{50}$ alloy and the sharp contrast in electrical and thermal transport properties between $n$-type Ni$_{50}$Fe$_{50}$ and $p$-type Bi$_{0.2}$Sb$_{1.8}$Te$_{3}$, we engineer an anisotropic structure that simultaneously exploits high electrical conductivity, large transverse thermopower, and low thermal conductivity to maximize $z_{yx}T$ in ATML. Through the direct measurements of these thermoelectric transport parameters, we obtained $z_{yx}T$ of 0.36 in Ni$_{50}$Fe$_{50}$/Bi$_{0.2}$Sb$_{1.8}$Te$_{3}$-based ATML, which is in excellent agreement with the analytical prediction of 0.36 owing to the low interfacial electrical and thermal resistances at the Ni$_{50}$Fe$_{50}$/Bi$_{0.2}$Sb$_{1.8}$Te$_{3}$ junctions. These results pave the way for the practical implementation of transverse thermoelectric materials around room temperature.

</details>


### [7] [Autferroics-based true random number generators with enhanced performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13838)
*Jun-Jie Zhang,Shuai Dong,Boris I. Yakobson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出利用自铁电材料（autferroics）作为多铁材料（multiferroics）的分支，构建物理熵驱动的真随机数发生器（true random number generators）。相比传统基于磁性隧道结的实现方式，自铁电隧道结凭借其跷跷板型磁电耦合带来的独特能量景观，显著增强了随机数生成的性能，并通过标准统计检验验证，同时具备多场可调性和内在多态随机性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理熵驱动的真随机数发生器对于新兴概率计算范式至关重要，但基于磁性隧道结（magnetic tunneling junctions）的传统实现已受限于固有权衡和弱可调性，达到性能瓶颈，亟需新型材料体系以突破这一限制。

Method: 提出自铁电（autferroics）体系——多铁材料的分支——用于构建真随机数发生器，利用其强跷跷板型磁电耦合（seesaw-type magnetoelectricity）产生的独特能量景观来增强随机数生成的性能。

Result: 基于自铁电隧道结（autferroic tunneling junctions）的随机数发生器通过标准统计检验（standard statistical tests），性能得到显著增强；同时展现出多场可调性（multi-field tunability）和内在多态随机性（intrinsic multi-state randomness）。

Conclusion: 自铁电基随机数发生器可为随机电路（stochastic circuits）中复数运算和量子混合态概率分布模拟提供高效的硬件实现途径，为概率计算范式开辟了新的材料平台。

Abstract: Physical entropy-driven true random number generators are essential for emerging probabilistic computing paradigms, but conventional implementations based on magnetic tunneling junctions have reached their performance plateaus limited by inherent tradeoffs and weak tunability. Here, autferroics, a sister branch of multiferroics, is proposed to construct true random number generators. Benefiting from its unique energy landscape due to strong seesaw-type magnetoelectricity, the performance of true random number generation can be significantly enhanced in autferroic tunneling junctions, verified by passing standard statistical tests. Furthermore, autferroics-based random number generators can exhibit multi-field tunability and intrinsic multi-state randomness, opening an avenue for efficient hardware realization of the complex-number arithmetic and simulation of probability distributions of quantum mixed states within stochastic circuits.

</details>


### [8] [Accurate and efficient calculation of atomic forces in solids with non-self-consistent hybrid functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14147)
*Damian Contant,Maria Hellgren*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

Abstract: Hybrid functionals are routinely employed self-consistently within the generalized Kohn-Sham framework. Here, we investigate the advantages of non-self-consistent hybrid functional calculations, focusing on the evaluation of atomic forces. The analytical force terms that arise due to non-self-consistency are computed using density functional perturbation theory (DFPT), as implemented within the Quantum ESPRESSO distribution. A non-self-consistent hybrid force calculation thus consists of self-consistent DFPT calculations with a local or semi-local functional and a single evaluation of the Fock exchange operator. Moreover, results for structural parameters are barely affected by self-consistency, and vibrational frequencies are typically agreeing within 0.5%, thus making non-self-consistent calculations an interesting alternative.

Thus, our results elucidate low-energy spin dynamics arising from combined crystal-field anisotropy and exchange interactions, with the persistent low-energy excitation providing a microscopic pathway for thermally activated spin relaxation. Abstract: Understanding how lattice dynamics mediate polaron hopping is essential for designing multifunctional correlated oxides. Experimental evidence of dispersive phonon-driven polaron hopping is scarce. INS measurements show significant dispersive phonon excitations at 21, 33, and 47 meV, implying collective lattice dynamics that enable delocalized polaron propagation via coupled charge-spin-phonon interactions. Dispersive phonons couple to charge carriers and promote correlated NiO6 lattice distortions, facilitating delocalized polaron hopping. Machine-learning phonon calculations replicate the experimentally observed phonon excitations and demonstrate lattice instability, which is compatible with dynamic local distortions caused by polaron production. These findings provide microscopic evidence for a coupled charge-spin-phonon mechanism that mediates polaron hopping in rutile oxide systems.

Simulating these highly anharmonic regimes is notoriously challenging, as methods such as molecular dynamics suffer from a critical slowdown near the transition point, while the harmonic approximation fails dramatically at saddle points of the energy landscape stabilized by quantum or thermal ionic fluctuations. This work introduces a new approach, based on the variational free-energy principle, to predict critical long-range behavior and dynamical spectra in strongly anharmonic systems, even when quantum ionic fluctuations dominate. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the method by computing the full free-energy Hessian and the critical temperature in a supercell with 1080 atoms. From a single evaluation, it returns the equation of state of a crystal and locates phase transitions among competing branches, including dynamically stabilized phases. </details>

### [18] [Asymptotically complete free-energy dissipation: a coarse MLSI holds at any positive temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13677)
*Jonas Köppl,Yannic Steenbeck*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文针对经典伊辛格劳伯（Ising Glauber）动力学，证明了自由能（free energy）在任意正温度下渐近完全耗散至平衡态自由能，方法是通过引入一种粗粒化修正对数苏博列夫不等式（coarse modified log-Sobolev inequality）。该结果涵盖了此前未解决的相共存区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的教学中认为非平衡系统耦合到固定温度热浴会随时间演化到热力学平衡，自由能单调下降。然而自由能仅具下半连续性（lower semicontinuity），因此"自由能是否渐近趋于平衡态自由能"这一问题并非显而易见。此前除了利用经典函数不等式可处理的唯一性区域外，这个问题并未被严格解决。

Method: 作者引入一种粗粒化修正对数苏博列夫不等式（coarse modified log-Sobolev inequality），该不等式在任意正温度下均成立，尤其适用于相共存（phase-coexistence）区域。

Result: 证明了经典伊辛格劳伯动力学中自由能随时间的渐近完全耗散（asymptotically complete dissipation），即自由能收敛至平衡态自由能。

Conclusion: 这一结果为相共存区域中自由能渐近耗散问题提供了首个严格证明，填补了此前仅唯一性区域可解的理论空白。

Abstract: Everybody learns in school that an out-of-equilibrium system coupled to a heat bath at a fixed temperature evolves to thermodynamic equilibrium as time goes on, and the free energy will only decrease on its way there. At least since Holley's 1971 work, mathematicians know this too, in the modest context of classical Ising Glauber dynamics. But does the free energy also asymptotically decrease to the free energy of an equilibrium state? A typical school kid would say "yes, of course", but since the free energy is only lower semicontinuous, this question is less straightforward than it initially seems. To the best of our knowledge, apart from the uniqueness regime, where one can make use of classical functional inequalities, this question has not previously been resolved rigorously. We prove the asymptotically complete dissipation of the free energy for the classical Ising Glauber dynamics by introducing a coarse modified log-Sobolev inequality, which holds at every positive temperature, in particular in the phase-coexistence regime.

</details>


### [19] [Non-Shattering at and Above the Dynamical Temperature in the Spherical Pure p-Spin Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14369)
*Taegyun Kim*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文针对球状纯p自旋玻璃（spherical pure p-spin glasses）中的碎裂（shattering）概念，在重叠参数q和逆温度β的特定范围内排除了碎裂现象，部分解决了Ben Arous和Jagannath提出的猜想1，并提出了证明非碎裂的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ben Arous和Jagannath引入了球形纯p自旋玻璃中关于重叠参数q的碎裂（shattering）概念，本文旨在确定碎裂发生与不发生的参数区域，以部分解决该领域的一个未决猜想（Conjecture 1）。

Method: 证明结合了两种关键技术：在首个参数区间内利用确定性的N+1界（deterministic N+1 bound）处理不相交带（disjoint bands）；在第二个区间内利用一般p态的符号律（sign law）表明这些带的总标记权重（marked weight）具有次主导自由能（subdominant free energy）。此外，球面编码界（spherical-code bound）与Hölder不等式提供了额外的q依赖障碍。

Result: 对每个p≥3且0<β≤β_sh(p)，当q≤2^(-1/2)或q>√((p-2)/(p-1))时排除碎裂。当p=3时，这两个区间已覆盖所有固定q∈(0,1)，因此在任何T≥T_sh时景观均不发生碎裂；当p≥4时，未覆盖的情形局限于2^(-1/2)<q≤√((p-2)/(p-1))且√(log2)<β≤β_sh(p)。此外，球面编码界与Hölder不等式排除了0<β≤√(log2)范围内所有固定重叠q的碎裂。

Conclusion: 本文部分解决了Ben Arous和Jagannath的猜想1，使碎裂的排除范围大幅扩展，并为证明非碎裂提供了新的技术途径，尤其对p=3情形给出了完整的否定结论。

Abstract: We consider the notion of shattering introduced by Ben Arous and Jagannath for spherical pure $p$-spin glasses with overlap $q$. For every $p\geq 3$ and $0<β\leqβ_{\mathrm{sh}}(p)$, we rule out shattering whenever $q\leq2^{-1/2}$ or $q>\sqrt{(p-2)/(p-1)}$. The proof combines a deterministic $N+1$ bound for disjoint bands in the first range with a general-$p$ sign law showing that their total marked weight has subdominant free energy in the second. A spherical-code bound and Hölder's inequality give an additional $q$-dependent obstruction; in particular, they rule out every fixed overlap for $0<β\leq\sqrt{\log2}$. For $p=3$, the first two ranges already exhaust every fixed $q\in(0,1)$, so the landscape is not shattered at any $T\geq T_{\mathrm{sh}}$. For $p\geq4$, the cases not covered by our criteria are confined to $2^{-1/2}<q\leq\sqrt{(p-2)/(p-1)}$ and $\sqrt{\log2}<β\leqβ_{\mathrm{sh}}(p)$. In particular, this paper partially resolves Conjecture 1 of the paper above and also suggests new methods to show non-shattering.

</details>


### [20] [Gradient Gibbs measures with non-convex potentials and the universality class of the Gaussian Free Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14526)
*Simon Buchholz,Codina Cotar,Florian Schweiger*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类广泛的梯度界面模型（gradient interface models），证明了其标度极限为高斯自由场（Gaussian Free Field, GFF），并将此前仅限于严格凸势能的结果推广到一大类非凸势能及任意温度，同时建立了Brascamp-Lieb不等式与无维数Poincaré不等式。核心创新在于将界面表示为具有严格凸势能的梯度界面模型的混合，扩展了Biskup-Spohn的想法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前关于梯度界面模型标度极限（scaling limit）为高斯自由场的结果主要依赖于势能函数的严格凸性（strict convexity），适用范围有限；本文旨在将这一结果推广到更广泛的非凸势能（non-convex potentials）和任意温度（arbitrary temperatures）情形，填补该方向的理论空白。

Method: 在势能函数满足偶函数、$V'(s)\ge αs$（$s\in[0,\infty)$）以及$-M\le V''\le C$等一般性条件下，建立了Helffer-Sjöstrand表示，并将界面表示为具有严格凸势能的梯度界面模型的混合。这一表示方法扩展了Biskup-Spohn此前仅在中等逆温度$\beta=1$下考虑高斯混合的思路，是本文的核心新贡献。

Result: 证明了模型的标度极限为高斯自由场，且协方差（covariance）的衰减速率与高斯自由场相同；此外还证明了Brascamp-Lieb不等式和无维数Poincaré不等式（dimension-free Poincaré inequalities）对该类模型成立。

Conclusion: 本文通过构造严格凸势能模型的混合表示，成功将高斯自由场标度极限等结果从严格凸势能情形推广到一大类非凸势能和任意温度，拓展了梯度界面模型的理论适用范围。

Abstract: We study a general class of gradient interface models with Hamiltonian $H=β\sum V(\nablaφ)$, $β>0$, assuming essentially that the potential $V$ is even, $V'(s)\ge αs$ on $[0,\infty)$ for some $α>0$, and $-M\leq V''\le C$. We establish a Helffer-Sjöstrand representation for these models, and use it to prove that their scaling limits are Gaussian Free Fields (GFFs), and that their covariances decay at the same rate as the GFF. This extends results for strictly convex potentials to a large class of non-convex potentials and to arbitrary temperatures. Additionally, we prove Brascamp-Lieb and dimension-free Poincaré inequalities for the models.

The construction of such a representation is one of the key new contributions of this work.

</details>

### [21] [Regular Black Holes from Collapsing Dust in a Dark Energy Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13619)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力坍缩中的中心奇点代表经典广义相对论的基本失效（fundamental breakdown），但其解法仍是一个开放挑战。现有的正则黑洞（regular black hole）模型通常依赖奇异物质或非线性电动力学，这些正则化机制的物理起源仍然模糊不清，因此需要寻找一种物理上可解释的奇点消除机制。

Result: 仅当相互作用在中心方向足够增强时，密度发散和中心质量贡献才能被同时消除，得到质量函数按M~χ³标度、有限Kretschmann标量（lim_χ→0 K < ∞）以及物理区域w≤-1下的类de Sitter核心。中心构型满足零能量条件、弱能量条件和主导能量条件，但违反强能量条件。与基于电荷的模型不同，相互作用参数α(t)在坍缩后稳定于非零值，使正则核心能在晚期构型中持续存在。

Conclusion: 该工作为常规正则黑洞模型提供了一种具有物理动机的替代方案，其基础是当代宇宙学对暗能量（dark energy）的理解，而非奇异场或电荷。这一机制暗示暗能量-尘埃间径向变化的相互作用可能在引力坍缩的奇点规避中起关键作用。

Abstract: The central singularity in gravitational collapse represents a fundamental breakdown of classical general relativity, yet its resolution remains an open challenge. While conventional regular black hole models invoke exotic matter or nonlinear electrodynamics, the physical origin of such regularization mechanisms remains obscure. In this work, we demonstrate that a radially varying interaction between dust and dark energy can naturally resolve the central singularity. We systematically investigate three cases: no interaction, constant interaction, and radially varying interaction. Only when the interaction grows sufficiently toward the center can the divergent behavior of the density and the central mass contribution simultaneously be eliminated near the center. This yields a mass function scales as $M \sim χ^3$, a finite Kretschmann scalar ($\lim_{χ\to 0} K < \infty$), and a de Sitter-like core for the physical regime $w \leq -1$ (with $w=-1$ corresponding to pure de Sitter core). At the center, the resulting configuration satisfies the null, weak, and dominant energy conditions, while the strong energy condition is violated. Unlike charge-based models, our interaction parameter $α(t)$ stabilizes at a non-zero value after the collapse, allowing the regular core to persist in the late-time configuration. This provides a physically motivated alternative to conventional regular black hole models, grounded in the modern cosmological understanding of dark energy rather than exotic fields or charges.

</details>


### [22] [Radiative Matching Across a Projector Bridge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13634)
*Medeu E. Abishev,Daulet Z. Berkimbayev*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文通过协变对应桥（covariant correspondence bridge）将两个不同的爱因斯坦–希尔伯特（Einstein–Hilbert）扇区联系起来，在保持总四极光度与广义相对论啁啾相位不变的同时，将发射功率分配给可见与隐藏通道，从而实现质量零自旋-2场之间的振幅转移，而非引力子质量化或传播振荡效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机在于探索两种爱因斯坦–希尔伯特扇区之间的协变对应关系，并考察该桥映射对应力-能量（stress-energy）的传递如何影响引力真空态结构与可观测的引力波信号，特别是振幅在可见与隐藏通道间的分配及其对标准警报距离（standard-siren distance）的系统性偏移。

Method: 构造一个度规无关的协变对应桥，通过双张量核（bitensor kernels）映射应力-能量；利用Bianchi恒等式保证守恒，使跨扇区的桥应力平衡交换；在正引力耦合与成对闵可夫斯基背景下，检验作用量的块对角结构与真空的质量零自旋-2双场内容；并引入一阶互易输运律以生成保持通量的出射数据旋转。

Result: 该构造在无Fierz–Pauli质量项的情况下给出两个无质量自旋-2场；通量保持的旋转将辐射功率分配至可见与隐藏通道，同时保持总四极光度与广义相对论啁啾相位；可见振幅减小导致系统性的标准警报距离偏移，其灵敏度可由引力波目录宇宙学结合独立距离标定估算；源级重标度在物理上不等价，会改变辐射反作用并需要补偿的桥能量流。

Conclusion: 该构造描述了质量零张量通道之间的振幅转移，明确区别于大质量引力子色散（massive-graviton dispersion）与传播诱导振荡；它提供了一种在保持广义相对论核心观测特征（如啁啾相位）的前提下解释引力波振幅变化的理论框架。

Abstract: Two distinct Einstein--Hilbert sectors are related by a covariant correspondence bridge that maps stress-energy through bitensor kernels. The Bianchi identities enforce conservation, with bridge stresses balancing cross-sector exchange. For positive gravitational couplings and paired Minkowski backgrounds, a fixed metric-independent bridge leaves the quadratic action block diagonal, so the vacuum contains two massless spin-2 fields and no Fierz--Pauli mass term. A reciprocal first--order transport law generates a flux--preserving rotation of the canonical outgoing data. This divides the emitted power between visible and hidden channels while preserving the total quadrupole luminosity and the general-relativistic chirp phase. The reduced visible amplitude produces a systematic standard-siren distance shift, whose present sensitivity can be estimated from gravitational--wave catalog cosmology combined with an independent distance calibration. A source--level rescaling is physically inequivalent: it changes radiation reaction and requires compensating bridge energy flow. The construction therefore describes amplitude transfer between massless tensor channels, distinct from massive-graviton dispersion and propagation-induced oscillations.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着引力波探测器灵敏度不断提升，有必要将最有前景的广义相对论以外（beyond-GR）候选理论置于与GR同等的地位。然而，渐进平坦时空框架是否适用于这些理论并不显然，需要仔细分析到达未来零无穷（future null infinity）的额外自由度，这正是本文所要回答的问题。

Method: 作者首先在存在任意应力-能量张量（stress-energy tensor）的情况下积分邦迪-萨克斯层级（Bondi-Sachs hierarchy），提取使标准度规衰减保持闭合所需的落降条件。随后将最一般的具有二阶运动方程的标量-矢量-张量（SVT）理论放入该框架，将场方程改写为有效爱因斯坦形式，并对照落降表评估每个算子，通过标量与矢量运动方程及真空稳定性进行约束，最终凝练为耦合泛函及其导数的约束表。

Result: 结果表明，仅有极少数条件存留：标量势在渐近真空中必须三次方阶消失，渐近牛顿常数必须有限且为正，标量和矢量模式必须正则归一化，共形耦合扇区存在框架微妙性。其余所有耦合泛函均受理论结构自身保护，无需额外约束。

Conclusion: 该约束表为筛选超越GR模型是否满足渐近平坦性提供了诊断工具，并确立了BMS群（Bondi-van der Burg-Metzner-Sachs group）作为整个可容许SVT理论类中的渐近对称群，从而使平衡通量方程（balance flux equations）可推广应用于这些理论。

Abstract: The asymptotic symmetry group of asymptotically flat spacetimes gives rise to balance flux equations that constrain, fully non-perturbatively, the asymptotic strain measured by gravitational-wave detectors. Such constraints are sharp tools for identifying features such as the memory effect. As detector sensitivities improve, it becomes imperative to place the most promising beyond-GR candidates on the same footing. Whether the asymptotically flat framework applies to such theories at all is far from obvious and requires careful analysis of the additional degrees of freedom reaching future null infinity. In this work, we address this question. We integrate the Bondi-Sachs hierarchy in the presence of an arbitrary stress-energy tensor and extract the falloff conditions its components must satisfy for the standard metric decay to close. We then feed the most general scalar-vector-tensor theory with second-order equations of motion through this framework. Recasting the field equations in the effective Einstein form $G_{μν}=T^{\rm eff}_{μν}/G_4(Φ,X)$ and evaluating every operator of $L_i$ against the falloff table, we condense the outcome into a constraint table for the coupling functionals and their derivatives at the asymptotic vacuum, sharpened by the scalar and vector equations of motion and vacuum stability. Remarkably few conditions survive. The scalar potential must vanish to cubic order at the asymptotic vacuum, the asymptotic Newton constant must be finite and positive, the scalar and vector modes must be canonically normalized, and the conformally coupled sector carries a frame subtlety. Every other coupling functional is protected by the structure of the theory. The constraint table thus provides a diagnostic for screening beyond-GR models against asymptotic flatness and establishes the BMS group as the asymptotic symmetry group across the entire admissible SVT class.

</details>

*Mushtaq Ahmad,M. Farasat Shamir,Ahdab K. Althukair*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在迹耦合引力（trace-coupled gravity）理论中研究了静态球对称可穿越虫洞，其作用量密度为 f(R,T)=R+λT²，时空由Schwarzschild的C∞变形构成，并在有限核心外精确归于Schwarzschild解，无需薄壳或接合面。通过代数重建（algebraic reconstruction）得到了各向异性源的密度与压强公式，并分析了参数空间的可行性与物质奇异来源的位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿越虫洞需要在喉部违反零能量条件（null energy condition），论文动机在于探讨在修正引力框架下是否能够“局域化”奇异物质而非消除它。选择f(R,T)=R+λT²这一特定迹耦合形式，希望在不引入额外场自由度、方程保持二阶的前提下，系统分析虫洞解是否存在以及物质重建是否自洽。

Method: 方法上，论文采用Schwarzschild度规的C∞光滑变形构造虫洞几何，变形仅在有限半径核心内非零，因此外部精确为Schwarzschild。由于f_R=1，场方程保持二阶，物质问题简化为局部代数重建；通过定义无量纲迹变量χ=λT/(4π)，推导出(rho, p_r, p_t)关于有效源的一统反演公式，并求三次方程的实根作为容许物质分支。

Result: 研究结果给出参数空间中存在正密度与负密度喉部区域、临界退化边界以及非容许分支失效扇区。几何本身与物质分支选择无关，但重建的物质依赖于物质拉格朗日量的具体选取。在容许分支上，径向零能量条件（NEC）仍在喉处失效，模型仅将奇异物质局域化于有限区间内，外部潮汐场精确为带ADM质量M的Schwarzschild解。

Conclusion: 结论指出，该结果应被视为在所展示的试探函数族（ansatz families）内的受控存在性与可行性论断，而非稳定性证明。模型能够在保持外部几何经典可检验特征的前提下重建内部物质分布，但无法消除喉部对零能量条件的破坏，只能将其限制在有限区域内。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric traversable wormholes in trace-coupled gravity with action density f(R,T) = R + lambda T^2. The spacetime is built from C-infinity deformations of Schwarzschild that vanish identically outside a finite core, so the exterior is exactly Schwarzschild and no thin shell or junction surface is needed. Because f_R = 1, the metric equations remain second order and the matter problem reduces to a local algebraic reconstruction. For an anisotropic source, we derive unified inversion formulas for (rho, p_r, p_t) in terms of the effective source and the dimensionless trace variable chi = lambda T/(4 pi), and show that the admissible matter branch is the vacuum-connected real root of a cubic equation. The resulting parameter space contains regular positive-density and negative-density throat regimes, a critical boundary where the reconstruction degenerates, and a nonadmissible branch-failure sector. The geometry itself is branch-independent, but the reconstructed matter depends on the matter-Lagrangian prescription. On the admissible branch the radial NEC still fails at the throat, so the model localizes exoticity rather than removing it. The Ricci support and reconstructed matter remain confined to a finite interval, while the exterior tidal field is exactly Schwarzschild with ADM mass M. The results should be read as controlled existence and viability statements within the displayed ansatz families, not as a stability proof.

</details>

### [25] [Explosions from Rotating Very Massive Star Collapses to Black Holes: Effects of Nuclear Burning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13642)
*Sho Fujibayashi,Alan Tsz-Lok Lam,Yuichiro Sekiguchi,Masaru Shibata*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文通过数值相对论模拟研究旋转超大质量与超巨星核心的坍缩过程，发现核心质量决定了坍缩类型（近似同调坍缩 vs. 失控型坍缩）以及黑洞形成和吸积盘的性质；盘反弹与黏性演化可产生质量达10–1000 M☉、动能达10^53–10^55 erg的喷出物，在低质量模型中还能产生丰富的铁族元素（如^56Ni）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转超大质量恒星核坍缩及其产物（黑洞、吸积盘与元素合成）在核天体物理学与引力波天文学中具有重要意义，但此前缺乏系统的高分辨率数值相对论研究。作者旨在通过系统参数扫描揭示核心质量与坍缩、盘形成及核合成之间的定量联系。

Method: 采用包含α链核反应网络（alpha-chain nuclear reaction network）和中微子冷却（neutrino cooling）的数值相对论（numerical relativity）模拟；新构建的初始核心质量范围为2×10^3至5×10^4 M☉，坍缩分别由对不稳定性（pair instability）或广义相对论不稳定性（general-relativistic instability）触发；对选定模型进一步跟踪吸积盘的黏性演化。

Result: 高质量核心呈近似同调坍缩，低质量核心因中微子冷却更高效而呈失控型坍缩；低质量模型形成的黑洞占核心质量比例更小，伴随更大无量纲角动量，利于形成快速旋转黑洞和大质量盘。盘反弹驱动的喷出物质量为10–10^3 M☉、动能为10^53–10^55 erg；仅最低质量模型产生显著^56Ni。黏性可增强喷出物质量与动能，并在≤10^4 M☉模型中使喷出物富含^56Ni。

Conclusion: 旋转超大质量恒星坍缩可产生大规模高能喷出物，且在核心质量足够低时可产生大量铁族元素，这对理解早期宇宙中重元素来源与超大质量黑洞形成具有重要启示。

Abstract: We investigate the collapse of rotating very massive and supermassive stellar cores using numerical relativity simulations including an alpha-chain nuclear reaction network and neutrino cooling. Our main survey focuses on newly constructed models with initial core masses of $2 \times {10}^{3}-5\times 10^4M_\odot$. The collapse is triggered either by pair instability in lower-mass cores or by general-relativistic instability in higher-mass cores. We find that higher-mass cores undergo a nearly homologous collapse, whereas lower-mass cores show a more runaway-like collapse because neutrino cooling becomes more efficient at their higher densities and temperatures. Consequently, the black hole formed in lower-mass models initially contains a smaller fraction of the core mass, and disk formation occurs while a larger amount of matter remains outside the black hole. The lower compactness of pair-unstable cores also allows larger dimensionless angular momentum, favoring the formation of rapidly rotating black holes and massive disks. The disk bounce drives mass ejection with ejecta masses of order $10-10^3M_\odot$ and kinetic energies of order $10^{53}-10^{55}\,\mathrm{erg}$. Significant $^{56}$Ni production in the disk-bounce ejecta occurs only in the lowest-mass models. For selected models, we further follow the viscous evolution of the disk and find that viscosity enhances the ejecta mass and kinetic energy. In models with $\lesssim10^4M_\odot$, the viscosity-driven ejecta can originate from disk matter that has reached nuclear statistical equilibrium and can therefore become rich in $^{56}$Ni. These results suggest that rotating very massive star collapses can produce massive, energetic ejecta and, for sufficiently low core masses, substantial iron-group elements.

</details>

### [26] [Poly(1,4-anthraquinone) as an Organic Cathode Material: Simulation of Observable Bonding Properties to Li, Na, Mg, and Ca](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14185)
*Laura Femmer,Lukas Köbbing,Juliane Heitkämper,Sibylle Riedel,Devran Cay,Florin Adler,Birgit Esser,Alexander J. C. Kuehne,Zhirong Zhao-Karger,Piotr de Silva,Juan Maria García-Lastra,Birger Horstmann*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本研究使用量子力学方法（DFT和DFTB）系统研究了锂、钠、镁、钙四种金属离子与聚(1,4-蒽醌)（P14AQ）的结合机制，开发了自动化构象体生成方法来应对P14AQ的结构复杂性，并结合恒电流测量解释了单价离子在P14AQ中具有更高容量的原因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P14AQ作为一种有前景的阴极材料，具有高容量和良好的循环稳定性，但其控制金属离子结合和电化学行为的原子尺度机制仍不清楚，亟需从理论上阐明不同价态金属离子的结合行为差异。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）和密度泛函紧束缚（DFTB）量子力学方法，开发了基于取向标记方案的自动化方法，生成周期性聚合物链中所有无旋转重复的P14AQ构象体；对每个构象体，系统性地将金属原子置于每个氧位点旁，完全探索所有可能的结合构型，并结合恒电流测量（galvanostatic measurements）进行实验验证。

Result: 观测到两种结构基序：单一金属-氧键和与两个相对氧原子的配位。锂和钠表现出连续的能量分布，而镁和钙在两个基序之间存在能量间隙，强烈偏好双氧结合构型。恒电流测量显示Ca和Mg的比容量低于Li和Na；Na实验中观测到的两个电压平台与其不同的金属-氧键数目相对应。

Conclusion: 单价离子在P14AQ中的更高容量源于两个因素：二价离子由于不利的能学无法高效结合单一氧位点，且聚合物链的构象分布阻碍了最优配位的实现，从而解释了单价离子相对于二价离子的容量优势。

Abstract: Poly(1,4-anthraquinone) (P14AQ) has emerged as a promising cathode material, offering high capacity and good cycling stability, yet the atomic-scale mechanisms governing metal-ion binding and electrochemical behavior remain poorly understood. To address this, we investigate the binding mechanisms of Li, Na, Mg, and Ca to P14AQ using quantum mechanical methods, particularly DFT and DFTB. A key challenge lies in the material's structural complexity: multiple conformers of P14AQ are energetically similar but kinetically isolated due to significant energy barriers. To account for this, we develop an automated method to generate all unique P14AQ conformers for a periodic polymer chain without rotational duplicates through an orientation labeling scheme. For each conformer, we systematically place a metal atom adjacent to every oxygen site, enabling a complete exploration of binding configurations. We observe two structural motifs: a single metal-oxygen bond and coordination to two opposite oxygen atoms. While Li and Na exhibit continuous energy distributions, Mg and Ca show an energy gap between the two motifs, with a strong preference for the two-oxygen binding configuration. Galvanostatic measurements support these findings by showing lower gravimetric capacities for Ca and Mg than for Li and Na. For Na, the different numbers of metal-oxygen bonds are reflected in the two voltage plateaus observed experimentally. Overall, the combined computational and experimental results explain the higher capacity of monovalent ions in P14AQ: divalent ions cannot bind efficiently to a single oxygen site due to unfavorable energetics, and the conformational distribution of the polymer chain prevents optimal coordination.

</details>


### [27] [GPU implementation of mixed quantum-classical Liouville molecular dynamics without momentum jump](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14544)
*Koji Ando*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文在GPU上实现了基于无动量跳跃理论（momentum-jump-free theory）的混合量子-经典Liouville分子动力学（mixed quantum-classical Liouville molecular dynamics）模拟，通过移除原先CPU实现中的轨迹繁衍（trajectory spawning）策略，避免了GPU上的线程发散（thread divergence）和动态内存分配开销，相比CPU版本获得了一个数量级的加速，并实现了随采样轨迹数线性扩展的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典Liouville分子动力学模拟在CPU上实现时依赖轨迹繁衍来增强采样，但该机制在GPU架构下会带来线程发散和动态内存分配的开销，限制并行性能的提升。

Method: 在GPU上实现了一种基于无动量跳跃理论的混合量子-经典Liouville分子动力学模拟，删除了原先CPU实现中用于采样增强的轨迹繁衍机制，以避免GPU上的线程发散和动态内存分配开销。

Result: 与带轨迹繁衍的CPU计算相比，GPU实现获得了一个数量级的加速，并且计算时间随采样轨迹数量呈线性扩展。

Conclusion: 去除轨迹繁衍策略的GPU实现是提升混合量子-经典Liouville分子动力学模拟性能的有效途径，兼顾了显著加速与良好扩展性。

Abstract: We implemented on GPU a mixed quantum-classical Liouville molecular dynamics simulation based on a momentum-jump-free theory. The trajectory spawning that was previously implemented on CPU for sampling enhancement was eliminated to avoid the overhead of thread divergence and dynamic memory allocation on the GPU. This achieved a speedup of an order of magnitude compared to the CPU computation with spawning, as well as a linear scaling with respect to the number of sampling trajectories.

</details>

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用LHCb探测器在质心能量13 TeV下采集的6 fb⁻¹质子-质子对撞数据，测量了B⁰_s → J/ψπ⁺π⁻衰变中的时间依赖CP不对称性，得到CP破坏相位φ_s = −0.077 ± 0.034 ± 0.007 rad、直接CP破坏参数|λ| = 0.993 ± 0.026 ± 0.007以及重质量本征态衰变宽度Γ_H = 0.610 ± 0.002 ± 0.004 ps⁻¹，结果与标准模型预期及既往测量一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: B⁰_s介子系统的时间依赖CP不对称性测量是检验标准模型（Standard Model）中CP破坏机制的重要途径。利用更大的统计样本（6 fb⁻¹、13 TeV）获得更精确的φ_s、|λ|和Γ_H测量值，有助于检验标准模型预言并寻找超出标准模型（BSM）的新物理信号。

Method: 使用LHCb探测器在2015–2018年间采集的质心能量13 TeV的质子-质子对撞数据（综合亮度6 fb⁻¹），对B⁰_s → J/ψπ⁺π⁻衰变通道进行时间依赖CP不对称性测量。通过拟合衰变时间分布，同时提取CP破坏相位φ_s、直接CP破坏参数|λ|以及B⁰_s系统重质量本征态的衰变宽度Γ_H，并分别给出统计和系统不确定性。此外，将结果与7 TeV和8 TeV数据的既有测量以及所有其他LHCb测量进行组合。

Result: 单次测量给出φ_s = −0.077 ± 0.034 ± 0.007 rad，|λ| = 0.993 ± 0.026 ± 0.007，Γ_H = 0.610 ± 0.002 ± 0.004 ps⁻¹（第一项为统计不确定度，第二项为系统不确定度）。与既往7和8 TeV数据组合后得φ_s = −0.046 ± 0.031 rad，|λ| = 0.975 ± 0.024，Γ_H = 0.610 ± 0.004 ps⁻¹；包含所有LHCb测量的组合给出φ_s = −0.041 ± 0.017 rad。

Conclusion: 本测量的φ_s、|λ|和Γ_H结果与既往测量一致，也与基于标准模型的预期相符，未发现超出标准模型的新物理迹象。组合所有LHCb测量后的φ_s精度达到0.017 rad，显著提高了该参数的实验约束能力。

Abstract: The time-dependent $C\!P$ asymmetry in $B^{0}_{s} \!\to J/ψπ^{+}π^{-}$ decays is measured using proton-proton collision data, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $6\,\text{fb}^{-1}$, collected with the LHCb detector at a centre-of-mass energy of $13\,\text{TeV}$ during $\mbox{2015--2018}$. The $C\!P$-violating phase, $φ_{s}$, the direct $C\!P$-violation parameter, $\left|λ\right|$, and the decay width of the heavy mass eigenstate in the $B^{0}_{s}$ system, $Γ_{\mathrm{ H}}$, are measured respectively to be $φ_{s} = -0.077 \pm 0.034 \pm 0.007\,\text{rad}$, $\left|λ\right| = 0.993 \pm 0.026 \pm 0.007$ and $Γ_{\mathrm{ H}} = 0.610 \pm 0.002 \pm 0.004\,\text{ps}^{-1}$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. These results are consistent with previous measurements and the expectation based on the Standard Model. The combination with previous measurements in $B^{0}_{s} \!\to J/ψπ^{+}π^{-}$ decays using $7\,\text{TeV}$ and $8\,\text{TeV}$ proton-proton collision data yields $φ_{s} = -0.046 \pm 0.031\,\text{rad}$, $\left|λ\right| = 0.975 \pm 0.024$ and $Γ_{\mathrm{ H}} = 0.610 \pm 0.004\,\text{ps}^{-1}$, while the combination including all other LHCb measurements gives $φ_{s} = -0.041 \pm 0.017\,\text{rad}$.

</details>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [29] [Single-impurity polarons in hard-core lattice bosons at low and intermediate fillings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13988)
*Chao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文通过无符号问题的虫算法量子蒙特卡洛模拟（worm-algorithm quantum Monte Carlo），系统研究了二维硬核玻色-哈伯德（Bose-Hubbard）浴中单个移动杂质的极化子（polaron）修饰行为，覆盖从弱到强耦合直至双组分硬核极限，并考察了填充率与杂质迁移率对缀饰（dressing）性质的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在关联、可压缩的晶格浴中强缀饰极化子的定量特征，以及浴填充率和杂质迁移率如何改变近核响应与缀饰云的空间范围，是本研究的主要动机。

Method: 采用大规模、无符号问题的虫算法量子蒙特卡洛模拟，从杂质格林函数（Green's function）提取动量空间准粒子性质，并通过虚时平均的杂质中心关联函数解析实空间的浴重新排布。通过改变杂质跳跃项t_imp考察强耦合区移动性降低的影响。

Result: 在可及的填充率范围内，杂质始终保持为缀饰准粒子，其基态能量、有效质量和残差随填充率n_b平滑变化。实空间中，提高填充率增强短程耗尽并使主导响应向杂质集中；在双组分硬核极限下，累积密度形变的大距离恢复仅表现出弱的填充依赖，而短程核心指标仍持续演化。

Conclusion: 研究定量刻画了关联、可压缩晶格浴中的强缀饰极化子，阐明了填充率和杂质迁移率如何修正近核响应及缀饰云的空间范围，填补了对该体系中极化子修饰随浴填充演化的系统性理解空缺。

Abstract: We investigate a single mobile impurity in a two-dimensional hard-core Bose--Hubbard bath at low and intermediate fillings and determine how polaronic dressing evolves with bath filling for impurity--bath couplings ranging from weak to strong and ultimately to the two-component hard-core limit. Using large-scale, sign-problem-free worm-algorithm quantum Monte Carlo simulations, we extract momentum-space quasiparticle properties from the impurity Green's function and resolve the accompanying real-space bath rearrangement from an imaginary-time-averaged impurity-centered correlator. We also vary the impurity hopping $t_{\rm imp}$ to assess how reduced mobility modifies dressing in the strong-coupling regime. For the fillings accessible at each coupling, the impurity remains a dressed quasiparticle whose ground-state energy, effective mass, and residue vary smoothly with filling $n_{\rm b}$. In real space, increasing $n_{\rm b}$ strengthens the short-range depletion while shifting the dominant response toward the impurity. In the two-component hard-core limit $U_{\rm ib}/t_{\rm b}\!\to\!\infty$, the large-distance recovery of the cumulative density deformation exhibits only weak filling dependence over the range considered here, whereas short-range core indicators continue to evolve. Our results quantitatively characterize strongly dressed polarons in a correlated, compressible lattice bath and resolve how filling and impurity mobility modify the near-core response and spatial extent of the dressing cloud.

</details>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [30] [Learning Spin Hamiltonians from Terahertz Two-Dimensional Coherent Spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14460)
*Martin Mootz,Chuankun Huang,Liang Luo,Jigang Wang,Yong-Xin Yao*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种监督式机器学习框架，通过非线性太赫兹二维相干谱（THz-2DCS）直接推断量子材料的有效哈密顿量（effective Hamiltonian）参数。该方法利用前向模型生成谱图，经过统一预处理后由神经网络学习从谱指纹到微观参数的逆映射，并在稀土正铁氧体（rare-earth orthoferrites）实验数据上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有效哈密顿量连接了量子材料中的微观相互作用与可观测的集体行为，但直接从实验数据确定其参数属于困难的反问题（inverse problem），现有方法缺乏系统有效的途径。

Method: 方法包括三个环节：首先，用校准的前向模型（forward model）从候选哈密顿量生成合成谱图；其次，采用统一的预处理流程将模拟谱图与实验谱图映射到相同表示空间；最后，训练神经网络学习从谱指纹到微观参数的逆映射。具体示例采用具有交换相互作用、Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用、各向异性和阻尼的双子格Landau–Lifshitz–Gilbert自旋模型。

Result: 合成基准测试表明，非线性谱能够编码超出线性响应所确定的额外参数，推断精度与物理谱灵敏度一致；使用多个脉冲间延迟可增强对噪声的鲁棒性。将方法应用于Sm0.4Er0.6FeO3的实验THz-2DCS数据后，推断出的参数能产生物理上合理的前向模拟，而残留偏差揭示了简化模型的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究确立了THz-2DCS作为有效哈密顿量推断与模型改进的数据丰富平台，支持实验驱动的微观相互作用识别，为理解、预测乃至最终控制量子材料的涌现特性（emergent properties）提供了基础。

Abstract: Effective Hamiltonians connect microscopic interactions to measurable collective behavior in quantum materials, but determining their parameters directly from experiment remains a challenging inverse problem. We introduce a supervised machine-learning framework that infers Hamiltonian parameters from nonlinear terahertz two-dimensional coherent spectra. A calibrated forward model generates spectra from candidate Hamiltonians, a common preprocessing pipeline maps simulated and experimental spectra into the same representation, and a neural network learns the inverse map from spectral fingerprints to microscopic parameters. We demonstrate the approach for rare-earth orthoferrites using a two-sublattice Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert spin model with exchange, Dzyaloshinskii--Moriya interaction, anisotropies, and damping. Synthetic benchmarks show that nonlinear spectra encode parameters beyond those fixed by the linear response, with inference accuracy tracking the physical spectral sensitivity and robustness against noise improved by using multiple inter-pulse delays. Applied to experimental THz-2DCS data from Sm$_{0.4}$Er$_{0.6}$FeO$_3$, the inferred parameters yield physically reasonable forward simulations, while remaining discrepancies identify limitations of the reduced model. These results establish THz-2DCS as a data-rich platform for effective-Hamiltonian inference and model refinement, enabling experimentally driven identification of microscopic interactions while providing a foundation for understanding, predicting, and ultimately controlling the emergent properties of quantum materials.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究双味Schwinger模型在真空角θ=π时的纠缠谱（entanglement spectrum），发现联合Schmidt分布可揭示截断依赖的规范扇区层级结构（gauge-sector hierarchy）和质量诱导的风味不对称性（flavor asymmetry）。通过定义组合权重和条件极化量，作者揭示了规范扇区排序、扇区权重和风味极化在全局有序纠缠谱中被混合的分离结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范理论（gauge theory）中的纠缠谱可以同时编码对称性破缺和规范扇区的组织方式，但此前对于这些多层信息如何分离和表征缺乏系统理解。本文旨在通过双味Schwinger模型这一具体例证，说明联合Schmidt分布如何揭示被全局纠缠谱所掩蔽的微观结构。

Method: 研究双味Schwinger模型（two-flavor Schwinger model）在真空角θ=π下的纠缠结构，使用联合Schmidt分布p_{Q_A,F_A}，以子系统规范电荷Q_A和风味不平衡度F_A为标记。定义了组合权重W_F^(q)和条件极化P_F^(q)两个诊断量，并考察不同截断方式（交错截断与单元胞内截断）对分布组织的影响。

Result: 改变交错截断（staggered cut）会重组规范电荷分布p_{Q_A}，而质量不平衡会破坏条件风味分布p_{F_A|Q_A}的F_A↔-F_A对称性。改变截断方式可使权重层级(W_F^(q))反转：在单元胞边界处W_F^(-1)>W_F^(+1)，在单元胞内截断处W_F^(+1)>W_F^(-1)。质量不平衡使条件极化P_F^(q)偏离零，且响应依赖于q和截断位置。

Conclusion: 对称性分解（symmetry resolution）能够将混合在全局有序纠缠谱中的规范扇区排序、扇区权重和风味极化三个层次的信息分离出来，为理解规范理论中纠缠结构的精细组织提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Entanglement spectra in gauge theories can encode both symmetry breaking and the organization of gauge sectors. In the two-flavor Schwinger model at vacuum angle $θ=π$, we find that the joint Schmidt distribution $p_{Q_A,F_A}$, labeled by the subsystem gauge charge $Q_A$ and flavor imbalance $F_A$, reveals a cut-dependent gauge-sector hierarchy and a mass-induced flavor asymmetry: changing the staggered cut reorganizes the gauge-charge distribution $p_{Q_A}$, while mass imbalance breaks the $F_A\leftrightarrow-F_A$ symmetry of the conditional flavor distribution $p_{F_A|Q_A}$. Defining the combined weight $W_F^{(q)}=p_{q,+1}+p_{q,-1}$ and conditional polarization $\mathcal P_F^{(q)}=(p_{q,+1}-p_{q,-1})/W_F^{(q)}$, we find that changing the cut reverses the weight hierarchy, $W_F^{(-1)}>W_F^{(+1)}$ at unit-cell boundaries but $W_F^{(+1)}>W_F^{(-1)}$ at intra-cell cuts, while mass imbalance drives $\mathcal P_F^{(q)}$ away from zero with a $q$-dependent cut response. Symmetry resolution therefore separates gauge-sector ordering, sector weight, and flavor polarization that are mixed in the globally ordered entanglement spectrum.

</details>

### [32] [Extinction drives emergent metastability in complex ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14416)
*Jong Il Park,Tim Rogers,Joseph W. Baron*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本研究将人口统计随机性（demographic stochasticity）引入大型复杂生态系统的规则模型，揭示了该随机性会快速剔除生态群落中的低丰度物种，从而提高系统稳定性。研究表明物种存活比例随时间呈现异常的厚尾衰减，涌现出具有稳健亚稳性（metastability）的残余群落，说明在考虑人口统计涨落时，生态系统即使在其确定性极限下被预测为混沌，也会通过降低自身多样性实现自我稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稳定性准则往往依赖确定性框架，忽略了本质上导致任何物种可能随机灭绝的内在种群涨落，因此无法全面理解物种多样性如何影响大型生态系统的稳定性。

Method: 作者将人口统计随机性纳入大型复杂生态系统的规则模型（rule-based model），并发展了一种自下而上的理论框架来刻画灭绝动力学的统计特性。

Result: 研究发现人口统计随机性会快速剪除生态群落中的低丰度物种，从而提高系统整体稳定性；物种存活比例随时间呈现异常的厚尾衰减（heavy-tailed decay），涌现出具有稳健亚稳性的残余群落。

Conclusion: 当考虑人口统计涨落时，即使生态系统在确定性极限下被预测为混沌，也会通过降低物种多样性实现自我稳定，强调了随机性在生态系统稳定性中的关键作用。

Abstract: Extinction is inevitable; every species eventually dies out, impacting the ecosystem it is part of. Over the past few decades, extensive research stemming from the stability-diversity debate has addressed how species diversity contributes to the stability of large ecosystems. However, conventional stability criteria often rely on deterministic frameworks, overlooking the intrinsic population fluctuations that allow any species to go extinct by chance. In this paper, we incorporate demographic stochasticity into large complex ecosystems with a rule-based model. We demonstrate that such demographic fluctuations rapidly prune the low-abundance species from the ecological community, thereby securing higher systemic stability. By developing a bottom-up theory to characterise the statistics of extinction dynamics, we discover that the fraction of surviving species exhibits an anomalous heavy-tailed decay over time, revealing the emergence of remnant communities with robust metastability. Our results highlight that when demographic fluctuations are accounted for, ecosystems self-stabilise by reducing their diversity even when they are predicted to be chaotic in the deterministic limit.

</details>

### [33] [Unknown Unknowns: Model Misspecification in Machine Learning for Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13633)
*Juan Cruz-Martinez,Carolina Cuesta-Lazaro,Alexander Held,Michael Kagan*

Main category: physics.data-an

TL;DR: 本文探讨机器学习在粒子物理与天文学逆问题（inverse problem）求解中面临的模型误设（model misspecification）挑战，提出了检测与缓解策略相结合、迭代循环的鲁棒分析框架，并强调应对未知未知（unknown unknowns）的关键在于分析方法的设计取向而非单一技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习已成为粒⼦物理与天文学中求解逆问题的核心工具，但模型基于模拟数据训练却部署于真实数据，从而引入了超出传统拟合优度范畴的风险——即模型以未被预期的方式出错（未知未知）。这种模型误设挑战在物理学中既可能正是我们想要发现的新物理信号，也可能需要被吸收进分析中而避免引入偏差，因此亟需系统性的应对框架。

Method: 论文采用概念性分析的方法，系统梳理了模型误设带来的三类核心问题：检测误差的诊端方法、缓解误设影响的减缓策略，以及二者之间构成迭代循环的更新机制。文章强调需要一组互补性诊断工具（battery of complementary diagnostics）而非单一方法，并指出机器学习既能放大误设效应，也能提供新的工具加以应对。

Result: 文章提出鲁棒分析（robust analysis）的核心判据：吸收我们不感兴趣的误设效应，同时保持对真正感兴趣的误设效应的灵敏性。没有任何单一诊断能确认模型的正确设定，检测与缓解必须构成交替进行的迭代循环。对未知未知的鲁棒性本质上取决于一种分析态度——愿意怀疑自身模型，并设计能在未预期方式出错时仍可存续的分析方案。

Conclusion: 该论文的结论是，模型误设是机器学习用于物理逆问题时不可回避的核心风险；解决之道不在于某个具体技术，而在于建立检测—缓解—更新的迭代分析范式，培养对自身模型的批判性怀疑态度，从而在物理发现与测量精度之间取得平衡。

Abstract: Machine learning is now a central tool for solving inverse problems in particle physics and astronomy. Models are trained on simulation and deployed on real data, raising the question not just of whether they fit, but of whether they are wrong in ways we did not anticipate: the unknown unknowns. This challenge of model misspecification is not unique to machine learning. In physics, misspecification is sometimes exactly what we want to find: new discoveries appear as failures of existing models. At other times, we want such effects absorbed into the analysis without biasing the measurement. A robust analysis is one that absorbs the misspecifications we are not interested in, while preserving sensitivity to the ones we are. Machine learning can both amplify misspecification and provide new tools to address it. We discuss the challenges of model misspecification, diagnostics for detecting it, and strategies for mitigation. No single diagnostic can confirm that a model is correctly specified: detection and mitigation are two halves of an iterative loop, in which a battery of complementary diagnostics is applied, the model is updated, and the process repeated. Robustness against unknown unknowns is ultimately less about any single technique than about a disposition: a willingness to suspect one's own model, and to design analyses that can survive being wrong in ways one did not anticipate.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模激子-极化激元（exciton-polariton）系统作为量子模拟和相干光学的潜在平台，其模式间相位关系的主动控制是实现更复杂量子态制备和光学信息处理的关键，但在实验上仍缺乏确定性的多模相位调控手段。

Conclusion: 该方法可推广至更大耦合阱网络，为在日益复杂的多模极化激元态中对相位关系实施光学控制提供可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate the formation of mutual coherence and deterministic phase control in a multimode exciton-polariton system realized in two optically induced traps with tunable intertrap coupling. As the coupling channel between the traps is progressively opened, the mutual first-order coherence rises sharply from nearly zero to values close to unity, revealing a threshold-like crossover from weakly correlated to highly coherent dynamics. We demonstrate deterministic multimode phase control with a short spatially localized nonresonant pulse, which allows the phase relations between selected polariton condensate modes to be set while keeping their amplitudes nearly unchanged. The induced phase transformation is read out directly from time-resolved interferograms and realized for different sets of modes in both intermediate- and high-coherence regimes. Within a reduced four-mode description, the control pulse implements an effective phase operator on the coherent multimode field with a fidelity exceeding 0.995. The same approach can be extended to larger networks of coupled traps, offering a route to optical control over phase relations in increasingly complex multimode polariton states.

Abstract: Measuring the angular displacement of a mechanical oscillator is a ubiquitous task; however, the multimode nature of angular optomechanical coupling makes coherent signal enhancement challenging. These results establish guided-mode resonance as an on-chip approach to angular displacement readout in quantum optomechanical sensors.

Result: 所提出的准则将大量失稳现象统一解释为质量重新分配失稳（mass-redistribution instabilities），即由局部平衡位移引起的自放大质量重分配。该方法成功应用于大肠杆菌Min系统和秀丽隐杆线虫极性系统的模型，并推广至体积-膜动力学耦合的混合维度系统。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种可解释、广泛适用且实验上可及的途径，可基于守恒律诊断和设计多组元非平衡系统中的图案形成，无需完整的动力学参数信息。

Abstract: Self-organized spatial patterns are central to nonequilibrium physics and cell biology, yet locating instabilities in multi-component, reaction-diffusion networks remains challenging because standard eigenvalue analyses scale with the number of biochemical states and rely on reaction kinetics often poorly constrained by experiments. Exploiting the common mass-conserving structure of protein reaction kinetics and the fact that nonlinear feedback is typically confined to membrane reactions while lateral membrane diffusion is negligible, we develop a geometric classification that predicts stationary, and approximately also oscillatory, pattern-forming instabilities from reactive equilibria. The developed criteria reduce the stability analysis from the full component space to the space of conserved species. On this reduced space, slope matrices---describing the change of equilibrium cytosolic densities with respect to total species densities---govern onset. Based on densities in chemical equilibrium, this approach eliminates the requirement of comprehensive kinetic knowledge frequently lacking in experimental systems. Thus, a broad range of instabilities can be understood as mass-redistribution instabilities---self-amplifying mass redistribution caused by shifting local equilibria. We apply these criteria to models for the Escherichia coli Min system and the Caenorhabditis elegans polarity system and show that the reduction extends to the mixed-dimensional dynamics in systems coupling bulk cytosolic dynamics with membrane dynamics on the boundary. Together, these results provide an interpretable, broadly applicable, and experimentally accessible framework for diagnosing and designing pattern formation in multicomponent nonequilibrium systems on the basis of conservation laws.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 对幂律形式的分解函数φ(x)=kx^q（对应修正Emden振荡器，modified Emden oscillators）采用Riccati约化方案（Riccati reduction scheme）获取闭式解；通过引入补充有广义Rayleigh耗散函数的Lagrangian刻画方程的变分结构；利用Jacobi乘子M(x)=x^{-2A_1}将运动方程转化为位置依赖质量（position-dependent-mass, PDM）形式。

Abstract: We introduce a class of nonautonomous nonlinear oscillator equations of mixed Liénard type that arises from a parametric deformation of the commutative factorization procedure applied to second-order ordinary differential equations with periodic solutions. Their solutions, in particular the isochronous waveforms, are obtained in closed form through a Riccati reduction scheme for the power-law choice of the factorization function, $φ(x)=kx^{q}$, $k\in \mathbb{R}$, and $q\in\mathbb{N}$, corresponding to the so-called modified Emden oscillators, and do not depend on the arbitrary deformation parameter $A_1$. The variational structure of the equation is characterised by a Lagrangian supplemented with a generalised Rayleigh dissipation function that contains a non-standard cubic term in $\dot{x}$. We also show that multiplying the equation of motion by the Jacobi multiplier $M(x)=x^{-2A_1}$ a position-dependent-mass (PDM) form is obtained with related friction and restoring force.

In this setting, localized nonlinear excitations are characterized by discrete spectral data consisting of a complex eigenvalue and an associated polarization vector. For a general class of small perturbations preserving the background, we derive the perturbation-induced evolution of the scattering data directly at the level of the Riemann--Hilbert problem. In the one-soliton sector, this yields a closed finite-dimensional dynamical system governing the slow evolution of the effective soliton parameters, including the spectral variables, the soliton center and phase, the residue amplitude, and the internal polarization state. The latter evolves according to a constrained dynamical equation with no scalar analogue. For localized perturbations, the modulation equations are expressed in explicit integral form in terms of the one-soliton eigenfunctions, providing a fully computable description of the dynamics. In the limit of vanishing boundary conditions, the resulting system reduces to the perturbation theory obtained by E. V. Doktorov, et al, Phys. Rev. A 77 (2008), no. 4, 043617.

Abstract: It is established that the reduced Hartree-Fock grand potential at positive temperature converges in the semiclassical limit to a Thomas-Fermi grand potential. In particular, a density-potential dual representation of the limiting functional is proposed for a general class of fermionic entropy functions. The corresponding dual problem has no duality gap and admits a maximizer. The resulting grand potential converges to the established zero-temperature grand potential as the temperature tends to zero and, for the Fermi-Dirac entropy, agrees with the standard positive-temperature Thomas-Fermi free-energy functional. The proof also establishes a semiclassical trace formula that is uniform over locally precompact families of potentials subject to a common confinement condition.

</details>


### [41] [The Okinawa Lectures on Entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14523)
*Klaas Landsman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为一门课程讲义，系统介绍了经典与量子熵理论，从经典概率论中的大偏差理论出发，经由桑诺夫、克拉默、盖特纳-埃利斯和瓦拉丹定理，过渡到量子概率论中由Umegaki、Araki和Uhlmann等引入的量子相对熵，并以冯·诺依曼代数与Haagerup非交换L^p空间为工具进行严格形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在填补统计物理与量子信息论之间关于熵理论的系统性教学空白，尤其关注经典熵与量子熵之间的深层联系——不仅停留于公式层面的相似性，而是通过统计假设检验中的关键作用揭示二者的本质统一。该课程最初源于黑洞热力学（black hole thermodynamics）的启发，尽管讲义本身不涉及该应用。

Method: 课程从经典概率论的大偏差理论（large deviation theory）出发，介绍桑诺夫（Sanov）、克拉默（Cramér）、盖特纳-埃利斯（Gärtner-Ellis）和瓦拉丹（Varadhan）定理，并将其应用于玻尔兹曼型和吉布斯型统计物理。此后引入量子版本：通过Umegaki量子相对熵、Rényi形变以及Araki-Uhlmann相对量子熵，将经典Kullback-Leibler熵纳入统一框架。关键技术工具包括冯·诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebra）中的模理论（Tomita-Takesaki theory）、Haagerup非交换L^p空间，以及Connes-Størmer-Narnhofer-Thirring熵作为量子动力系统版本。

Result: 讲义展示了经典与量子熵在统计假设检验这一统一视角下的结构性联系：经典Kullback-Leibler熵、Umegaki量子相对熵及其Rényi形变均自然地嵌入此框架。Araki-Uhlmann相对量子熵作为更一般的构造，统一涵盖了Kullback-Leibler与Umegaki熵作为特例；通过Haagerup非交换L^p空间可在密度算子和迹的层面重新定义并计算这些熵。Connes-Størner-Narnhofer-Thirring熵为Kolmogorov-Sinai熵的量子类比。

Conclusion: 该课程提供了从经典到量子熵理论的严格而系统的数学框架，兼顾数学严谨性与理论物理学的可读性。其前置知识要求为本科概率论、泛函分析和量子理论。讲义不讨论黑洞热力学应用及热力学第二定律，仅为该方向提供理论准备。

Abstract: After a historical introduction, the most important classical and quantum entropies are introduced as constructions in classical and quantum probability theory. Classical entropies are studied from large deviation theory, including theorems of Sanov, Cramér, Gärtner-Ellis, and Varadhan, and are illustrated in some applications to both Boltzmannian and Gibbsian statistical physics. Quantum entropies superficially connect to classical entropies at the formula level, but more deeply do so via the crucial role of entropy in statistical hypothesis testing. The classical (relative) Kullback-Leibler entropy, its quantum counterpart introduced by Umegaki, as well as their deformations proposed by Renyi all fit naturally in this context. Quantum entropy faces the new problem of defining and computing the relative entropy of a pair of states on a subsystem, here formalized as a von Neumann algebra. This requires modular (aka Tomita-Takesaki) theory, which provides the framework for the relative quantum entropies introduced by Araki and Uhlmann (these encompass both the Kullback-Leibler and Umegaki entropies as special cases). To (re)define and compute these entropies in terms of density operators and traces, further constructions are needed, namely Haagerup's noncommutative L^p spaces. Von Neumann algebras also provide the setting for the Connes-Stormer-Narnhofer-Thirring entropy, which is a quantum version of the Kolmogorov-Sinai entropy in dynamical systems and ergodic theory, to which we also provide an introduction. This course was originally inspired by, and should be relevant to, black hole thermodynamics, although we discuss neither this application nor the second law. The course tries to be both mathematically rigorous and interesting to theoretical physicists. Prerequisites are undergraduate probability theory, functional analysis, and quantum theory.

</details>

*Raj Pradip Khawale,Elaheh Mehdizadeh,John Brigham,Evgueni T. Filipov*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于杆臂单元（bar and hinge）的广义降阶模型（reduced-order model），用于高效分析任意剪纸（kirigami）结构。该模型通过桁架杆、弯曲铰链和扭簧捕捉系统的变形与内力，相较于有限元（Finite Element, FE）模拟，在保持小于5%形变预测差异的同时，实现了至少十倍的提速，并可支撑大规模变形仿真与高通量设计探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剪纸（kirigami）结构在可展开和可重构结构中具有独特优势，但现有有限元（Finite Element, FE）方法计算成本高且易出现收敛问题，而简化的理论模型通常局限于特定设计与分析场景，缺乏通用的高效分析工具。

Method: 提出一个普遍适用的、基于杆臂单元（bar and hinge）的降阶模型，将整个剪纸结构表示为桁架杆（truss bars）、弯曲铰链（bending hinges）和扭簧（torsional springs）的组合；推导了经历拉伸与面外弯曲变形的结构，以及仅经历面内拉伸与旋转结构的刚度表达式。

Result: 通过与有限元分析及实验数据对比，模型在单切口及多切口复杂剪纸设计上均验证了准确性：形变预测差异小于5%，计算速度至少提升十倍；并成功模拟了剪纸蒙皮爬行器（kirigami-skinned crawlers）、高通量性能空间探索及面内剪纸启发超材料等复杂场景。

Conclusion: 该降阶模型兼具通用性、准确性和计算效率，能够支撑超越传统有限元方法实际限制的大变形结构仿真与高通量设计探索，为剪纸结构的分析设计提供了新的计算工具。

Abstract: Kirigami, the art of cutting sheets, offers unique characteristics such as shape morphing, stretchability, and adaptability, with applications in deployable and reconfigurable structures. While Finite Element (FE) approaches are widely used to analyze kirigami structures, they are computationally intensive and prone to convergence issues. On the other hand, existing simplified and theoretical models for evaluating kirigami are typically restricted to specific designs and analytical scenarios. This paper proposes a generalized, computationally efficient reduced-order model based on the bar and hinge approach for analyzing any kirigami system. The model represents the entire kirigami structure using truss bars, bending hinges, and torsional springs, capturing both the deformation and internal forces of the system. We derive stiffness expressions for kirigami structures that experience stretching and out-of-plane bending deformations, as well as for structures that experience only in-plane stretching and rotations. The accuracy and robustness of the model are validated through comparisons with FE analysis and experimental data on both single-cut kirigami designs and more complex designs with multiple cuts. Our results demonstrate less than 5% difference in deformation predictions and achieve at least a tenfold computational speedup compared to FE simulations. Additionally, we demonstrate the model's capabilities through simulating complex scenarios, including kirigami-skinned crawlers, high-throughput property-space exploration, and in-plane kirigami-inspired metamaterials. These examples demonstrate computational capabilities that enable large-deformation structural simulations and high-throughput design exploration beyond the practical limits of conventional FE methods.

</details>

*Emanuele Maggio*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该论文利用黎曼-布洛赫态（Riemann-Bloch states）在全布里渊区中一致地描述了孤立电子态的贝里相位（Berry phase），研究了开放路径贝里相位与有界位置算符（bounded position operator）的关系，并首次采用复平面正则化程序与准对称性来解释闭合回路中贝里相位随绕数或高斯型原子轨道展宽发生的非解析跳跃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝里相位是凝聚态物理中描述电子态拓扑性质的核心概念，但其数值计算严重依赖于倒空间中积分路径的选择以及基函数的具体形式。论文旨在建立一种在全布里渊区中一致且可正则化的贝里相位计算框架，并理解非解析跳跃产生的深层原因。

Method: 采用黎曼-布洛赫态对全布里渊区中的电子态进行一致描述，针对开放路径贝里相位考察其与有界位置算符的关系，并引入不同的高斯型原子轨道（GTO）形状因子；对于闭合回路上的贝里相位，在复平面中实施正则化程序，并首次利用额外的准对称性（quasi-symmetries）来理性化非解析跳跃的发生机理。

Result: 开放路径贝里相位与有界位置算符之间存在可解析的联系，且其具体形式依赖于GTO形状因子的选取；在闭合回路情形下，复平面正则化配合准对称性分析能够解释贝里相位随绕组数（winding number）或GTO展宽的演化中出现的非解析跳跃。

Conclusion: 该工作为在全布里渊区内一致计算贝里相位提供了一种新的理论框架，通过准对称性和复平面正则化首次系统解释了非解析跳跃的起源，对理解拓扑性质在高斯型局域基下的数值稳定性具有重要意义。

Abstract: The Berry phase of an isolated electronic state is evaluated for different integration paths in reciprocal space, thanks to Riemann-Bloch states providing a consistent description in the whole Brillouin zone.
  The relationship with the bounded position operator is investigated for the open-path Berry phase and with different form factors of the constituent Gaussian type atomic orbitals (GTOs).
  When the Berry phase is evaluated over a closed loop in reciprocal space, a regularisation procedure in the complex plane is adopted and additional \textit{quasi}-symmetries are exploited here for the first time in order to rationalise the occurrence of non-analytical jumps as a function of the winding number or of the GTO broadening.

</details>

Result: 理论表明，利用适当布置的辐射约束结构可实现强光电流产生效率；在III-V半导体结构中，所得光响应（photoresponse）比石墨烯中测得的结果高出若干个数量级。

Abstract: Electrons in two-dimensional electron gases in the presence of an out-of-plane magnetic field propagate along the edge with a high velocity of the order of the Fermi velocity. Under microwave and terahertz radiation, photon absorption by these electrons provides a pathway to realising sensitive radiation detection. Here, we develop a detailed quantum theory of the photocurrent generated in such a system by an incident electromagnetic wave and propose an experimental geometry for observing the predicted phenomenon. By using suitably arranged radiation confinement structures, a strong photocurrent generation efficiency can be obtained. We also demonstrate that the resulting photoresponse in III-V semiconductor structures can be orders of magnitude higher than that measured in graphene.

Conclusion: 净资源优势条件为4w^2(2^Δm - 1) > Δλ log(1/ε)。该方法无需修改QPE本身，可与读出阶段和态制备滤波结合，为小能隙量子系统的高效量子模拟提供了实用的电路压缩途径。

Abstract: We show that quantum phase estimation (QPE) circuits can be significantly compressed in depth by preprocessing the input operator with a sigmoid spectral filter before estimation. Applying a soft-step transformation f(lambda; tau,w) amplifies the effective gap to Delta_f > Delta_lambda (for w < 1/4), reducing the required precision to m_f = ceil(log2(1/Delta_f)) and compressing circuit depth by 2^(alpha Delta_m), where alpha = 1 for the LMR density-matrix exponentiation framework and alpha is in [0.11,0.42] for controlled-phase-gate circuits. We further show that the threshold parameter tau requires only O(w) accuracy, so classical preprocessing such as covariance diagonalisation or CASSCF avoids circularity. A net resource advantage occurs when 4w^2(2^Delta_m - 1) > Delta_lambda log(1/epsilon). For LiH, QPE output fidelity improves from 0.66 to 0.98 at a 1% hardware error rate. The method preserves the principal subspace to machine precision, requires no modification of QPE, and can be combined with readout-stage and state-preparation filtering. Abstract: Hybrid approaches that combine different spin qubit encodings offer promising advantages. By enabling error detection at the lowest encoding level, our approach effectively converts charge and nuclear noise into erasures, thereby suppressing error propagation and potentially enhancing the performance of quantum error-correction schemes. Abstract: We introduce cleft extensions for Hopf algebroids. Conclusion: 自对齐可变形活性膜为连接细胞力学、形状适应与自对齐到受限几何中集体组织迁移提供了通用框架，支持将单细胞尺度变形能力与组织尺度物质输运直接关联。

Abstract: Collective cell migration emerges from the interplay between motility, deformability and mechanical interactions, yet incorporating these ingredients into computationally efficient tissue models remains challenging. Here, we benchmark self-aligning deformable active membranes in a confined-flow geometry that mimics epithelial monolayer migration around a circular obstacle. In this model, cells are represented as deformable, adhesive membranes whose self-propulsion direction relaxes towards their velocity. By systematically varying the self-alignment timescale, cell-cell adhesion and inlet forcing, we characterize the resulting flows through collective alignment, relative density, neighbor rearrangements and spatial velocity fields. The model captures a broad spectrum of tissue behaviors, ranging from disordered, liquid-like flows to highly aligned, solid-like states. Compared with a related multiparticle model, self-aligning active membranes achieve stronger collective alignment, exhibit a more systematic density response and access states closer to both limits of the solid-liquid spectrum. We further show that increasing the target shape index promotes cell elongation and accelerates tissue flow, directly linking cell-scale deformability to tissue-scale transport. Finally, we compare simulated velocity profiles with experimental measurements from in vitro migrating MDCK epithelial cell monolayers and find qualitative agreement across multiple horizontal and vertical transects around the obstacle. These results establish self-aligning deformable active membranes as a versatile framework for connecting cell mechanics, shape adaptation and self-alignment to collective tissue migration in confined geometries.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 结果进一步表明，离子材料中增强的介电效应导致离子对非理想性比水溶液中的变化更大，因而对配对的影响可能比水释放更为显著。

Abstract: The dependence of ion pairing on osmotic stress may strongly affect the performance of ionic materials and membranes whose interior is often osmotically stresses, yet quantitative understanding of this dependence and, specifically, the effects of water and ion activities is limited. Motivated by this gap, we analyze the enhancement of ion pairing with osmotic pressure for concentrated aqueous KCl, NaCl, and LiCl solutions using molecular dynamics simulations. Based on rigorous thermodynamic relations, we separate the contributions of ion non-ideality to the pairing constant, varying with osmotic pressure, from other effects including water release and type of ion-pair. Our analysis reveals that ion non-ideality indirectly generates a stronger effect on pairing than the direct one of water release. However, its effect is moderated and may even be reversed for more hydrated pairs by a similarly large and opposite effect of ion-pair non-ideality assigned to varying dielectric properties of the solution and water restructuring upon pairing. The interplay between these contributions, including large and pair type-specific hydration effects on the cost of pairing, explains the observed opposing trends: pairing decreases with osmotic pressure for more hydrated solvent-separated pair types while increasing for contact pairs. The trend becomes more pronounced for more hydrated smaller cations, but was fairly independent of the water model used. The results further suggest that dielectric effects enhanced in ionic materials-and, as a result, larger variations of ion-pair non-ideality, compared with aqueous solutions, should have a more significant impact on pairing than water release.

Abstract: Drying particle suspensions is widely used to assemble particles and to fabricate porous functional layers for various applications, in which microstructural properties critically influence the overall device performance. Understanding the mechanisms governing drying-induced microstructure formation is therefore essential for predictive control of the resulting structures. In this work, we numerically investigate the evolution of microstructures during the drying of particle suspension films, with a particular focus on the role of particle-particle interactions. For weakly interacting particles, the particles assemble into hexagonal structures at the interface, and upon drying, trigger subsequent layer-by-layer assembly. We present a simple theoretical model to predict the time evolution of the layer thickness, validated against our simulation results. With strong particle interactions, the particles aggregate and form a network-like structure during drying, leading to a porous deposit. The porosity of the structure follows a power-law relationship with a dimensionless adhesion parameter that characterizes the particle-particle interaction force relative to the capillary force. By systematically varying the adhesion parameter, three packing regimes of the final structure are identified: hexagonal close packing, random close packing, and adhesive packing. Overall, our results demonstrate that particle-particle interactions play a decisive role in determining the final porous structure, providing practical guidance for tailoring functional layers through controlled manipulation of particle interactions.

Abstract: The reptation model postulates that entangled polymers slide within a fractal tube. Here we employ a model-independent relation between the zero-displacement probability and the mean-square displacement that applies to time-dependent fractal structures, enabling direct measurement of the fractal dimension $d_\mathrm{f}$ of the geometry experienced by monomer motion. For two-dimensional obstacle arrays and in the slip-link model, $d_\mathrm{f}$ agrees with the reptation prediction $d_\mathrm{f}=1/ν$ (where $ν$ is the Flory exponent). In polymer melts, however, we find $d_\mathrm{f} \approx 2.6$ --- a value close to the fractal dimension of percolation clusters, not the reptation value $d_\mathrm{f}=2$. This contrasts sharply with the reptation picture, in which a Rouse chain slides in a fractal structure with $d_\mathrm{f}=2$, spectral dimension $d_\mathrm{s}=1$, and walk dimension $d_\mathrm{w}=4$; our results point instead to a percolation-like scenario, characterized by $d_\mathrm{f}\approx 2.6$, $d_\mathrm{s}\approx 1.3$, and $d_\mathrm{w}\approx 4$ --- revealing a dynamically emergent, finite-size fractal geometry distinct from the static tube.

</details>

Motivation: 经典的Johnson-Kendall-Roberts（JKR）粘附接触理论是针对圆形接触而建立的，但实际软聚合物粘附接触中大量存在非圆形接触情形。已有研究缺乏对非圆形几何下接触形成与分离机制的系统的实验考察，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用聚二甲基硅氧烷（PDMS）交叉圆柱实验，将交叉角从30°变化至90°，从而在保持材料对不变的前提下，产生从高度伸长椭圆到近似圆形的一系列非圆形接触几何。实验分别测量加载与卸载分支，并利用面积等效半径c=sqrt(a*b)与几何平均曲率半径Reff=sqrt(R1*R2)对数据进行量纲化处理，同时引入Johnson-Greenwood椭圆形接触拟合和简化的粘弹性缩减模型进行分析。

Abstract: Adhesive contact measurements on soft polymers are commonly interpreted using Johnson-Kendall-Roberts (JKR) theory, which is formulated for circular contacts. Here, we examine contact formation and detachment in non-circular soft adhesive contacts using PDMS crossed-cylinder experiments. The crossing angle was varied from 30 to 90 degrees, producing contacts from highly elongated ellipses to nearly circular geometries while keeping the material pair fixed.
