This motivates use of the area-equivalent radius c=sqrt(a*b) and geometric-mean curvature radius Reff=sqrt(R1*R2). The loading branches follow a JKR-type linearization and yield a nearly angle- and preload-independent work of adhesion, W_load=24 mJ/m^2. Johnson-Greenwood elliptical-contact fits give comparable values. In contrast, unloading and pull-off are strongly history dependent. The unloading branches require a substantially larger effective separation energy, W_unload,eff, which increases with preload and decreasing crossing angle. A reduced viscoelastic model based on the same area-equivalent description captures the principal unloading response over 50-80 degrees using a single shared parameter set across angles and preloads. These results show that contact formation is governed primarily by area-equivalent scaling, whereas detachment is governed by geometry- and history-dependent dissipative separation.

</details>

Conclusion: 实验结果确立了Fourier准晶体作为一种物理平台，其波传输行为可通过倒易空间中衍射峰的排布方式加以控制。

Abstract: Fourier quasicrystals are aperiodic structures whose diffraction spectrum consists not of a dense set of Bragg peaks, as in ordinary quasicrystals, but of isolated ones scattered across a discrete, nonperiodic set. We put this prediction to the test using surface water waves scattering off a two-dimensional Fourier quasicrystal. Full-field measurements reveal three distinct transport regimes as the incident wavevector increases: transparency, selective scattering, and strong scattering. By reconstructing the structure factor from the measured wavefields, we directly relate these regimes to the underlying reciprocal-space structure. Our results establish Fourier quasicrystals as a physical platform in which wave transport can be controlled through the organization of diffraction peaks in reciprocal space.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Magneto-active elastomers (MAEs) -- composites of magnetic nano-/micro-particles embedded in a soft polymer matrix -- are promising for soft robotics, as their shape and mechanical properties can be controlled by an applied magnetic field. Most MAEs are filled with magnetically soft (MS) micro-particles, such as carbonyl iron powder (CIP). We employ molecular dynamics to study the differences between thin MAE layers with MS and magnetically hard (MH) filler particles having the same saturation magnetization. We find that both MH and MS elastomers converge to the same high-field state -- a labyrinth of bundled, field-aligned chains -- but do so through distinct pathways: MH MAEs break their zero-field chains, which lie parallel to the MAE layer plane (in-plane), and rotate them into alignment with an external magnetic field, whereas MS MAEs gradually build up field-aligned chains from neighboring particles. We show that the MS model reproduces the magnetization curves and surface roughness of CIP-based MAEs for magnetic fields close to saturation, while maintaining the observed qualitative features at lower field strengths. The mismatch between simulation and experimental results at low fields suggests the need for a MS model that accounts for the multi-domain nature of carbonyl iron microparticles.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在检验参考文献中关于环电流和时间反演对称破缺的声称，判断其数据结论是否可靠，特别是评估NQR谱线宽、不对称线形和涌现场（h_loc）等观测结果是否被错误归因。

Conclusion: 参考文献中的数据不足以确立由环电流引起的时间反演对称破缺，也不足以支持在T*≈120 K处存在相变的结论。

Abstract: In this Comment, exact diagonalization and quantitative analysis show that the reported signatures of loop currents in Ref. [arXiv:2605.05101; https://doi.org/10.1038/s41567-026-03339-8] can instead be explained by crystalline mosaicity and pre-transitional CDW fluctuations. Specifically: (i) the NQR linewidth tracks a standard Curie-Weiss (CW) law with no anomalies; (ii) the Zp-NQR asymmetric lineshapes stem from an angular mosaic spread, reproducing the spin-transition-dependent skewness; and (iii) the reported emergent field ($h_{\rm loc}$) arises from particular fitting constraints and attributing the angular distribution to an internal field rather than mosaicity. Consequently, the data in Ref. [arXiv:2605.05101; https://doi.org/10.1038/s41567-026-03339-8] remain insufficient to establish TRSB due to loop currents or a phase transition at $T^* \sim$ 120 K.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究已表明，当单个对称性物种介导跃迁且局域自旋算符仅包含该物种的一次出现时，点群完全决定了磁跃迁强度随Q的依赖关系，且此类普适函数已被列表归纳。本文的动机是探讨当这些条件不满足时，点群对称性是否仍能对跃迁强度施加约束，从而推广消光现象的研究范围。

Method: 作者对单晶进行非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）实验，解析磁跃迁强度随动量转移矢量Q的变化。在理论上，作者推导了一个方程，其每个零点（zero）均为消光点；该方程由两个能级的对称性物种、磁性位点的位置和散射振幅共同构建。当对称等价位点的每个轨道对介导物种贡献单次出现，或位点相位在某个子群下以单一公因子重现时，消光点能以闭式（closed form）给出。

Result: 结果表明，即使不满足先前"单一对称性物种介导跃迁"的充分条件，点群对称性仍能确定若干特定动量转移点，使跃迁强度对所有具有该团簇对称性的哈密顿量严格为零。作者将此类动量转移命名为"消光"，并在两种情形下给出了消光点的闭式解。

Conclusion: 本文确立了消光现象在更一般条件下仍然存在，即点群对称性对磁跃迁强度的约束作用比此前认识到的更为普遍。消光方程及其闭式解为利用中子散射数据识别分子团簇的对称性和磁性结构提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Inelastic neutron scattering on a single crystal resolves how the intensity of a magnetic transition of a molecular spin cluster varies with the momentum-transfer vector $\mathbf{Q}$. The point group fixes that dependence completely when a single symmetry species mediates the transition and the local spin operators contain only one occurrence of that species. Functions for such universal $\mathbf{Q}$ dependences have been tabulated. As we show here, even when these conditions are not fulfilled, the point group still fixes, at a given geometry, momentum transfers at which the intensity vanishes for every Hamiltonian that has the symmetry of the cluster. We call such momentum transfers extinctions and derive an equation whose every zero is an extinction, built from the symmetry species of the two levels and from the positions and scattering amplitudes of the magnetic sites. The extinctions follow in closed form in two cases: when each orbit of symmetry-equivalent sites contributes a single occurrence of the mediating species, and when the site phases recur under a subgroup up to one common factor.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文证明，在支配变分计算的范数（norm）下，描述相互作用费米子所需的Slater行列式（Slater determinant）最多不超过两个，且与粒子数和目标精度无关。该结论建立了广义Slater-Jastrow神经量子态（Slater-Jastrow neural quantum states）的变分完备性（variational completeness）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述相互作用费米子通常需要组合数量级的Slater行列式，当前最先进的费米子神经量子态也依赖于堆叠越来越多的行列式。本文旨在回答一个基础性问题：变分描述究竟需要多少个Slater行列式，并探索是否存在更经济的构造方式。

Method: 作者从数学上证明了一个普遍性定理：在监督变分能量（variational energy）的一阶Sobolev范数（first-order Sobolev norm）下，任意费米子波函数可由一个预先指定的通用Slater行列式乘以光滑玻色子波函数来逼近，该构造在至多三维空间内成立且不依赖系统和具体态。对于库仑相互作用需要二阶Sobolev范数（second-order Sobolev norm）的情形，仅在三维空间中需要额外增加一个固定行列式。

Result: 核心结果包括：（1）一阶Sobolev范数下只需一个通用Slater行列式即可逼近任意费米子波函数；（2）二阶Sobolev范数下最多需要两个固定行列式，且仅在三维空间中需要第二个；（3）反称性（antisymmetry）的代价至多为两个通用行列式，不牺牲表达能力，即广义Slater-Jastrow神经量子态是变分完备的。

Conclusion: 反称约束最多只要求两个通用Slater行列式就能实现变分完备性，这一结果从根本上简化了费米子神经量子态的构造，为变分蒙特卡罗（variational Monte Carlo）方法中的局部能量方差控制提供了理论保证。

Abstract: How many Slater determinants does an accurate variational description of interacting fermions require? Exact expansions in a finite basis need combinatorially many, and state-of-the-art fermionic neural quantum states stack growing numbers of them. We prove that, in the norms that govern variational calculations, at most two are needed, independently of the number of particles and of the target accuracy. A single universal Slater determinant-specified in advance, independent of both the system and the state-multiplied by a smooth bosonic wave function approximates any fermionic wave function in up to three spatial dimensions in the first-order Sobolev norm, which controls the variational energy. Reaching the second-order Sobolev norm-for Coulomb interactions, the domain of the Hamiltonian, which bounds the variance of the local energy at the core of variational Monte Carlo-requires at most one additional fixed determinant, and only in three dimensions. Antisymmetry therefore costs at most two universal determinants and no expressiveness: generalized Slater-Jastrow neural quantum states are variationally complete.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期的数值研究中发现了多种SU(2)陈-西蒙斯-物质理论的出现，但对其拓扑相和量子临界性的解析理解仍然不足。作者旨在通过解析方法对这些涌现的奇异拓扑相进行系统分类和定量刻画。

Result: 对于奇数k，拓扑序可出现在任意LSM反常的系统中且对称性不能置换任意子；非平凡LSM反常时恰有一个SET相，平凡LSM反常时有九个SET相。对于偶数k，拓扑序只能在平凡LSM反常系统中出现；当k不在{6,10,14,…}中时有16个SET相，当k在该集合中时有4种任意子置换方式对应64个SET相。两种物质场类型的耦合理论中均获得了双线性算符标度维数的1/N_f阶修正结果。

Conclusion: 这些结果加深了对涌现奇异拓扑相和量子临界性的理解，并为进一步的探索提供了有用的指导。

Abstract: Motivated by recent numerical studies where various $SU(2)$ Chern-Simons-matter theories emerge, we analytically study topological phases and quantum criticality in two-dimensional systems described by such theories. First, we classify $SU(2)_k$ topological orders in all lattice spin systems with a $p4\times SO(3)$ symmetry, where $k$ is an arbitrary nonzero integer. We find that for each odd $k$, the topological order can emerge in systems with an arbitrary Lieb-Schultz-Mattis (LSM) anomaly, and the symmetry cannot permute anyons. If the system has a nontrivial (respectively, trivial) LSM anomaly, then there is exactly one (respectively, nine) symmetry-enriched topological (SET) phases. On the other hand, $SU(2)_k$ topological order with any even $k$ can only emerge in systems with a trivial LSM anomaly. If $k\notin\{6, 10, 14, \cdots\}$, the symmetry cannot permute anyons, and there are 16 SET phases. If $k\in\{6, 10, 14, \cdots\}$, there are 4 different ways how the symmetry can permute anyons, and there are 64 SET phases. Next, we analyze the $SU(2)_k$ Chern-Simons theories coupled to $N_f$ flavors of gapless matter fields that can be either bosonic or fermionic. For both types of theories, we consider a joint large-$N_f$ and large-$k$ limit with $N_f/k$ fixed, and compute the scaling dimensions of the bilinear operators of the bosons or fermions to the order of $1/N_f$. These results sharpen our understanding of these emergent exotic topological phases and quantum criticality, and provide useful guidance to explore them further.

*Samuel Klein,Thomas M. Linker,Louis Conreux,Daniel Ratner,Apurva Mehta,Makoto Tachibana,Jiemin Li,Jonathan Pelliciari,Valentina Bisogni,Wei He,Xiangpeng Luo,Mark P. M. Dean,Marton K. Lajer,Michael Kagan,Joshua J. Turner,Yongqiang Cheng,Sean Gasiorowski*

Abstract: The Nernst effect, which enables the conversion of a heat current into a transverse voltage under magnetic field or spin magnetization, holds significant promise for energy harvesting and thermal management in future electronics. However, the conventional Nernst effect is fundamentally constrained by the orthogonality requirement that the applied field or spontaneous magnetization must be perpendicular to the plane defined by the temperature gradient and the induced voltage. Here we report that symmetry-tailored ultrathin films of a prototypical ferromagnetic oxide exhibit anomalous Nernst effect arising from intrinsic coupling to spontaneous in-plane spin magnetization. Systematic magnetothermoelectric measurements under spherical rotations of the magnetic field reveal that a pronounced Nernst signal, comparable in magnitude to the out-of-plane response, emerges robustly associated with out-of-plane orbital magnetization.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: A magnetic impurity embedded in a metal is collectively screened by Fermi-surface quasiparticles into a many-body spin-singlet ground state, forming a Kondo cloud of size $ξ_{\rm K}\sim\hbar v_F/(k_B T_{\rm K})$. This kinematic picture collapses in flat bands, where $v_F=0$ and the hierarchy of dispersive energy shells is absent. Here we show that the missing organizing principle is quantum geometry. A magnetic impurity coupled to an isolated flat band selects a single active bath mode: a coherent superposition of flat-band Bloch states weighted by the hybridization factor $v(\mathbf{k})$, while all orthogonal flat-band modes remain dark. The resulting flat-band Kondo problem is a quantum geometric molecule, with an algebraic Kondo scale set by the total projected hybridization strength rather than a logarithmic-renormalization scale. In real space, the impurity-bath spin correlation defines a quantum geometric Kondo cloud. Its cloud-size tensor admits a gauge-invariant decomposition into a hybridization-weighted quantum metric, a dressed Berry-connection covariance, and a positive hybridization-gradient term, yielding the lower bound $ξ_{\rm K}^2\geq \sum_{\mathbf{k}}ρ(\mathbf{k}){\rm Tr}\,g(\mathbf{k})$, where $ρ(\mathbf{k})=|v(\mathbf{k})|^2/\sum_{\mathbf{k}}|v(\mathbf{k})|^2$. Our result reveals that, in flat bands, Kondo screening is governed by the quantum geometry and interference structure of the impurity-selected Bloch wave packet, rather than Fermi-surface kinematics.

Abstract: Twisted transition metal dichalcogenides offer an exceptionally tunable moiré platform for studying correlation physics beyond conventional condensed matter systems. In particular, the intriguing interplay between the displacement field and the twist angle remains to be fully resolved. In this paper, we use large-scale density matrix renormalization group simulations to study the minimal moiré Hubbard model on a triangular lattice at half-filling, where the displacement field effect is captured by a spin-dependent staggered flux. We find that the displacement field significantly enriches the triangular Hubbard phase diagram in several qualitative ways. It rapidly destabilizes the chiral spin liquid phase beyond a narrow weak-field regime, induces pronounced chiral correlations in the strong-coupling $120^\circ$-antiferromagnetic phase, and stabilizes incommensurate spin-density wave phases at weaker coupling. We further find signatures of a continuous transition between the chiral spin liquid and chiral antiferromagnetic phases at a finite displacement field, potentially driven by spinon condensation. Our results uncover rich displacement-field-driven many-body physics and provide useful guidance for future experiments in moiré superlattice systems.

</details>

### [82] [Cosmographic Reconstruction of the Quintessence Potential in Scalar-Tensor Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14183)
*G. Antonopoulos,L. Perivolaropoulos*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文将Chakraborty、Dunsby & Scherrer的宇宙学重建程序推广到标量-张量引力（scalar-tensor gravity）情形。作者在Jordan框架下，以非最小耦合函数F(Φ)和宇宙学参数(q,j,s)为基础，推导出了势斜率、耦合斜率及其曲率参数的封闭形式表达式，并将其全部转化为可由观测数据直接约束的形式。利用DESI DR2重子声学振荡测量和月球激光测距（Lunar Laser Ranging）数据评估重建结果，发现势斜率可精确恢复至十分之几量级，而曲率参数基本不受约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有宇宙重建程序仅适用于最小耦合情况，需要将其推广到更一般的标量-张量引力框架，以检验非最小耦合引入的额外自由度如何影响对暗能量势（dark energy potential）的重建约束。

Method: 在Jordan框架下，对一般非最小耦合F(Φ)推导势斜率λ_U、耦合斜率λ_F及其曲率参数Γ_U和Γ_F的精确封闭表达式，将这些量用宇宙学参数(q,j,s)、Planck质量运行率α_M及其导数表示；进而将α_M及其导数用描述引力常数G∝F^{-1}时间变化的系数g_n表达，并利用DESI DR2 BAO数据与月球激光测距对G点变化的约束进行数值评估，同时传播宇宙学参数的不确定性至重建势。

Result: 所有表达式在最小耦合极限下可解析地还原为Chakraborty等人的结果（已通过符号验证）。势斜率λ_U可在十分之几的精度内恢复，而曲率Γ_U基本不受约束。标量动能项的正定性等价于w_eff,0≥-1，因此最小耦合quintessence所封闭的宇宙学参数空间区域恰为幽灵区（phantom region），而非最小耦合通过引力塔系数g_2重新开启该区域。由于月球激光测距限制|g_1|≲2×10^{-4}而重建对g_2~O(0.1)敏感，该框架预设当前时刻α_M正穿过零点，这与Damour-Nordtvedt最小耦合吸引子（least-coupling attractor）的预期一致。

Conclusion: 该重建框架成功将标量-张量引力中的势与耦合参数转化为可观测形式，并验证了最小耦合极限的正确性。非最小耦合可以重新打开最小耦合quintessence所禁止的幽灵参数空间，但其可行性取决于α_M在当前时刻过零，这一假设由Damour-Nordtvedt吸引子机制自然支持。

Abstract: We generalise the cosmographic reconstruction programme of Chakraborty, Dunsby & Scherrer to scalar-tensor gravity. Working in the Jordan frame with a general non-minimal coupling $F(Φ)$, we derive exact closed-form expressions for the potential slope $λ_U$, the coupling slope $λ_F$, and their curvature parameters $Γ_U$ and $Γ_F$ in terms of the cosmographic parameters $(q,j,s)$, the Planck-mass running $α_M = d\ln F/d\ln a$ and its derivatives. We express $α_M$ and its derivatives in terms of a set of coefficients $g_n$ describing the time variation of the gravitational constant $G\propto F^{-1}$, casting all four quantities in fully observable form. We then evaluate the reconstruction against current data, including DESI~DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation measurements and Lunar Laser Ranging constraints on $\dot G/G$, propagating the cosmographic uncertainties through to the reconstructed potential. All expressions reduce analytically to those of Chakraborty, Dunsby & Scherrer in the minimally-coupled limit, which we verify symbolically. We find that the potential slope $λ_U$ is recovered at the few-tenths level while the curvature $Γ_U$ is essentially unconstrained. We further show that the positivity of the scalar kinetic term is equivalent to $w_{\rm eff,0}\ge-1$, so that the region of cosmographic parameter space closed to minimally-coupled quintessence is exactly the phantom region, and that the non-minimal coupling reopens it through a single coefficient $g_2$ of the gravitational tower. Because Lunar Laser Ranging forces $|g_1|\lesssim2\times10^{-4}$ while the reconstruction is sensitive to $g_2\sim O(0.1)$, the framework presupposes that $α_M$ is passing through zero at the present epoch, as expected on the Damour--Nordtvedt least-coupling attractor.

</details>

### [83] [One-sided type-D Ricci-flat multi-centre metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14410)
*Yu Chen*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文基于Tod形式的简化版本（其本身建立在LeBrun–Tod形式上），构造了一类单侧D型（type-D）Ricci平坦多中心度量。这些度量是Hermitian非Kähler且共形Kähler的，由一对生成势决定：辅助三维平坦空间中的轴对称调和函数（与Tod最初提出的不同）及其调和共轭。作者系统地研究了这些多中心度量的杆结构（rod structure）、渐近结构（asymptotic structure），并从其中恢复了若干已知闭式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多中心Ricci平坦度量在广义相对论和数学物理中具有重要地位，而构造满足特定性质（如Hermitian非Kähler、共形Kähler）的新类型度量仍是开放问题。本文旨在通过简化Tod假说（Tod's ansatz），系统地构造并分类一类新的D型Ricci平坦多中心度量，以期扩展已知解族并揭示其与逆散射方法（inverse-scattering method）构造的平直空间多孤子解之间的联系。

Method: 作者采用简化形式的Tod假说（基于LeBrun–Tod假说）构造度量，利用辅助三维平坦空间中的轴对称调和函数及其调和共轭作为生成势来完全确定度量。通过对度量的系统性分析（包括杆结构、渐近结构），并与作者此前基于逆散射方法构造的平直空间多孤子解框架进行对比，研究这些度量的自由孤子（free-soliton）与幻影孤子（phantom-soliton）数目的差异。

Result: 构造了一类单侧D型Ricci平坦多中心度量，证明了它们是Hermitian非Kähler且共形Kähler的。当中心数n≤3时，这些度量在平直空间多孤子解框架中的自由孤子数比幻影孤子数多2或3；作者推测该结论对一般n也成立。此外，从这些度量中恢复了多种已知的闭式例子。

Conclusion: 本文提出的简化Tod假说能够系统地生成一类新的、具有良好几何性质的多中心度量，并揭示了其与逆散射方法构造的多孤子解之间的深刻联系。自由孤子数与幻影孤子数的差异规律可能对一般中心数n普遍成立，这为更高中心数的推广研究指明了方向。

Abstract: We employ a simplified form of Tod's ansatz---which is built on the LeBrun--Tod ansatz---to construct one-sided type-D Ricci-flat multi-centre metrics. These metrics are Hermitian non-K{ä}hler and conformally K{ä}hler, and they are determined by a pair of generating potentials: an axisymmetric harmonic function (different from the one originally proposed by Tod) in an auxiliary 3D flat space and its harmonic conjugate. We carry out a systematic study of these multi-centre metrics, including their rod structure, asymptotic structure and recovering from them various known closed-form examples. In particular, we show that, when fitted into the scheme of multi-soliton solutions on flat space constructed by the present author using the inverse-scattering method, these metrics, with number of centres $n\le 3$, have a free-soliton number 2 or 3 greater than their phantom-soliton number; we conjecture that this is true for general $n$.

</details>

### [84] [Pairton: Iterative Reconstruction of Short-Lived Particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14278)
*Andreas Hermansen,Chris Scheulen,Tobias Golling*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: Pairton是一种迭代式框架，用于在高能碰撞事件中重建短寿命粒子。它将粒子重建形式化为图结构上的掩码预测过程，利用基于pairformer的架构动态更新成对表示（pairwise representations），在完全强子$t\bar{t}$衰变中取得了最先进性能，并可为其他拓扑结构提供通用的重建范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能碰撞事件中短寿命粒子无法直接被探测器观测，需要通过其衰变产物进行重建，而现有方法在复杂拓扑下准确重建粒子层次结构方面存在挑战。本文旨在提出一种通用且灵活的重建框架，以处理粒子衰变关系的推断问题。

Method: 将粒子重建形式化为图结构上的掩码预测过程（masked prediction process），学习与衰变产物因子分解一致的条件分布，并迭代预测表示粒子衰变关系的邻接矩阵（adjacency matrix）中的边。采用基于pairformer架构的方法，动态更新成对表示以纳入全局事件一致性（global event consistency）。

Result: 在完全强子$t\bar{t}$衰变事件上展示了最先进的性能（state-of-the-art performance），验证了该方法在粒子重建任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: Pairton提供了一种通用、灵活的粒子重建范式，可轻松扩展到其他衰变拓扑，将现代生成建模（generative modelling）的思想与高能物理相结合。

Abstract: We present Pairton, an iterative framework for reconstructing short-lived particles in high-energy collision events. By formulating particle reconstruction as a masked prediction process over graph structures, Pairton learns conditional distributions consistent with a factorised decomposition of decay products and iteratively predicts edges in the adjacency matrix representing particle decay relationships. Leveraging a pairformer-based architecture with dynamically updated pairwise representations, our method incorporates global event consistency. We demonstrate state-of-the-art performance on fully hadronic $t\bar{t}$ decays. Pairton provides a general, flexible paradigm for particle reconstruction and can be readily extended to other topologies, bridging ideas from modern generative modelling and high-energy physics.

</details>


### [85] [Enhanced Three-Particle Contribution to Electroweak Penguin $B$-Meson Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14341)
*Yong-Kang Huang,Yu-Ming Wang,Xue-Chen Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次使用软共线有效理论（soft-collinear effective theory, SCET）计算了由三粒子B介子分布振幅（three-particle B-meson distribution amplitude）导致的稀有b→{s,d}ℓ⁺ℓ⁻遍举衰变的次前导扭曲（subleading twist）修正，并达到次前导阶（NLO）精度，由此补全了重夸克展开中弱有效哈密顿量强子矩阵元在领前幂次上的分解分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀有b→{s,d}ℓ⁺ℓ⁻衰变的完整分解分析尚缺最后一项关键要素，即三粒子B介子分布振幅贡献的次前导扭曲修正，有必要填补这一空白以获得完整的因子化框架。

Method: 采用软共线有效理论（soft-collinear effective theory, SCET），计算三粒子B介子分布振幅在次前导阶（next-to-leading order, NLO）精度下的次前导扭曲修正；同时纳入迷观散射（spectator-scattering）振幅与弱湮灭（weak annihilation）拓扑结构的次次前导阶（NNLO）QCD修正。

Result: 提供了改进的场论预言，针对电弱企鹅（electroweak penguin）B→{K,π}ℓ⁺ℓ⁻衰变中的现象学感兴趣观测量给出了精化的理论预测。

Conclusion: 该工作补全了重夸克展开在领前幂次上对弱有效哈密顿量强子矩阵元进行完整因子化分析的最后缺失环节，并改善了相关稀有衰变观测量（observable）的理论预言精度。

Abstract: We compute for the first time the subleading twist correction to the exclusive rare $b \to \left \{s, d \right \} \ell^{+} \ell^{-}$ decays from the three-particle $B$-meson distribution amplitude at next-to-leading order accuracy by employing the soft-collinear effective theory. This constitutes the last missing ingredient for the complete factorization analysis of the hadronic matrix element of the weak effective Hamiltonian at leading power in the heavy quark expansion. Incorporating further a variety of the next-to-next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to the spectator-scattering amplitude and the weak annihilation topology, we then present the improved field-theoretic predictions for phenomenologically interesting observables in the electroweak penguin $B \to \left \{K, π\right \} \ell^{+} \ell^{-}$ decays.

</details>


### [86] [NNLO QCD corrections to $WWγ$ production at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14419)
*Paolo Garbarino,Massimiliano Grazzini,Stefan Kallweit*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了 WWγ 产生过程在量子色动力学（QCD）次次领头阶（NNLO）下的辐射修正，完整考虑了 W 玻色子的轻子衰变与离壳效应（off-shell effects）。除双圈振幅的有限部分采用软光子近似（soft-photon approximation）评估外，计算是精确的。数值结果表明，在质心能量 13 TeV 下，NNLO 修正使次领头阶（NLO）结果增加约 16%，微扰不确定度降至 ±4% 水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三玻色子产生过程（triboson production processes）对于研究四重规范玻色子耦合（quartic gauge-boson couplings）至关重要，而 WWγ 产生过程的精确理论预言是相关实验测量的基础，因此需要更高阶的辐射修正来计算。

Method: 作者在 QCD NNLO 精度下计算 WWγ 产生过程，完整纳入 W 玻色子的轻子衰变和离壳效应。计算中除双圈振幅的有限部分用软光子近似处理外均为精确计算；以 Zγ 产生过程为参考过程，利用其精确双圈振幅来推导近似方法的保守误差估计，再应用于 WWγ 产生过程。

Result: 在质心能量 √s=13 TeV 和典型选择截断下，NNLO 修正使 NLO 结果增加约 16%，微扰不确定度降至 ±4% 水平。软光子近似带来的不确定度在千分之几量级，远小于残余微扰不确定度。

Conclusion: 软光子近似的误差在基准截面（fiducial cross section）和主要微分分布（differential distributions）中均处于千分之几量级，显著低于残余微扰不确定度，说明该近似方法对 WWγ 产生过程的高阶修正计算是可靠且有效的。

Abstract: Triboson production processes are crucial to study quartic gauge-boson couplings. We present the computation of the radiative corrections to $WWγ$ production at the next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in QCD. The leptonic decays of the $W$ bosons and off-shell effects are fully included. The calculation is exact, apart from the finite part of the two-loop amplitudes, which is evaluated in a soft-photon approximation. We validate our approach by using $Zγ$ production as a reference process, where we take advantage of the availability of the exact two-loop amplitudes to derive a conservative error estimate for our approximation, and then apply it to $WWγ$ production. For typical selection cuts, at the centre-of-mass energy $\sqrt{s}=13$ TeV, the NNLO corrections increase the next-to-leading order (NLO) result by about $16\%$, and the perturbative uncertainties are reduced to the $\pm4\%$ level. The uncertainty from the soft approximation turns out to be at the few per mille level, largely subdominant compared to the residual perturbative uncertainties, both for the fiducial cross section and the most relevant differential distributions.

</details>


### [87] [Understanding the enhanced $φη^{\prime}$ decay mode of the $φ(2170)$ through strange-meson loops](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14440)
*Qin-Song Zhou,Jun-Zhang Wang,Dan Guo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本研究针对类奇异子（strangeonium）态φ(2170)的争议本质，通过联合分析e+e-→φη和e+e-→φη′截面，发现短距离机制（真空夸克对产生）无法解释φη′/φη强度比，而奇介子圈（strange-meson loop）导致的远距离跃迁自然增强该比值。分析还揭示高精度φη数据倾向于支持一个位于约2.15 GeV、宽度约25 MeV的额外窄矢量态存在，该态可能成为轻矢量区外强子（exotic hadron）候选者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: φ(2170)的物理本质长期存在争议。最近的e+e-→φη(′)测量显示一个引人注目的谜团：在φη′道中出现宽的φ(2170)类结构而φη道中没有，尽管φη′存在强烈的相空间抑制。这一反常大的φη′衰变份额亟需理论解释。

Method: 在激发奇异子（excited strangeonium）框架下，联合分析e+e-→φη和e+e-→φη′截面：包含来自真空夸克对产生（vacuum quark-pair creation）的短距离振幅，以及由奇介子圈介导的远距离跃迁。同时测试了Γ(Y→φη)/Γ(Y→φη′)=0.25、1和4三种比值作为拟合输入。

Result: 短距离机制预测的φη′/φη强度比过小，无法解释数据；而奇介子圈因K(*)K(*)η(′)顶点处的SU(3)味道因子天然压低φη跃迁并增强φη′跃迁，从而自然提升该比值。两种截面的整体描述支持远距离动力学在激发矢量奇异子的隐奇异（hidden-strangeness）衰变中的重要作用。此外，高精度φη数据仍倾向于存在一个位于约2.15 GeV、宽度约25 MeV的额外窄矢量态。

Conclusion: 激发奇异子贡献结合远距离圈机制可同时描述φη和φη′截面。若窄态被证实，将成为轻矢量区有前景的外强子候选者。然而，当前数据无法通过Γ(Y→φη)/Γ(Y→φη′)比值来区分该窄态的不同内部构型。

Abstract: The nature of the strangeonium-like $φ(2170)$ remains controversial. Recent measurements of $e^+e^-\toφη^{(\prime)}$ reveal a striking puzzle: a broad $φ(2170)$-like structure appears in the $φη^\prime$ channel but not in $φη$, despite the strong phase-space suppression of $φη^\prime$. We investigate this unexpectedly large $φη^\prime$ decay fraction within the excited-strangeonium assignment. A combined analysis of the $e^+e^-\toφη$ and $e^+e^-\toφη^\prime$ cross sections is performed by including short-distance amplitudes from vacuum quark-pair creation and long-distance transitions mediated by strange-meson loops. The short-distance mechanism predicts too small a $φη^\prime/φη$ strength ratio to explain the data. In contrast, strange-meson loops naturally enhance this ratio because the SU(3)-flavor factor at the $K^{(*)}K^{(*)}η^{(\prime)}$ vertex suppresses the transition to $φη$ relative to $φη^\prime$. The resulting overall description of both cross sections supports an important role for long-distance dynamics in hidden-strangeness decays of excited vector strangeonia. More intriguingly, although the excited strangeonium contributions have been included, the current high-precision $φη$ data still favor the existence of an extra narrow vector state near $2.15~\mathrm{GeV}$ with a width of about $25~\mathrm{MeV}$. If confirmed, it would be a promising exotic-hadron candidate in the light-vector sector. Furthermore, we test $Γ(Y\toφη)/Γ(Y\toφη^\prime)=0.25$, $1$, and $4$ as fit inputs and obtain similarly good descriptions of the cross sections in all three cases. The present data therefore do not allow this ratio to discriminate among different internal configurations of the narrow state.

</details>


### [88] [Vector-Like Fermions at FCC-ee: NLO Higgs-Strahlung Signatures and Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14538)
*Carlo Marzo,Vinicius Padovani,Daniele Rizzo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了矢量类费米子（vector-like fermions，VLFs）在单圈阶对Higgs-strahlung（ZH产生）截面的修正效应。研究表明，在由Yukawa耦合驱动的情景中，当前LHC搜索仍然允许的相当一部分参数空间可产生FCC-ee千分比灵敏度可探测的截面偏移；而在混合驱动的情景中，现有电弱精确测量对混合角的约束已排除可观测效应。结论指出VL Yukawa耦合是未来e⁺e⁻对撞机Higgs-strahlung精密测量最有前景的探测目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Higgs-strahlung截面积是未来e⁺e⁻ Higgs工厂（如FCC-ee）的旗舰精密观测量，而矢量类费米子作为标准模型（Standard Model，SM）扩展的常见候选新粒子，可能通过辐射修正影响该可观测量。论文旨在系统评估不同VLF模型情景下这一精密测量对新物理的探测潜力。

Method: 考虑四种标准模型基准扩展情景：两种由纯内部Yukawa耦合驱动（VL夸克或VL轻子双重态及其单态伙伴耦合到Higgs玻色子），两种由单态VL直接与第三代带电轻子或中微子混合驱动。全程在壳重整化方案（on-shell renormalization scheme）下工作，并对重整化群（renormalization group）跑动施加微扰性约束；在单圈阶分别计算普遍性自能贡献、非普遍性顶点贡献，以及倾斜参数（oblique parameters）S和T及H→γγ衰变率作为互补交叉检验。

Result: 在两种Yukawa驱动情景中，当前LHC搜索仍允许的相当大比例参数空间可产生达到或超过FCC-ee千分比灵敏度的σ(ZH)偏移。在两种混合驱动情景中，现有电弱精密测量对相关混合角的约束已排除可观测效应。

Conclusion: VL Yukawa耦合而非与SM费米子的残余混合，是未来e⁺e⁻对撞机上Higgs-strahlung精密测量更有前景的探测目标。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop effects of vector-like fermions (VLFs) on the Higgs-strahlung cross-section, the flagship precision observable of future $e^+e^-$ Higgs factories such as FCC-ee. We consider four benchmark extensions of the Standard Model (SM): two in which a VL quark (VLQ) or VL lepton (VLL) doublet, together with its singlet partners, couples to the Higgs boson through purely internal Yukawa interactions, and two in which a single VL singlet mixes directly with the third-generation charged lepton or neutrino. Working throughout in the on-shell renormalization scheme and imposing perturbativity bounds on the relevant couplings under renormalization-group (RG) running, we compute both the universal self-energy and the non-universal vertex contributions to the Higgs-strahlung amplitude at one loop, together with the oblique parameters $S$ and $T$ and the $H\toγγ$ rate as complementary cross-checks. In the two Yukawa-driven scenarios, we find that a sizable fraction of the parameter space still allowed by current LHC searches produces shifts in $σ(ZH)$ at or above the per-mille sensitivity envisioned for FCC-ee, offering a radiative probe of these couplings. In the two mixing-driven scenarios, existing electroweak-precision bounds on the relevant mixing angles already preclude an observable effect, although we characterize the underlying loop dynamics in full generality for future reference. These results identify the VL Yukawa couplings, rather than any residual mixing with the SM fermions, as the more promising target for Higgs-strahlung precision measurements at a future $e^+e^-$ collider.

</details>

### [89] [On first-order thermodynamic equilibrium conditions for fluid-fluid and solid-phase interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13603)
*Nicodemo Di Pasquale,Thomas Hudson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于Larché和Cahn针对固体与流体接触系统的约束变分表述，发展了一个统一的热力学框架，将体相与界面平衡条件统一为单一热力学泛函的驻定条件。与忽视界面贡献的早期理论不同，该框架将界面热力学直接纳入变分原理，为体相、界面与构型热力学提供了共同的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期理论未完整处理界面贡献，因此无法描述表面效应显著的系统。作者旨在构建一个能够同时涵盖体相、界面及构型平衡的统一热力学框架，以弥补这一理论空白。

Method: 从Larché和Cahn的固体-流体接触系统约束变分表述出发，构建一个统一的热力学泛函；先针对流体-流体系统建立基础数学结构，再推广至固态-流体界面。不同类别的容许变分（admissible variations）自然地导出经典的非均质系统平衡关系。

Result: 体相与界面平衡条件均作为单一热力学泛函的驻定条件（stationarity conditions）出现；流体-流体系统的框架顺利扩展至固体-流体界面，经典平衡关系从不同类别的容许变分中自然涌现。

Conclusion: 该框架为体相热力学、界面热力学与构型热力学（configurational thermodynamics）提供了一个统一的理论底座，能够描述表面效应显著的系统。

Abstract: Starting from the constrained variational formulation of Larché and Cahn for solids in contact with fluids, a unified thermodynamic framework for such systems is developed. Both bulk and interfacial equilibrium conditions arise as stationarity conditions of a single thermodynamic functional. While earlier theory neglects a full treatment of interfacial contributions and therefore cannot describe systems in which surface effects are significant, the present formulation incorporates interfacial thermodynamics directly into the variational principle. The framework is introduced first for fluid-fluid systems to establish the underlying mathematical structure, and is then extended to solid-fluid interfaces. Within this setting, classical equilibrium relations for heterogeneous systems emerge naturally from different classes of admissible variations, providing a common theoretical basis for bulk, interfacial, and configurational thermodynamics.

</details>


### [90] [Far-from-equilibrium topological phase transition in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13658)
*Ze-Min Huang,Gustav John,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种远离平衡态（far-from-equilibrium）拓扑相变机制：在一维致密相位模型从随机初始条件确定性演化中，由相位致密性产生涡旋（vortex），而均匀不动点抑制涡旋成核，二者竞争驱动连续的非平衡相变，其普适类（universality class）被识别为有向渗流（directed percolation, DP），并通过有效场论构建和动力学标度分析数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的在寻找一种不依赖唯象假设（phenomenological postulates），而仅建立在拓扑与对称性基础上的远离平衡态拓扑相变机制，以深化对该类相变普遍原理的理解。

Method: 采用一维致密相位模型（compact phase model），从随机初始条件确定性演化；通过构建对应的有效场论（effective field theory）揭示相变机制，并进行动力学标度分析（dynamical-scaling analysis）以数值确认临界标度行为。

Result: 发现涡旋诱导的无序化与向均匀态弛豫之间的竞争驱动了连续的非平衡相变，其普适类被识别为有向渗流（DP），数值结果确认了DP临界标度。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种由拓扑和对称性（而非唯象假设）推导出的远离平衡态拓扑相变机制，为理解此类相变的普遍性提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We uncover a mechanism for far-from-equilibrium topological phase transitions, via a one-dimensional compact phase model evolving deterministically from random initial conditions. It rests on topology and symmetry rather than on phenomenological postulates: phase compactness permits vortices, and a homogeneous fixed point suppresses their nucleation, with the fixed point itself implied by phase-shift symmetry. The competition between vortex-induced disordering and relaxation toward homogeneity drives a continuous nonequilibrium transition, whose universality class we identify as directed percolation (DP). We demonstrate this by constructing the corresponding effective field theory and numerically confirming DP critical scaling through dynamical-scaling analysis.

</details>


### [91] [Far-from-equilibrium scaling of non-abelian Goldstone modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13666)
*Carl Philipp Zelle,Gustav John,Orla Supple,Romain Daviet,Sebastian Diehl*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过构造SO(2)×O(N)对称性的非平衡非线性sigma模型，将Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）物理扩展至非阿贝尔连续对称破缺的时间晶体，发现弱非平衡驱动在d≤2维度下导致强耦合非热固定点并破坏平衡临界行为，在d>2时恢复有效平衡，且旋转相中不同Goldstone扇区具有不同的普适动力学指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KPZ物理局限于单个SO(2)时间平移对称破缺（chronon），缺乏对非阿贝尔对称时间晶体的非平衡理论描述，而驱动量子材料、活性物质和光学诱导周期态中自然涌现这类更复杂的对称结构。

Method: 构建SO(2)×O(N)对称性的非平衡非线性sigma模型描述非阿贝尔时间晶体；对旋转相和振荡相推导Goldstone理论；利用单环重整化群分析考察非平衡微扰的稳定性；在1+1维直接模拟验证弱动态标度的解析结果。

Result: 在d=1,2维中任意弱非平衡扰动都使平衡固定点失稳并产生强耦合非热固定点；d>2时弱扰动无关并恢复有效平衡。旋转相中强耦合Goldstone扇区虽属同一序参量却获得不同普适动力学指数，该弱标度在1+1维模拟中得到验证。紧致性和拓扑缺陷最终破坏长程序但留下可观测的非热标度窗口。

Conclusion: 这些结果将KPZ普适性推广到非阿贝尔对称破缺，揭示了连续对称破缺与弱非平衡驱动相互作用可产生一类广泛的非热相，其维度依赖的标度行为和多扇区动力学指数为实验探测提供了明确预言。

Abstract: We identify a broad class of nonthermal phases generated by the interplay of continuous symmetry breaking and weak nonequilibrium driving. Extending Kardar-Parisi-Zhang (KPZ) physics beyond the single $SO(2)$ chronon associated with periodically broken time translations, we construct nonequilibrium nonlinear sigma models for $SO(2)\times O(N)$ symmetry, describing non-Abelian time crystals with coexisting temporal and internal order. This symmetry structure arises naturally in driven quantum materials, active matter, and optically induced periodic states. For rotating and oscillating phases, we derive the Goldstone theories and show that chronon-$O(N)$ couplings remain finite deep in the ordered regime. One-loop renormalization group analysis reveals a KPZ-like dimensional structure: in $d=1,2$, arbitrarily weak nonequilibrium perturbations destabilize the equilibrium fixed point and generate strongly coupled nonthermal fixed points, realizing emergent equilibrium breaking. By contrast, for $d > 2$, weak perturbations are irrelevant and effective equilibrium is restored. A central result is unconventional weak dynamic scaling in the rotating phase: strongly coupled Goldstone sectors acquire distinct universal dynamical exponents despite belonging to the same order parameter. We characterize this scaling analytically and corroborate it through direct simulations in $1+1$ dimensions. In the oscillating phase, we recover and extend weak-scaling regimes known from drifting polymers. Finally, compactness and topological defects ultimately destroy long-range order but leave experimentally accessible nonthermal scaling windows. Together, these results extend KPZ universality to non-Abelian symmetry breaking.

</details>


### [92] [Small-world structure of quantum computer hardware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13855)
*S. J. da Silva Junior,D. L. Shepelyansky,J. Lages*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文表明量子计算机（quantum computer）的量子寄存器（quantum register）状态网络，经由量子比特间的残余两体相互作用耦合后，展现出类似人类社会复杂网络的小世界（small-world）性质，并利用Åberg判据构建有效网络，发现其逾渗转变（percolation transition）与量子混沌（quantum chaos）和动态热化（dynamical thermalization）的起始高度吻合，且可将系统规模扩展至30个量子比特。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者旨在建立量子计算与复杂网络理论之间的联系，探索量子寄存器状态空间中是否存在类似人类社会网络的拓扑结构，并寻找一种比精确对角化（exact diagonalization）计算成本更低的方法来刻画量子混沌和热化的发生条件。

Method: 研究人员将量子寄存器状态视为网络节点，以量子比特间残余两体相互作用为连边构建网络；采用Åberg判据筛选仅保留大于局域能量间距的相互作用，构建有效（Åberg）网络，并分析其小世界特性和逾渗行为。

Result: 量子寄存器状态网络具有与人类社会网络相似的小世界性质，任两状态间最可能的Erdős数约为9，与Milgram社会网络报告的“六度分隔”相当；在临界耦合强度之上，有效网络逾渗形成跨越几乎整个量子寄存器空间的巨连通分量（giant component），该逾渗转变与先前通过精确对角化确立的量子混沌与动态热化起始点精确匹配，且可将系统规模扩展至n_q=30个量子比特。

Conclusion: 量子寄存器状态网络在结构上与社交网络同构，且其逾渗转变可作为量子混沌和热化发出的有效判据，为无需昂贵精确对角化即可探测大规模量子系统混沌行为提供了新途径。

Abstract: We show that the network of quantum register states of a quantum computer (QC), coupled through residual two-body interactions between qubits, exhibits small-world properties analogous to those of complex networks found in human society. The most probable Erdős number between any two states is about 9, comparable to the six degrees of separation reported by Milgram for social networks. Using the $\mathring{A}$berg criterion, which retains only interactions exceeding the local energy spacing, we construct an effective ($\mathring{A}$berg) network and show that, above a critical coupling strength, this network percolates into a giant component spanning nearly the whole quantum register space. This percolation transition closely matches the onset of quantum chaos and dynamical thermalization established previously via costly exact diagonalization, while our approach extends the accessible system size up to $n_q=30$ qubits.

</details>


### [93] [Generalizing the multidimensional thermodynamic uncertainty relation to combinations of arbitrary counting variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14276)
*Niklas Buschmann,Udo Seifert,Alexander M. Maier*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将多维热力学不确定性关系（MTUR）推广到具有任意初始状态、任意测量时长、且含时间依赖驱动的马尔可夫过程（Markov network）中。该推广的统一框架允许使用一组时间反对称、对称或不对称的计数观测（counting observables），包括净电流、交通量或流量，甚至可应用于底层跃迁无法区分的粗粒化观测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不确定性关系（uncertainty relations）为部分可访问的马尔可夫网络中隐藏的物理量（如平均熵产生率和总动力学活动量）提供下界。热力学不确定性关系（thermodynamic uncertainty relation, TUR）是最突出的代表，其多维推广（MTUR）通过利用一组净电流的协方差给出更紧的界，但现有结果未覆盖时间依赖驱动、任意初始状态和任意测量时长的普遍情形，且缺乏对多种类型计数观测的统一处理。

Method: 作者将MTUR推广到时间依赖驱动（time-dependently driven）的过程，允许任意初始状态与任意测量时长。该界可应用于一组计数观测，每个观测可为时间反对称（净电流）、时间对称（交通量）或时间不对称（流量）；同时允许粗粒化观测，其中每个计数观测可包含底层系统的多条难以区分的跃迁。

Result: 该推广以一种统一的方式包含了MTUR、基于单电流的时间依赖过程的TUR扩展，以及基于单一流量或单一交通量的估计器。作者通过简单示例对一般不确定性关系进行了说明。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了适用于更广泛场景（时间依赖、任意初态、任意时长、多类观测及粗粒化）的统一不确定性关系，为部分可访问马尔可夫网络中隐蔽量的估计提供了更一般的下界工具。

Abstract: Uncertainty relations provide lower bounds for otherwise hidden quantities of a partially accessible Markov network like the mean entropy production rate and the total dynamical activity. The thermodynamic uncertainty relation (TUR) is arguably the most prominent one and involves the precision of a fluctuating net current. One of its major generalizations is the multidimensional TUR (MTUR), which yields a tighter bound by using covariances of a set of observed net currents. We generalize this latter bound to time-dependently driven processes with arbitrary initial state and arbitrary measurement duration. Furthermore, this bound can be used with a set of fluctuating counting observables each of which can be time-antisymmetric, time-symmetric or time-asymmetric, i.e., a net current, a traffic or a flow. We can even allow for coarse-grained observations in which each counting observable could consist of multiple indiscernable observed transitions of the underlying system. Thus, we generalize the MTUR, extensions of the TUR for time-dependent processes based on one current, and an estimator based on one flux or on one traffic in a unifying way. We illustrate this general uncertainty relation with simple examples.

</details>


### [94] [Linear response across interaction regimes in two-dimensional ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14477)
*Aaron Müller,Pavel E. Dolgirev,Oleksii Malyshev,Eugene Demler*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于高斯和（sum of Gaussians）表示分布函数的方法，使量子玻尔兹曼方程（quantum Boltzmann equation, QBE）中的碰撞积分变得可处理，从而能够在二维铁磁体中精确计算动量分辨和频率分辨的线性响应，并分辨出从弹道（ballistic）到流体动力学（hydrodynamic）的温度驱动交叉行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维铁磁体（2D ferromagnets）中磁振子-磁振子相互作用强烈依赖动量，低动量磁振子弱散射而高动量磁振子强散射并呈现集体流体动力学行为，理解这些系统的输运需要在统一框架下描述弹道和流体动力学区域。然而，量子玻尔兹曼方程因多维碰撞积分而求解困难，亟需一种可计算的微观方法。

Method: 提出一种将分布函数表示为高斯函数之和的高效表示方法，使碰撞积分可处理；基于此方法求解线性化的量子玻尔兹曼方程（linearized QBE），计算二维铁磁体在宽温度和磁场范围内的动量分辨与频率分辨线性响应。

Result: 成功解析出温度驱动的交叉：从低动量弱相互作用磁振子主导的弹道区域，过渡到高动量强相互作用模式主导的集体流体动力学区域。将该方法应用于单层CrCl3时，与近期氮-空位中心（nitrogen-vacancy center）退相干实验报告的反常磁噪声（符合磁振子声学行为）取得了良好一致。

Conclusion: 该方法为量子玻尔兹曼动力学（quantum Boltzmann kinetics）可描述的相互作用二维量子系统，建立了计算动量分辨和频率分辨线性响应的通用框架。

Abstract: Recent discoveries of two-dimensional (2D) ferromagnets have stimulated intense interest in understanding and controlling their spin transport properties. A central microscopic feature of these systems is that exchange-driven magnon--magnon interactions are strongly momentum dependent: low-momentum magnons interact weakly, while high-momentum ones can scatter strongly and exhibit collective hydrodynamic behavior. Understanding transport in such systems therefore requires a microscopic description capable of capturing ballistic and hydrodynamic regimes on equal footing. The natural framework is the quantum Boltzmann equation (QBE), whose solution is notoriously difficult because of the multidimensional collision integrals. Here, we develop a method based on an efficient representation of distribution functions as sums of Gaussians, which renders the collision integrals tractable. This approach enables accurate solution of the linearized QBE and computation of momentum- and frequency-resolved linear response in 2D ferromagnets across a broad range of temperatures and magnetic fields. In particular, we resolve a temperature-driven crossover from a ballistic regime dominated by weakly interacting low-momentum magnons to a collective hydrodynamic regime governed by strongly interacting high-momentum modes. Applying this method to monolayer CrCl$_3$, we obtain good agreement with recent nitrogen-vacancy-center dephasing experiments that reported anomalous magnetic noise consistent with magnon sound. More broadly, our work establishes a general framework for computing momentum- and frequency-resolved linear response in interacting 2D quantum systems describable within quantum Boltzmann kinetics.

</details>

### [95] [Contrastive Learning for Interpretable Anomaly Detection at Collider Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13652)
*Haoyi Jia,Sagar Addepalli,Julia Gonski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ORCA（Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection）两阶段框架，通过监督对比学习构建嵌入空间，再在其中运行标准自编码器生成事件级异常分数。相较于基准自编码器架构，ORCA在模拟高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）数据上对新的物理信号展现出更宽的灵敏度和更深的敏感度，且对比嵌入使异常样本可解释，可用最大似然模板拟合来归属事件并量化不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对撞机物理中的通用事件级异常检测（anomaly detection）存在两个反复出现的问题：异常分数难以解释，且分数与能量标度和物体多重性（object multiplicity）高度相关。这些问题限制了异常检测在寻找新物理信号中的实用性与可解释性，亟需一种既能提升灵敏又能提供物理解释的框架。

Method: ORCA采用两阶段框架：首先利用覆盖多样化物理过程的监督对比学习（supervised contrastive learning）训练嵌入空间，随后在该嵌入空间中运行标准自编码器（autoencoder）以生成事件级异常分数。此外，通过最大似然模板拟合（maximum-likelihood template fit）将嵌入分布中的异常事件归属到已知模板物理过程，并给出量化不确定性。

Result: 在与高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）条件一致的模拟数据集上，ORCA相比基准自编码器架构在新物理信号的灵敏度和深度上均有显著提升。拟合能准确恢复注入的信号产量（signal yield），包括对训练嵌入时未包含的信号；对于模板库中缺失的信号，也能通过其最相似已知过程进行刻画，且包含量化不确定性。

Conclusion: ORCA为对撞机上可解释的基于异常检测的搜寻提供了一条可行路径。嵌入几何（embedding geometry）相比标准一维输出拟合携带更高维度的物理信息，可增强下游统计分析。这使其成为连接异常发现与物理解释之间鸿沟的关键方法。

Abstract: Generic event-level anomaly detection for collider physics has two recurring problems: anomaly scores are hard to interpret, and they correlate strongly with energy scale and object multiplicity. We present Organized Representation via Contrastive learning for Anomaly detection (ORCA), a two-stage framework that first learns an embedding space via supervised contrastive learning across a diverse set of physics processes, then runs a standard autoencoder in that space to generate event-level anomaly scores. On a simulated dataset consistent with conditions at the High-Luminosity Large Hadron Collider, ORCA delivers significant gains in both breadth and depth of sensitivity to new physics signals with respect to a baseline autoencoder architecture. Beyond improved sensitivity, the contrastive embedding makes the anomalous sample interpretable: because known processes occupy distinct regions of the space, a maximum-likelihood template fit to the embedding distributions can attribute events in an anomalous sample to template physics processes with quantified uncertainties. We demonstrate that the fit accurately recovers injected signal yields, including for signals excluded from the training of the embedding, and characterizes signals absent from the template library through the known processes they most resemble. These results establish ORCA as a route to interpretable anomaly detection-based searches at colliders, where the embedding geometry carries higher dimensional physics information compared to standard one-dimensional output fits, enhancing downstream statistical analysis.

</details>

### [96] [Elastic wakes mediate collective viscoelastic fluid-structure interactions in side-by-side cantilever arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13908)
*Arisa Yokokoji,Amy Q. Shen,Simon J. Haward*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本研究通过自下而上的方法系统探究低雷诺数粘弹性流中柔性悬臂阵列的流固耦合（FSI）行为，发现弱剪切稀化流体中弹性尾流在临界Weissenberg数下由分离转变为合并并伴随阵列级结构响应，而强剪切稀化流体则完全抑制该集体失稳，表明集体不稳定性由弹性、剪切稀化与几何构型共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低雷诺数下粘弹性流经过可变形结构的流固耦合（FSI）虽与纤毛、鞭毛等生物系统及工程微系统密切相关，但目前仍缺乏深入理解，有必要系统地揭示其机制。

Method: 采用自下而上的实验方法，系统变化并列柔性悬臂阵列中悬臂数量和测试流体流变性，比较弱剪切稀化（WS）与强剪切稀化（HS）的聚环氧乙烷溶液在不同Weissenberg数（Wi）下的FSI行为。

Result: 两种流体中单悬臂后均随Wi增大形成拉长的弹性尾流；WS流体在临界Weissenberg数（Wi*）处发生尾流从分离到合并的转变，伴随发散流场及悬臂内向展向偏转，且Wi*随阵列中悬臂数从二增至三而增大；HS流体在全Wi范围内尾流合并、流场发散和内向偏转均被抑制。

Conclusion: 弹性效应足以促进尾流形成但不足以诱发集体失稳，后者需同时具备剪切稀化行为与相邻悬臂间的相互作用；粘弹性FSI受流体弹性、剪切稀化及几何构型的联合作用调控，弹性尾流相互作用介导了局部尾流向阵列级结构响应的耦合。

Abstract: Fluid-structure interaction (FSI) in viscoelastic flows past deformable structures at low Reynolds numbers remains poorly understood, despite its relevance to biological systems such as cilia and flagella, and to engineered microsystems. We investigate viscoelastic FSI in side-by-side flexible cantilever arrays using a bottom-up approach that systematically varies the number of cantilevers and the rheology of the test fluid, comparing weakly shear-thinning (WS) and highly shear-thinning (HS) polyethylene oxide solutions. For both fluids, with increasing Weissenberg number (Wi), an elongated elastic wake develops behind a single isolated cantilever. For multiple cantilevers, the WS fluid undergoes a transition at a critical Weissenberg number (Wi*) from separated to merged elastic wakes, accompanied by the emergence of a divergent flow field and coordinated inward spanwise cantilever deflection. The critical Wi* increases as the number of cantilevers in the array is increased from two to three, demonstrating a strong dependence of the onset on the array configuration. For the HS fluid, wake merger, flow divergence, and inward cantilever deflection are suppressed across the full range of Wi investigated, despite comparable elasticity and the formation of elastic wakes. This contrast shows that elasticity alone promotes wake formation but is insufficient to produce the collective instability, which instead requires both shear-thinning and interactions between neighboring cantilevers. These findings demonstrate that viscoelastic FSI in flexible arrays is governed by the combined effects of fluid elasticity, shear-thinning, and geometric configuration, with elastic wake interactions mediating the collective instability and linking local elastic wakes to array-scale structural response.

</details>

### [97] [Complete Suppression of Thermomagnetic Instabilities in Nb Superconducting Films by Combined Metallic Layers and Ion Irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13699)
*D. Carmo,A. M. H. de Andrade,R. Giulian,A. A. M. Oliveira,W. A. Ortiz,F. Colauto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文通过Cu等正常金属覆盖层与Ar离子辐照两种互补手段来稳定超导Nb薄膜，两种方法分别抑制低场和高场区的热磁不稳定性（thermomagnetic instabilities），当二者结合时可完全消除磁通雪崩（flux avalanches），实现光滑磁化曲线与无雪崩磁通穿透。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导薄膜中的热磁不稳定性会触发磁通雪崩，破坏临界态并损害超导器件的性能，因此需要寻找有效的稳定化手段。

Method: 采用两种互补途径稳定Nb薄膜：添加正常金属覆盖层与Ar离子辐照处理；通过磁化测量（magnetization measurements）和磁光成像（magneto-optical imaging）对薄膜行为进行表征。

Result: 金属覆盖层能在低磁场下抑制雪崩的起始，离子辐照则通过将雪崩活动的上阈值向低场移动来缩小高场段的不稳定区域；将两种处理结合于同一试样可完全抑制热磁不稳定性。辐照剂量为5×10^16 ions/cm²的Nb薄膜镀上1 μm Cu层后，表现出光滑磁化曲线且磁通穿透无雪崩发生。

Conclusion: 联合处理能够恢复稳定的临界态（critical state）行为，为薄膜超导技术中稳定Nb超导薄膜提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Thermomagnetic instabilities in superconducting films can trigger flux avalanches that disrupt the critical state and impair the performance of superconducting devices. Here we investigate the stabilization of Nb thin films subjected to a perpendicular magnetic field by two complementary approaches: the addition of normal-metal overlayers and Ar ion irradiation. Magnetization measurements and magneto-optical imaging show that the metallic layers suppress the onset of avalanches at low applied fields, whereas ion irradiation reduces the high-field portion of the instability region by shifting the upper threshold for avalanche activity to lower fields. When combined in the same sample, these two partial stabilization effects lead to complete suppression of thermomagnetic instabilities. In particular, a Nb film coated with a 1~\(μ\)m-thick Cu layer after irradiation at a fluence of \(5\times10^{16}\)~ions/cm\(^2\) exhibits smooth magnetization curves and avalanche-free flux penetration. This combined treatment restores stable critical-state behavior and provides a practical route for stabilizing Nb superconducting films in thin-film superconducting technologies.

</details>


### [98] [Superconductivity in strongly overdoped cuprates: beyond the single-band model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14090)
*Ruichao Chen,Linda Sederholm,Yannick Klein,Ludovic Delbes,Benoît Baptiste,Paraskevas Parisiades,Emmanuel Maisonhaute,Davide Delmonte,Edmondo Gilioli,Maarit Karppinen,Ronald I. Smith,David A. Keen,Yann Le Godec,Andrea Gauzzi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文通过中子与同步辐射X射线粉末衍射研究了高氧过掺杂（overdoped）钇钡铜氧（YBa₂Cu₃O_y，y≈7.4）的晶体结构，发现超导转变温度（T_c）并不随氧含量增加而消失，并提出了超导区域延伸归因于额外空穴占据a₁对称性（由d_{3z²-r²}轨道构成）态而非常规b₁对称性Zhang-Rice单重态态的新解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多个过掺杂铜氧化物中观测到超导区域延伸，与常规“穹顶”（dome）图像相矛盾。该论文旨在通过高氧过掺杂样品的晶体结构研究解释这一异常现象。

Method: 采用中子衍射（neutron diffraction）和同步辐射X射线粉末衍射（synchrotron x-ray powder diffraction）方法研究高压下制备的YBa₂Cu₃O_y（y≈7.4）晶格结构，并使用键价和（bond valence sum）分析估算额外空穴在CuO₂平面中的分布比例。

Result: 研究表明，过量氧所产生的额外空穴约1/5转移到CuO₂平面，使空穴密度增至p=0.27 hole/Cu，按穹顶图像本该消灭超导；然而实测T_c随y保持恒定，证实此前Okai等人（2002）的观测。数据分析表明，顶角氧（apical oxygen）与平面铜（planar Cu）离子之间显著更短的键合距离是解释该差异的关键。

Conclusion: 额外空穴可能占据由d_{3z²-r²}轨道构成的a₁对称性态，而非通常由d_{x²-y²}轨道构成的b₁对称性Zhang-Rice单重态态。这一两带（two-band）图像若被单晶光谱测量证实，将要求对铜氧化物超导机理采用完全不同的理论框架进行解释。

Abstract: In order to explain the observation of an extended superconducting region in several overdoped cuprates, which contrasts the dome scenario, by means of neutron and synchrotron x-ray powder diffraction we study the crystal structure of YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{y}$, where strong oxygen overdoping up to $y = 7.4$ is achieved under high-pressure. A bond valence sum analysis indicates that 1/5 of the extra holes created by the excess oxygen are transferred to the CuO$_2$ planes, thus increasing the hole density up to $p=0.27$ hole/Cu, where superconductivity is expected to vanish according to the dome scenario. Instead, our data confirm a previous observation [Okai, Ono and Mitsuhashi, Physica C: Superconductivity {\bf 366}, 164 (2002)] that the superconducting critical temperature, $T_c$, remains constant with $y$. Our data analysis accounts for this discrepancy in terms of the much shorter bond between the apical oxygen and the planar Cu ion, which suggests that the extra holes occupy the $a_1$-symmetry states formed by $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ orbitals, instead of the usual $b_1$-symmetry Zhang-Rice singlet states formed by $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals. Suitable spectroscopic measurements on single crystals may support such a two-band scenario, which would require a totally different theoretical approach to explain superconductivity in cuprates.

</details>


### [99] [Simplified Silicon Nitride Nanomembrane Circuits for van der Waals Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14158)
*Tommaso Confalone,Vasilisa Gerega,Flavia Lo Sardo,Shreya Kumbhakar,Davide Massarotti,Francesco Tafuri,Shigeyuki Ishida,Hiroshi Eisaki,Kornelius Nielsch,Golam Haider,Nicola Poccia*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一种简化的自上而下（top-down）策略，制备SiNx纳米膜（nanomembrane, NMB）可转移电路，实现二维材料与范德华（van der Waals, vdW）异质结的电学集成而无需直接加工活性材料。通过将该方法用于集成四单元层厚的掺杂Bi2Sr2-xLaxCuO6+δ（Bi2201）薄片，观测到接近母体晶体本征值的超导转变温度（Tc~32K），验证了材料本征性质的保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维材料及其范德华异质结构为量子器件工程提供了出众的平台，但传统纳米加工（nanofabrication）工艺会损害原子级薄晶体的本征性质。因此，需要一种电学集成方案，在不直接处理活性材料的条件下实现电路与器件组装的有效解耦。

Method: 作者引入SiNx纳米膜电路，采用简化的自上而下策略，相较于此前自下而上（bottom-up）方法，显著降低了制造复杂度、加工步骤及对专业设备的依赖。以超导薄片Bi2201作为材料保持性的严格基准，将该电路与四单元层厚、最优掺杂的Bi2201薄片进行电学集成。

Result: 集成后的器件表现出超导转变温度Tcinf≈32K，接近母体晶体通过磁化率（susceptibility）测量的Tconset≈34K，表明本征超导性质得以完整保持。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了无需暴露于常规洁净室（cleanroom）工艺即可对脆弱层状材料实现电学集成，为集成日益复杂的范德华异质结构、莫尔材料（moiré materials）及混合量子架构提供了通用平台。

Abstract: Two-dimensional (2D) materials and van der Waals (vdW) heterostructures provide an exceptional platform for engineering quantum devices, yet realizing their potential requires electrical integration without compromising the pristine properties of atomically thin crystals through conventional nanofabrication. Transferable circuitry addresses this challenge by decoupling circuit fabrication from device assembly, enabling electrical contacting without directly processing the active material. Here, we introduce SiN$_x$ nanomembrane (NMB) circuits realized through a simplified top-down strategy that reduces fabrication complexity, processing steps and specialized tools required by our previous bottom-up approach. As a stringent benchmark of material preservation, we electrically integrate a four-unit-cell-thick, optimally doped Bi$_2$Sr$_{2-x}$La$_x$CuO$_{6+δ}$ (Bi2201) flake and observe a superconducting transition at T$_c^{inf}$~32K, close to the T$_c^{onset}$~34K measured by susceptibility in the parent crystals. The preservation of superconductivity demonstrates electrical integration of fragile layered materials without direct exposure to conventional cleanroom procedures, providing a versatile platform for integrating increasingly complicated vdW heterostructures, moiré materials, and hybrid quantum architectures.

</details>
