### [100] [Quasinormal modes as exterior probes of black hole interiors in our Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13643)
*Sašo Grozdanov,Vita Movrin,Samuel Valach*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究封闭在反射性类时腔内的黑洞在受到线性扰动时的类正态谱（quasinormal spectra），结合作者之前的arxiv:2606.11297工作，建立了热乘积公式（thermal product formula）并推导出高类正态过音（high quasinormal overtones）的普适渐近表达式。谱特征可直接编码黑洞内部及其奇点信息，且原则上可被事件视界外的观察者获取。论文进一步提出利用吸积等离子体作为部分透射镜的观测方案，对小型原初黑洞（primordial black holes），可通过射电波段的电磁波提取黑洞内部的具体信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞内部及奇点信息传统上被认为无法被外部观察者直接获取（事件视界censorship），本文旨在探索一种透过类正态谱（quasinormal spectrum）间接探测黑洞内部结构的新途径。通过在类时腔内引入反射性边界，期望获得更丰富的谱学信息来编码内部结构，并寻找实际可行的观测方案。

Method: 论文在数学层面考虑将黑洞置于反射性类时腔（reflecting timelike cavity）中并施加线性扰动，分析引力、电磁及其他扰动的类正态谱（quasinormal spectra），结合arXiv:2606.11297的结果建立热乘积公式（thermal product formula）并推导高类正态过音（high quasinormal overtones）的普适渐近表达式，验证其在渐近平坦、de Sitter和Anti-de Sitter时空中的适用性。在物理层面，提出吸积等离子体（accreting plasma）充当部分透射镜（partially transmissive mirror）的具体观测配置，以聚焦小型原初黑洞场景。

Result: 成功建立了适用于渐近平坦、de Sitter与Anti-de Sitter三种时空背景的热乘积公式和高过音普适渐近表达式（此前已有相关结果）。所得谱学特征（spectral signature）直接编码黑洞内部及奇点信息，且至少原则上可被事件视界外部的观察者获取。对于小型、假想的原初黑洞，通过射电波谱（radiowave spectrum）中的电磁波可提取黑洞内部的具体信息。

Conclusion: 类正态谱可以作为一种可观测的窗口，直接反映黑洞内部及奇点结构，突破了传统上信息无法穿越事件视界的观念壁垒。基于吸积等离子体的部分透射镜方案为实际观测提供了可行性路线，尤其针对小型原初黑洞，射电波段的观测有望实现这一目标。

Abstract: We consider a scenario in which a black hole is enclosed in a reflecting timelike cavity and linearly perturbed. For the resulting quasinormal spectra of gravitational, electromagnetic, and other perturbations, in conjunction with our arXiv:2606.11297, we establish a thermal product formula and derive a universal asymptotic expression for high quasinormal overtones. Both results hold in asymptotically flat, de Sitter, and Anti-de Sitter spacetimes (known previously). This spectral signature directly encodes information about the black hole interior and its singularity, and is, at least in principle, accessible to an observer outside the event horizon. We then propose a concrete observational setup in which accreting plasma plays the role of a partially transmissive mirror. For small, hypothetical, primordial black holes, we argue that one may thereby be able to extract concrete information about the black hole interior from electromagnetic waves in the radiowave spectrum.

</details>


### [101] [Localizing Momentum Waves on Mazes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13647)
*Tobi Ramella,Nicholas P. Warner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过计算纯M5膜上动量波的具体实例，验证了arXiv:2404.14477中关于M2-M5膜迷宫中动量波定位的提议，发现即使没有显式的M2膜，动量波仍会定位在AdS_7中与M5膜相关的AdS_3因子上，其定位形式分为显式奇点源和光滑局部凸起函数两种，支持独立动量波可在每个膜交点处定位的更广泛提议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在检验arXiv:2404.14477中提出的广义框架——即在相交M2和M5膜（intersecting M2 and M5 branes）迷宫中放置动量波（momentum waves）的提议，通过具体计算简单实例来验证该框架的正确性和适用范围。

Method: 作者计算了纯M5膜上动量波的一族简单实例，重新分析了近膜M5-动量（M5-P）系统的BPS方程，采用完整M2-M5-P系统所指定的超对称结构（supersymmetry structure），并验证arXiv:2404.14477中获得的通用线性系统能够完整描述M5-P系统上的所有零动量波（null momentum waves）。

Result: 在缺乏显式M2膜的情况下，动量波仍然定位在AdS_7中与M5膜相关联的AdS_3因子上，表明超对称规则仍然反映了M2-M5-P结构。动量定位呈现两种形式：显式奇异源（explicit singular sources）和光滑的局部凸起函数（smooth localized bump functions），后者反映了其他微态几何（microstate geometries）中出现的“动量迁移”（momentum migration）特征。

Conclusion: 动量定位的简单物理形式支持了arXiv:2404.14477中的更广泛提议，即独立动量波可以在每个膜交点处定位。作者进一步建议在膜交点处推广动量波的研究方向，这可能在全息原理（holography）中具有重要启示。

Abstract: We examine the proposal and analysis of arXiv:2404.14477 for putting momentum waves on mazes of intersecting M2 and M5 branes by computing a family of simple examples of momentum waves on pure M5 branes. We re-analyze the BPS equations for the near-brane M5-P system with the prescribed supersymmetry structure of the full M2-M5-P system and show that the general linear system obtained in arXiv:2404.14477 captures all of the null momentum waves on the simpler M5-P system. Despite the absence of explicit M2 branes in our examples, we find that the momentum waves still localize on an AdS$_3$ factor of the AdS$_7$ associated with M5 branes, showing that the supersymmetry prescription still reflects the M2-M5-P structure. This momentum localization takes two forms: explicit singular sources and smooth, localized bump functions reflecting ''momentum migration'' that is a feature of other microstate geometries. The simple, physical form of the momentum localization in our examples supports the broader proposal in arXiv:2404.14477 that independent momentum waves can localize at each brane intersection. We suggest further generalizations of momentum waves at brane intersections that could have important implications in holography.

</details>


### [102] [Properties of holographic superconductors from Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13731)
*Aliou Togo,Yang Yu,Kaniba Mady Keita,Jian-Pin Wu,Younouss Ham`eye Dicko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用受机器学习启发的现代数值优化技术研究全息超导体（holographic superconductor），通过构建余弦（cosine）和指数多项式（exponential polynomial）两种互补试探函数，最小化本征值λ²的变分泛函来获取临界温度，结果与文献中的解析预测高度一致，展示了变分方法在解析与数值优化结合下的高效性和扩展潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全息超导体的临界温度与相关物性通常在理论框架中依赖精确或半解析求解，传统方法在处理复杂边界条件或附加扇区（如backreaction）时存在灵活性不足的问题。作者旨在探索一种同时具备解析可控性与数值适应性的新途径，从而为全息超导体研究提供更高效且可推广的工具。

Method: 采用变分方法，通过对本征值λ²的变分泛函（variational functional）进行最小化来获得临界温度。提出两类试探函数：单参数的简单余弦ansatz F(z)=cos(az)和至多含19个系数的指数多项式F(z)=exp(Σaₙzⁿ)，后者利用多起点（multi-start）L-BFGS-B算法结合暖启动（warm-starting）策略进行优化，两种试探函数均自动满足标准边界条件。

Result: 余弦ansatz通过单参数最小化，在大范围Δ内得到λ²(Δ)和T_c/√ρ，且Δ=1和Δ=2处与精确值高度吻合；指数多项式ansatz在19个系数优化后与已知精确结果吻合更好。所有数值结果与文献中解析预测一致，验证了变分方法的稳健性。

Conclusion: 解析试探函数与现代数值优化的结合为全息超导体研究提供了一种功能强大、灵活且高效的工具，并能方便地扩展到包含backreaction或其他扇区的情形。

Abstract: We investigate holographic superconductors using modern optimisation techniques inspired by machine learning. The critical temperature is obtained by minimising the variational functional for the eigenvalue $λ^2$ with two complementary trial functions: a simple cosine ansatz $F(z)=\cos(a z)$ and a flexible exponential polynomial $F(z)=\exp(\sum_{n=2}^{N} a_n z^n)$, both of which automatically satisfy the standard boundary conditions. For the cosine ansatz, we perform a one-parameter minimisation and obtain $λ^2(Δ)$ and $T_c/\sqrtρ$ over a wide range of $Δ$, including the exact values at $Δ=1$ and $Δ=2$ to high accuracy. The exponential polynomial ansatz, with up to 19 coefficients, is optimised using a multi-start L-BFGS-B algorithm with warm-starting, yielding even better agreement with known exact results. Our numerical data for $λ^2(Δ)$ and $T_c/\sqrtρ$ match the analytical predictions from the literature, confirming the robustness of the variational approach. This work; therefore, demonstrates that a combination of analytic trial functions and modern numerical optimisation provides a powerful, flexible, and efficient tool for exploring holographic superconductors, and can be readily extended to include backreaction or other sectors in this field.

</details>


### [103] [Dependence of Critical Exponents Accuracy on the Number of Fields in the O(N) -Invariant φ^4 Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13776)
*Abouzeid M. Shalaby*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文利用Shalaby等人提出的整超几何重求和算法（entire-hypergeometric resummation algorithm），对$O(N)$对称模型在临界点处的七圈发散$\varepsilon$-级数进行重求和，获得了$N\ge4$情况下临界指数$\nu$、$\eta$和$\omega$的高精度估计，证实了在大$N$极限下重求和方法的精度随$N$增大而提高的预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来大$N$极限下$O(N)$对称临界模型的研究备受关注，此前的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）研究已提供了$N\ge4$情况下最精确的临界指数估计。在重整化群（renormalization group, RG）框架下，由于$\varepsilon$-展开的有效展开参数$\sigma=\frac{3}{N+8}$随$N$增大而减小，理论上重求和技巧在大$N$区域应具有更好的精确度，作者希望通过实际数值检验这一预期。

Method: 作者采用Shalaby等人开发的整超几何重求和算法，对最新获得的$N\ge4$的七圈发散$\varepsilon$-级数进行重求和处理，从而提取临界指数$\nu$、$\eta$和$\omega$的高精度估计值。

Result: 分析确认了重求和精度随$N$增大而显著改善的预期。在相同的七圈阶数下，$O(2)$情形的误差比实验、蒙特卡洛模拟和共形场论（conformal field theory）分析的结果大约一个数量级；而对于足够大的$N$，本工作的误差与蒙特卡洛和非微扰重整化群方法的结果处于同一数量级。

Conclusion: 研究表明在大$N$区域内，整超几何重求和算法具有强大的预测能力，其精度可与蒙特卡洛和非微扰重整化群方法相媲美，验证了$\varepsilon$-展开重求和技巧在大$N$极限下的有效性。

Abstract: The study of large-$N$ cases of the $O(N)$-symmetric model at criticality has attracted significant attention in recent years. In particular, a recent high-precision Monte Carlo study ( Physical Review B 105, 054428 (2022)) reported the most accurate estimates to date for the critical exponents $ν$, $η$, and $ω$ for $N \ge4$. Within the framework of the renormalization group (RG)---the cornerstone of the modern theory of critical phenomena---powerful computational approaches such as the $\varepsilon$-expansion and non-perturbative RG are available. Since the effective expansion parameter in the $\varepsilon$-expansion, $σ= \frac{3}{N+8}$, decreases with increasing N, one expects resummation techniques to become progressively more accurate in the large-N regime.
  To test this expectation, we apply the entire-hypergeometric resummation algorithm developed by Shalaby et al. to the recently obtained seven-loop divergent $\varepsilon$-series for $N \ge 4$, yielding high-precision estimates for $ν$, $η$, and $ω$ . Our analysis confirms the anticipated improvement in accuracy with increasing N. To assess the significance of these results, we note that at the same seven-loop order the $O(2)$ case exhibits errors that are an order of magnitude larger than those obtained from experiment, Monte Carlo simulations, and conformal-field-theory analysis. In contrast, for sufficiently large N, the errors in the present work are of the same order of magnitude as those from Monte Carlo and non-perturbative RG methods, demonstrating the strong predictive power of our resummation approach in the large-N regime.

</details>


### [104] [Unfolding the Exotic Supergravity Multiplet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13801)
*Carlo Iazeolla,Per Sundell,Brenno Carlini Vallilo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个具有显式超共形对称性（含共形对偶变换）的线性化六维奇异超引力（exotic supergravity）的无展开（unfolded）表述，其构造基于超振荡子（superoscillator）实现的超群 MOSp(16|16)及其中包含的 Howe 对偶对 MOSp(8*|8)×SU(2)。奇异引力子势以 SU(2) 三重态与 OSp(8*|8) 分类超张量的直积中出现，并通过对偶对的形成提供顶点的来源，同时构造了两个自然的二次可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 六维奇异超引力（exotic supergravity）作为一种具有共形对偶变换特性的理论，其线性化表述此前缺乏显式超共形对称性的系统构建。本文旨在填补这一空白，提供一种基于无展开（unfolded）形式的统一表述。

Method: 采用超振荡子（superoscillator）实现元合群（metaplectic supergroup）MOSp(16|16)，其中包含 Howe 对偶对（Howe dual pair）MOSp(8*|8)×SU(2)。奇异引力子势由 SU(2) 三重态与 OSp(8*|8) 的超无跡分级对称二阶超张量的直积中构成共形对偶的二元势对，其来源通过相对 Chevalley-Eilenberg 上循环（relative Chevalley-Eilenberg cocycles）实现。

Result: 零形式（zero-form）取值为 SO(1,1)×Spin(1,5)×USp(8) 协变单形限制的扩展超单态（extended supersingleton），其为无穷维 SU(2)-不变的 Hermitian 左模。构造了两个自然二次可观测量：(i) 由手征 Weyl 零形式及其对偶组成的在壳闭合零形式；(ii) 由共形对偶二元势构成的离壳拓扑六形式，其可约化为超庞加莱背景下手征两点函数的生成函数。

Conclusion: 该构造提供了奇异超引力多重态的显式实现，其在结构上类比于 psu(2,2|4) 的伴随表示，并为奇异引力子势提供了无展开的几何一致性框架。构建的二次可观测量分别联系到 4D Vasiliev 系统内部 twistor Z 空间中第二陈类的在壳值，以及超庞加莱背景下手征两点函数的离壳生成函数。

Abstract: We provide an unfolded formulation of linearised 6D exotic supergravity with manifest superconformal symmetry including conformal duality transformations. The construction is based on a superoscillator realisation of the metaplectic supergroup $MOSp(16|16)$ containing the Howe dual pair $MOSp(8^\ast|8)\times SU(2)$. Exotic graviton potentials arise in conformally dual pairs of two-forms in direct products of $SU(2)$-triplets and supertraceless graded-symmetric rank-two supertensors of $OSp(8^\ast|8)$ generated from a hypercharge by the supercharges, providing an analogue of the adjoint representation of $\mathfrak{psu}(2,2|4)$. The potentials are sourced via relative Chevalley-Eilenberg cocycles by a conformally dual pair of chiral and anti-chiral Weyl zero-forms valued in $SO(1,1)\times Spin(1,5)\times USp(8)$-covariant monomorphic restrictions of an extended supersingleton, i.e., an infinite-dimensional $SU(2)$-invariant Hermitian left-module of $MOSp(8^\ast|8)$ containing various irreps associated with different boundary conditions, including the unitarizable exotic supergravity multiplet. Two natural quadratic observables are i) an on-shell closed zero-form built from the chiral Weyl zero-form and its dual, akin to the on-shell value of the second Chern class on the internal twistor $Z$-space of 4D Vasiliev systems; and ii) an off-shell topological six-form built from the conformally dual pair of two-form potentials, reducing on-shell to a generating function for chiral two-point functions in super-Poincaré backgrounds.

</details>


### [105] [Investigation of the black-hole quantum atmosphere from the effective proper temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13943)
*Sojeong Cheong,Wontae Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过有效固有温度研究D维史瓦西黑洞量子大气层，证明大气层半径随时空维度增加而单调递减，并在高维极限下仍与视界保持有限径向距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 霍金辐射的空间起源可在视界外有限区域内分布，但此前对量子大气层（quantum atmosphere）的定量研究有限，需要更精确刻画其空间范围及其与维度和量子态的关系。

Method: 基于D维史瓦西黑洞的二维径向扇区构建可处理模型，分别考虑Hartle–Hawking态与Unruh态；在Hartle–Hawking态中利用共形反常（conformal anomaly）从热力学第一定律导出固有温度，在Unruh态中将其分解为左右手征温度T_L与T_R，并定义大气层半径为有效固有温度取极大值的径向位置。

Result: 数值计算表明大气层半径随时空维数增加单调递减；在大维度极限下，大气层半径与视界之间仍保持有限径向因子，说明量子大气层在所有维度中以扩展外区域形式持续存在。

Conclusion: 量子大气层并非高维效应可消除的薄壳，而是一个在所有维数下均具有限延伸的外围区域，为理解霍金辐射的空间来源提供了统一描述。

Abstract: Hawking radiation may be regarded as originating at the event horizon; however, its spatial origin can instead be distributed over a finite region outside the horizon. In this paper, using an effective proper temperature, we investigate the quantum atmosphere within a tractable model based on the two-dimensional radial sector of $D$-dimensional Schwarzschild black holes. In the Hartle--Hawking state, the proper temperature is derived from the first law of thermodynamics in the presence of the conformal anomaly associated with Hawking radiation. In the Unruh state, we decompose the proper temperature into two chiral temperatures, $T_{\rm L}$ and $T_{\rm R}$, associated with the ingoing and outgoing fluxes, respectively. Demonstrating $T_{\rm R}$ as the effective proper temperature characterizing the outgoing Hawking flux with the proper temperature in the Hartle-Hawking state, we define the atmospheric radius effectively as the radial position at which the effective proper temperature attains its maximum. We then numerically compute the atmospheric radius of the quantum atmosphere for various spacetime dimensions and show that it decreases monotonically as the number of spacetime dimensions increases. In the large-dimensional limit, we find that the atmospheric radius remains separated from the horizon by a finite radial factor, indicating that the quantum atmosphere can persist as an extended exterior region in any dimension.

</details>


### [106] [Dirac, Majorana, and Weyl Spinors in Arbitrary Dimension and Signature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14193)
*Jan Hajer*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文给出了任意时空符号下费米子场类型的约化克利福德代数（Clifford algebra）分类。通过分别处理完整实克利福德代数及其偶数子代数，区分了pinor与spinor、Pin-等变Majorana结构与仅Spin-等变结构；统一的符号运算将伴随、复共轭、转置交错算子与镜像提升结合，最终按Bott类和模8维数组织为三十二个周期性扇区，分别识别可用的Dirac、Weyl、Majorana、辛Majorana、Majorana-Weyl和辛Majorana-Weyl场，并区分不变配对的存在性与交错算子相容性、Grassmann与手性选择规则、协变指标收缩及局域双线性算子的厄米性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意时空符号下，费米子场类型通常仅从复Spin表示推断，这会掩盖与实结构、不连通时空反射以及作用量级双线性约束相关的区别。作者旨在给出一种惯例明确（convention-explicit）的分类方法，以澄清这些被掩盖的物理与数学内涵。

Method: 方法上，论文将完整实克利福德代数与其偶数子代数分开处理，从而区分pinor与spinor，以及Pin-等变与仅Spin-等变的Majorana型结构。作者将伴随、复共轭和转置交错算子（intertwiner）与基本及集合反射提升（reflection lift）结合，构建统一的符号运算（sign calculus），并依据Bott类与总维度模8将局部克利福德分类组织为三十二个周期性扇区。

Result: 针对每个扇区，论文识别了可用的Dirac、Weyl、Majorana、辛Majorana（symplectic Majorana）、Majorana-Weyl和辛Majorana-Weyl场。同时，论文区分了不变配对的存在性与交错算子相容性、Grassmann与手性选择规则、协变指标收缩以及局域双线性算子的厄米性等不同层面的约束。

Conclusion: 该分类是局域的，不包含依赖具体理论的规范、反常或全局约束，但为后续在任意符号下研究费米子场的物理性质提供了系统性的数学框架。

Abstract: Fermionic field types in arbitrary signature are often inferred from complex Spin representations alone, which obscures distinctions associated with real structures, disconnected spacetime reflections, and action-level bilinear constraints. We give a convention-explicit classification that treats the full real Clifford algebra and its even subalgebra separately, thereby distinguishing pinors from spinors and Pin-equivariant Majorana-type structures from structures that are only Spin-equivariant. Adjoint, complex-conjugation, and transposition intertwiners are combined with elementary and collective reflection lifts into a unified sign calculus. The resulting local Clifford classification is organised into thirty-two periodicity sectors determined by the Bott class and the total dimension modulo eight, without including theory-dependent gauge, anomaly, or global constraints. For each sector, we identify the available Dirac, Weyl, Majorana, symplectic Majorana, Majorana-Weyl, and symplectic Majorana-Weyl fields. The existence of an invariant pairing is distinguished from intertwiner compatibility, Grassmann and chiral selection rules, covariant index contraction, and Hermiticity of local bilinear operators.

</details>


### [107] [Classical gravitational scattering with a massive scalar mediator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14267)
*Birgitta Biendarra,Kays Haddad,Jan Plefka*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了两个引力致密天体在存在大质量标量中介子（massive scalar mediator）时的经典散射问题，将其作为奇异现象（exotic phenomena）的简化模型，并将线性冲量和散射角计算到第二后闵可夫斯基（post-Minkowskian，PM）阶。研究发现散射角在标量力作用距离与碰撞参数（impact parameter）相当时出现共振，而在大质量极限下存在屏蔽效应（screening effect）——标量云携带负能量导致引力质量减小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望通过包含大质量标量中介子的散射模型来探索超越标准引力理论的奇异物理现象，并理解这类修改引力理论中经典散射过程的解析结构。

Method: 采用量纲分析（dimensional analysis）论证该过程仅在存在引力时才能保持经典性，由此得出对标量—世界线耦合的微扰展开与后闵可夫斯基展开不可分离的结论。作者将渐近可观测量中的傅里叶变换约化为单变量参数积分，以便数值求解，并得到第二后闵可夫斯基阶的解析结果。

Result: 获得了线性冲量和散射角到第二后闵可夫斯基阶的解析结果。散射角在标量中介力的范围与碰撞参数相当时呈现共振特征；在大质量极限下发现屏蔽效应，即每个致密物体激发的标量云携带负能量，使其引力质量线性地减小。

Conclusion: 大质量标量中介子可产生共振和屏蔽两种显著的下一个前导阶（next-to-leading-order）效应，它们可作为探测此类奇异媒介存在的独特可观测信号。

Abstract: We consider the classical scattering of two gravitating compact objects in the presence of a massive scalar mediator, providing a simple model of exotic phenomena. Through dimensional analysis, we argue that such a process can only be classical in the presence of gravity, a consequence of which is that perturbing in the coupling of the scalar to a worldline is not separate from the post-Minkowskian expansion. When computing asymptotic observables, the massive mediator complicates the Fourier transforms to impact-parameter space at next-to-leading order. We reduce these to univariate parametric integrals - amenable to numerical integration - and produce analytic results for the linear impulse and the scattering angle to the second post-Minkowskian order. The scattering angle exhibits a resonance when the range of the scalar-mediated force is comparable to the impact parameter, offering a distinctive signature of a massive mediator. In the opposite, large-mass regime we uncover a screening effect: the scalar cloud sourced by each compact object carries negative energy, reducing its gravitational mass by an amount linear in the scalar's mass. Both of these phenomena are next-to-leading-order effects.

</details>


### [108] [Information Spreading in Diffusion Models from Effective Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14308)
*Navonil Neogi,Nabil Iqbal*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文研究具有卷积架构的得分匹配扩散模型（score-matching diffusion models），提出将物理学中的有效场论（effective field theory）框架应用于描述其去噪动力学，并在简单的可解析玩具模型和MNIST数据集上验证了该框架的预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积架构的扩散模型具有局域性归纳偏置（inductive bias of locality），作者认为这种特性使得物理学中的有效场论思想可以被用于描述扩散模型的去噪动力学（denoising dynamics），从而为该类模型的动态行为提供理论框架。

Method: 作者首先将有效场论形式应用于一个允许解析描述的简单玩具模型（toy example），随后将该框架应用到MNIST数据集上进行验证，通过比较两个点之间的互信息（mutual information）增长行为与有效场论预测的一致性来检验该方法的有效性。

Result: 在玩具模型和MNIST两个案例中，两点之间的互信息增长均符合简单布朗运动（Brownian motion）有效场论的预测，验证了所提出框架的适用性。

Conclusion: 研究表明物理学中的有效场论框架能够有效描述卷积架构扩散模型的去噪动力学，为理解和预测此类模型的动态行为提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: We study score-matching diffusion models with a convolutional architecture. We argue that the inductive bias of locality means that the machinery of effective field theory from physics can be usefully applied to describe the denoising dynamics. We apply this formalism first to a simple toy example which permits an analytical description, and thereafter to MNIST, and show that in both cases, the mutual information between two points grows in a manner predicted by a simple effective field theory of Brownian motion.

</details>


### [109] [Large lumps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14384)
*D. Bazeia,I. Bezerra,M. A. Marques,R. Menezes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过kink-反kink对（kink-antikink pair）的叠加来构造lump孤子解（lump solution）的方法，其中kink与原点之间的距离由单一参数$a$控制。当$a$取大值时，解中出现宽平台结构，称为大lump（large lump）。该方法利用一阶方程构造相应的势能，并在多种已知标量场模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有lump解的构造方法缺乏系统性，作者旨在提出一种通用的程序，通过父kink（parent kink）的叠加来生成新型lump解，并进一步检验任意给定lump解是否可由该方法获得。

Method: 该方法的核心是将一对kink与反kink进行叠加，其间距由参数$a$控制；利用一阶方程（first-order equation）构造与lump解对应的势能函数（potential）。随后在多个已知标量场模型中验证该构造程序，并通过分析lump解的尾部行为（tail）来判定其属于短程指数衰减还是长程幂律衰减。

Result: lump解继承了父kink的尾部行为，可呈现短程指数衰减（short-range exponential）或由不同幂律衰减（power-law decay）表征的长程廓线。此外，作者展示了如何验证任意lump解是否可由该程序获得，并以此为工具构造了一个新型的无真空lump（vacuumless lump）。

Conclusion: 所提出的kink叠加程序为lump解的构造提供了系统性框架，并可推广至具有不同尾部行为的多种标量场模型；同时，该程序具备判据功能，可用于检验给定lump解的可构造性。

Abstract: We introduce a procedure to obtain lump solutions via the formation of a kink-antikink pair, consisting of the superposition of kinks whose distance from the origin is controlled by a single parameter $a$. For large values of $a$, a wide plateau appears in the solution, which we call a large lump. The procedure involves the use of a first-order equation that allows the construction of the potential associated with the lump solution. We then investigate several known scalar field models where the parent kinks are capable of giving rise to novel lumps. The lump inherits the tails of the parent kink, allowing for either short-range exponential profiles or long-range profiles characterized by distinct power-law decays. We also show how to verify if an arbitrary lump solution can be obtained via our method and illustrate this possibility with a novel vacuumless lump.

</details>


### [110] [The Phase Space of Gravity on Null Hypersurfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14449)
*Luca Ciambelli,Laurent Freidel,Robert G. Leigh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了无焦散零线段（caustic-free null segment）上广义相对论（general relativity）的体动力学泊松结构（bulk kinematical Poisson structure），在施加Raychaudhuri和Damour约束之前实现。通过展示Ehresmann联络的移位（即Carroll boost）在引力中为纯规范量，将联络固定为背景结构，得到零引力数据的主相空间（prime phase space），包含自旋0、自旋1和自旋2三个扇区，并通过三阶段Dirac约化构造泊松括号，最终还通过哈密顿向量场的显式实现独立验证了括号结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为零约束代数、可观测量和量子化的研究提供经典正则框架（classical canonical arena）。在零类超曲面（null hypersurface）上建立动力学泊松结构此前尚未系统完成，特别是未施加Raychaudhuri和Damour约束之前的运动学层级（kinematical level）。

Method: 首先证明Ehresmann联络的移位（即Carroll boosts）为纯规范量，从而将联络固定为背景结构，由此构建零引力数据的主相空间；该相空间包含面积及其共轭（自旋0对）、零方向及其共轭（自旋1对）、以及由单模横向度量（unimodular transverse metric）编码的自旋2扇区。随后通过三步Dirac约化（归一化、水平性与剪切约束）构造括号，并利用辛形式的哈密顿向量场的显式实现独立验证。

Result: 发现自旋2括号是非局域的，包含沿零生成元的一阶输运算子的反对称Green核；自旋0和自旋1动量的自括号分别涉及横向度量的垂直与水平导数与自旋2传播子的缩并。

Conclusion: 这些结果为研究零约束代数、可观测量和量子化提供了经典的规范正则框架，为后续量子和约束结构的进一步分析奠定基础。

Abstract: We construct the bulk kinematical Poisson structure of general relativity on a caustic-free null segment, prior to imposing the Raychaudhuri and Damour constraints. We first show that shifts of the Ehresmann connection, also known as Carroll boosts, are pure gauge in gravity. This allows the Ehresmann connection to be fixed as a background structure and leads to the prime phase space of null gravitational data. The resulting phase space comprises of a spin-0 pair that includes the area and its conjugate, a spin-1 pair including the null direction and its conjugate, and the spin-2 sector encoded in the unimodular transverse metric. We then construct the brackets through a three-stage Dirac reduction implementing a normalization, horizontality, and shear constraints. We find that the spin-2 bracket is non-local and involves an antisymmetric Green kernel for a first-order transport operator along the null generators. The self-brackets of the spin-0 and spin-1 momenta involve, respectively, the vertical and horizontal derivatives of the transverse metric contracted with the spin-2 propagator. We finally provide an independent derivation of the brackets from the explicit realization of the Hamiltonian vector fields of the symplectic form. These results provide the classical canonical arena for studying the null constraint algebra, observables, and quantization.

</details>


### [111] [Four-point functions, Twistors and Supersymmetry in the Symplectic Bi-Grassmannian for CFT$_4$ and AdS$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14451)
*Dhruva K. S*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四维共形场论（4D conformal field theories）的辛双格拉斯曼（symplectic bi-Grassmannian）框架中开启四点函数研究，推导了与散射振幅类似的因式分解公式，并通过自举（bootstrap）方法获得了包含标量、光子、费米子、胶子和引力子的 AdS5 交换关联函数。随后发展了 GL(2,ℝ) 扭量空间（twistor space）形式体系，包括彭罗斯变换（Penrose transform）及 N=1 超对称扩展，并在 AdS5 N=1 超杨-米尔斯理论中验证了该形式体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四点函数在共形场论中具有重要地位，但动量空间中相应的关联函数极为复杂。研究者旨在寻找一种更简洁的数学框架（辛双格拉斯曼与扭量空间）来描述和计算这些关联函数，同时扩展至超对称情形。

Method: 本文采用辛双格拉斯曼框架推导四点函数的因式分解公式，类比散射振幅方法；通过自举方法构造 AdS5 交换关联函数。进一步在扭量空间中推导彭罗斯变换和扭量格拉斯曼（twistorial Grassmannian），并扩展至 N=1 超对称，得到超对称彭罗斯变换和超扭量格拉斯曼（super-twistor Grassmannian），再通过半傅里叶变换得到旋量螺旋度（spinor-helicity）变量下的辛双格拉斯曼形式。

Result: 获得的关联函数是格拉斯曼矩阵子式（minors）的有理函数，比动量空间表达远为简洁。在 AdS5 N=1 超杨-米尔斯理论中，通过因式分解约束和输入自举得到的四费米子关联函数，成功导出了涉及胶子、标量和费米子的关联函数。还发现该形式体系可推广至超引力理论，并给出了 GL(2,ℝ) 扭量空间中的极简表达式。

Conclusion: 辛双格拉斯曼框架结合扭量空间方法为四点关联函数提供了远比动量空间简洁的描述，并自然兼容 N=1 超对称。该形式体系在 AdS5 N=1 超杨-米尔斯理论中得到了验证，并有望应用于超引力理论中的相关计算。

Abstract: We initiate the study of four point functions in the symplectic bi-Grassmannian framework for four dimensional conformal field theories. We derive factorization formulae analogous to those for scattering amplitudes and bootstrap several examples of AdS$_5$ exchange correlation functions involving scalars, photons, fermions, gluons and gravitons. These correlators are rational functions of the minors of the Grassmannian matrices in contrast to their momentum space counterparts which are much more complicated.
  We then develop the $GL(2,\mathbb{R})$ twistor space formalism, deriving the Penrose transform and the twistorial Grassmannian where we find remarkably simple expressions. We also find an elegant and natural extension to $\mathcal{N}=1$ supersymmetry, deriving the supersymmetric Penrose transform and the super-twistor Grassmannian. Performing a half-Fourier transform from twistor space, we derive the supersymmetric symplectic bi-Grassmannian corresponding to spinor-helicity variables.
  We test our formalism in the context of AdS$_5$ $\mathcal{N}=1$ super-Yang Mills theory where we impose consistent factorization and input the bootstrapped four-fermion correlator to obtain those involving gluons, scalars and fermions. Finally, we discuss the application to supergravity theories.

</details>


### [112] [Static and Dynamic perturbations of scalar DBI analogue of a black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14463)
*Pedro Meert,Horatiu Nastase*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了标量狄拉克-玻恩-因费尔德（DBI）理论中球对称解的微扰，发现其服从弯曲背景中无质量标量场的运动方程，该背景具有黑体温度与零信息传播速度的事件视界（即黑洞模拟体）。通过静态与动态两种场景分析，分别获得了标量极化率（Love 数）与类似准正规模的高通滤波行为，进一步支持标量黑洞模拟体的类比分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在验证标量黑洞模拟体（black hole analogue）能否像真实黑洞一样被分析，为此需要检验其背景几何的类黑洞性质，并考察微扰在不同动力学场景下的行为。

Method: 首先回顾了微扰传播的背景几何性质；然后在静态场景中求解得到 Love 数（Love numbers）或更广义的标量极化率（scalar polarizability）；在动态场景中，采用与准正规模（quasi-normal modes）计算类似的边界条件进行分析，论证背景几何充当经典高通滤波器（high-pass filter）。

Result: 静态分析中获得了标量 Love 数；动态分析中表明背景几何表现为经典高通滤波器，即仅高频扰动能够通过事件视界传播。

Conclusion: 通过静态与动态两类分析，作者提供了进一步证据表明标量黑洞模拟体可以像真实黑洞一样进行分析。

Abstract: Perturbations around a spherically symmetric solution of the scalar Dirac-Born-Infeld (DBI) theory obey the equation of motion of a massless scalar field in a curved background with black hole-like properties like finite temperature and an event horizon with zero speed of propagation of information (a black hole analogue). After reviewing some properties of the background geometry for the propagations, we investigate the solutions considering static and dynamic regimes. In the former case we obtain the so-called Love numbers, or more generally scalar polarizability. In the latter case, we employ boundary conditions analogous to the computation of quasi-normal modes, and argue that the background geometry acts as a classical high-pass filter. We therefore provide further evidence that the scalar black hole analogues can be analyzed similarly to a black hole.

</details>


### [113] [Towards a Non-Perturbative Classical Double Copy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14512)
*Kymani Armstrong-Williams*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在四次双伴随标量理论（quartic biadjoint scalar theory）、复化SU(2)杨-米尔斯理论和四维共形平坦度规的广义相对论之间建立了一个精确的解生成对应关系，并由此构建了一个非微扰经典双拷贝（classical double copy）映射，覆盖受限的能层（ansatz sector）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典双拷贝关系此前主要局限于微扰框架或特定对称性简化，作者希望探索在非微扰层面上，标量理论、杨-米尔斯理论与引力之间是否存在精确的运动学对应，并从中获得新的物理洞见。

Method: 通过一个共同的标量种子（scalar seed）统一生成因子化的双伴随场、Corrigan-Fairlie-'t Hooft-Wilczek规范势和共形度量，并在耦合常数识别（identification of couplings）下证明三者运动方程约化为同一标量方程。进一步通过提取爱因斯坦方程的迹自由部分，获得引力与杨-米尔斯之间的源关系，从而建立非微扰经典双拷贝字典。

Result: 构造了有理数和常数剖面分别对应AdS₄、平面dS₄和闵可夫斯基空间，以及雅可比椭圆函数（Jacobi-elliptic）剖面生成正能量的负宇宙学常数再碰撞分支和负能量的正宇宙学常数弹跳分支，并给出了一个新的Kerr-Schild双拷贝实例。

Conclusion: 该对应关系在受限能层内保留了三个理论腿上的非线性动力学，提供了经典双拷贝在非微扰水平上的具体实现，并展示了其作为解生成工具和新型克尔-希尔德双拷贝实例的适用性。

Abstract: We construct an exact solution-generating correspondence between restricted ansatz sectors of quartic biadjoint scalar theory, complexified $SU(2)$ Yang--Mills theory and four-dimensional general relativity with conformally flat metrics and traceless energy-momentum. A factorised biadjoint field, a Corrigan--Fairlie--'t Hooft--Wilczek gauge potential and a conformal metric are generated by a common scalar seed. Their equations of motion reduce to the same equation of motion under the identification of couplings, which retains the nonlinear dynamics on all three legs of the correspondence. The scalar equation captures only the trace of the Einstein equations; deriving the trace-free part of the Einstein equation additionally yields a source relation between Gravity and Yang-Mills. From this, we construct a non-perturbative classical double copy for the restricted ansatz sectors between quartic biadjoint scalar theory, $SU(2)$ Yang--Mills theory and gravity. We illustrate the dictionary of mappable solutions with rational and constant profiles representing $AdS_4$, planar $dS_4$ and Minkowski space, and with Jacobi-elliptic profiles generating a positive-energy recollapsing branch for negative cosmological constant and a negative-energy bouncing branch for positive cosmological constant. Using these results, we provide a new example of the Kerr-Schild double copy.

</details>


### [114] [Building a Quantum Black Hole Microstate: A Bulk Path Integral for a Heavy Virasoro Primary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14541)
*Chethan Krishnan,Rajdeep Mitra*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过在半无限实心圆柱上的level-k SL(2,R)×SL(2,R) Chern-Simons理论中，沿时间方向插入unitary principal continuous series中的Wilson线，构造了BTZ黑洞微观态的体（bulk）实现。该构造在τ=0切口处制备位于BTZ阈值之上的重Virasoro primary，其对应的半经典鞍点是具有奇异视界的BTZ黑洞；模不变性（modular invariance）决定的密度态将配分函数中的holonomy鞍点固定为具有光滑可收缩热循环的Euclid BTZ黑洞，即“模系综”（modular ensemble）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在从体构造角度实现BTZ黑洞微观态，特别是通过Chern-Simons理论与DS约化（Drinfel'd-Sokolov reduction）为高于BTZ阈值（BTZ threshold）的重Virasoro primary提供半经典对应，并解释为何永恒Lorentzian黑洞具有光滑视界对应的是“模系综”而非任意热系综。该工作也旨在阐明这类微观态不打破黑洞等距对称性，从而区别于fuzzball图像。

Method: 在半无限实心圆柱上的level-k SL(2,R)×SL(2,R) Chern-Simons理论中，于空间圆盘中心沿时间方向插入unitary principal continuous series中的Wilson线；利用DS约化将仿射模（affine module）转化为Virasoro模；在半经典极限下，DS约束变为渐近AdS₃引力中熟悉的DS规范；在大k极限使用WKB方法分析单个primary的鞍点，并通过对微观态求（grand-）canonical配分函数来研究温度与化学势对holonomy鞍点的约束，最终通过解析延拓获得对应的Lorentzian黑孔几何。

Result: 在τ=0切口处，该Euclid路径积分制备了BTZ阈值之上的重Virasoro primary的体对偶；半经典鞍点对应于具有奇异视界的BTZ黑洞。由模不变性决定的态密度将配分函数的holonomy鞍点固定为具有光滑可收缩热循环的Euclid BTZ黑洞，因此永恒Lorentzian黑洞对应的是由半经典奇异、无视界微观态构成的“模系综”。作者计算了该系综中的热涨落，并再现了半经典广义相对论预期的面积涨落形式；这些微观态不打破黑洞等距性。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了基于Chern-Simons理论和DS约化的BTZ黑洞微观态体构造，表明永恒黑洞的光滑视界对应的是“模系综”，其微观态半经典上是奇异视界、无水平等距破坏的态。所有讨论中不需要直接涉及理论的系综（ensembles of theories）。

Abstract: Consider level-$k$ $SL(2,R) \times SL(2,R)$ Chern-Simons theory on a semi-infinite solid cylinder, with a Wilson line in the unitary principal continuous series inserted along the time direction at the center of the spatial disc. We argue that at the $τ=0$ cut, this Euclidean path integral prepares the bulk dual of a heavy Virasoro primary above the BTZ threshold, with Drinfel'd-Sokolov (DS) reduction playing a crucial role in converting the affine module into a Virasoro module. Semi-classically, the DS constraint turns into the DS gauge, familiar from asymptotically AdS$_3$ gravity. Together with boundary gravitons, these primaries provide a bulk construction of BTZ black hole microstates at finite $k$. In the large-$k$ WKB limit, the saddle that controls an $individual$ primary is the BTZ black hole with a $singular$ horizon. We study the (grand-)canonical partition function of the microstates with density of states dictated by modular invariance. The temperature and chemical potential then fix the holonomy saddle to be the Euclidean BTZ black hole with a $smoothly$ contractible thermal cycle. A density of heavy primaries growing as $e^{2πγQP}$ would instead lead to a defect (or excess) saddle, except at the Cardy value $γ=1$ fixed by modular invariance. After analytic continuation, the eternal Lorentzian black hole with a smooth horizon is therefore not just any thermal ensemble, but the ``modular ensemble" of (semi-classically singular) horizonless microstates. We compute the thermal fluctuations in this ensemble, and reproduce the fluctuations in the area of the form expected from semi-classical general relativity. Ensembles of theories do not play a direct role in any of our discussions. We also note that these microstates, unlike fuzzballs, do $not$ break the isometries of the black hole.

</details>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Optical Characterization Measurements of Commercial Polyvinyltoluene Scintillators for Accurate Geant4 Detector Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13710)
*Fergus Martin Huggins,Micah S. Johnson,Matthew Quinn Buckner*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文首次报道了六种商用聚乙烯基甲苯（polyvinyltoluene, PVT）基塑料闪烁体的波长依赖折射率 n(λ) 和体吸收长度 L_abs(λ) 的全面测量结果（370-660 nm），并验证了精确光学参数对蒙特卡洛模拟和探测器设计的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 塑料闪烁体探测器的精确蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）依赖于波长依赖的光学特性，而此前缺乏对商用 PVT 基闪烁体这类特性的系统测量数据。

Method: 采用精密折射装置测量折射率，通过变厚度透射测量获取体吸收长度；研究对象为六种商用闪烁体（BC-400, BC-404, BC-408, BC-418, BC-422, BC-430），测量波长范围为370-660 nm。

Result: 测得的材料色散与制造商标称值及纯 PVT 文献值存在显著差异；对 BC-422 探测器的验证模拟显示，使用实测 n(λ) 比使用恒定制造商值时探测光子计数减少7.2%；成功测得了 BC-430 在大部分可见光谱范围的吸收长度（2.2-12.4 mm）及所有材料在372 nm处的吸收长度（21-38 mm），并对弱吸收配方在长波长处确定了超过750 mm的下限。

Conclusion: 精确的波长依赖光学特性数据对探测器设计与校准至关重要，测量结果揭示了制造商规范值的局限性，为改进塑料闪烁体的蒙特卡洛模拟精度提供了基础数据。

Abstract: Accurate Monte Carlo simulation of plastic scintillator detectors requires precise wavelength-dependent optical properties. We present the first comprehensive measurements of wavelength-dependent refractive index $n(λ)$ and bulk absorption length $L_{\mathrm{abs}}(λ)$ for six commercial polyvinyltoluene (PVT)-based scintillators (Saint Gobain BC-400, BC-404, BC-408, BC-418, BC-422, and BC-430) across the 370-660 nm range. Using a precision refraction apparatus and variable-thickness transmission measurements, we characterize material-specific dispersion that deviates substantially from both the manufacturer specification and literature values for pure PVT. Validation simulations of a BC-422 detector demonstrate a 7.2% reduction in detected photon counts when using our measured $n(λ)$ compared to the constant manufacturer value, highlighting the practical importance of accurate optical property data for detector design and calibration. We successfully measure absorption lengths for BC-430 across most of the visible spectrum (2.2-12.4 mm) and for all materials at 372 nm (21-38 mm), while establishing lower bounds exceeding 750 mm for weakly absorbing formulations at longer wavelengths.

</details>


### [116] [High-Frequency Thermal Noise in Michelson Interferometers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16710)
*Daniel Grass,Sander M. Vermeulen,Ian A. O. MacMillan,Lee McCuller*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文为兆赫兹（MHz）频段的光学实验开发了更普适的热噪声模型，涵盖基底与镀层的布朗噪声（Brownian noise）、热弹性噪声（thermoelastic noise）以及镀层热折射噪声（thermorefractive noise），并与低频建模和Holometer实验数据验证后应用于在建的GQuEST实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高功率光学实验（如引力波探测器）目前主要受量子噪声（quantum noise）限制，但采用光子计数读出的新实验将规避散粒噪声（shot noise），使热噪声成为主导噪声源。然而现有建模中使用的准静态近似（quasistatic approximation）在兆赫兹频段不再适用，需要开发更通用的热噪声模型以精确表征信号并设计更灵敏的实验。

Method: 作者开发了更普适的基底与镀层机械（布朗）噪声、基底与镀层热弹性噪声以及镀层热折射噪声模型，适用于兆赫兹频段。通过与以往低频建模结果及已运行实验Holometer的高频谱比较来验证模型，随后将新模型应用于正在建造中的GQuEST实验。

Result: 新模型在低频区域与既有模型一致，并在高频段与Holometer实验数据相符，验证了模型的有效性；模型被成功应用于GQuEST实验的设计与信号表征。

Conclusion: 所开发的通用热噪声模型填补了兆赫兹频段建模空白，为利用光子计数读出的新型干涉仪（如GQuEST）精确表征弱高频信号、设计更灵敏实验奠定了基础。

Abstract: Thermal noise in optics constrains the precision of optical experiments, including Michelson interferometers, optical clocks, and optomechanical sensors. While high-power optical experiments such as gravitational-wave detectors are currently limited mainly by quantum noise, new experiments incorporating recent insights from quantum metrology are being developed that evade the quantum shot noise background. In particular, Michelson interferometers that use photon-counting readout will look for weak, high-frequency signals. Since shot noise is no longer the dominant noise source with these readout schemes, it is important to accurately model thermal noise to characterize signals and design more sensitive experiments. However, previous modeling uses approximations that are no longer valid in these frequency regimes. In the MHz band, the quasistatic approximation does not apply. We therefore develop more general models of substrate and coating mechanical (Brownian) noise, substrate and coating thermoelastic noise, and coating thermorefractive noise. We validate the models with comparisons to previous low-frequency modeling and high-frequency spectra from an experiment that has already taken data, the Holometer. We then apply the new models to GQuEST, an experiment under construction.

</details>


<div id='math.OA'></div>

# math.OA [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Choi--Jamiołkowski-type isomorphisms for von Neumann algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13750)
*Marcin Marciniak,Michał Cholewiak*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 本文研究了Choi–Jamiołkowski同构在无穷维自由度数系统（即III型von Neumann代数）中的推广。作者证明了对于任意von Neumann代数M和N，正规完全有界映射的空间与M与N的反代数（opposite algebra）的空间张量积的前对偶空间之间存在典范序同构，并证明了对M的自然性要求使得用N自身替代反代数的条件既是充分的也是必要的，从而表明Connes构造的III型因子不存在Choi–Jamiołkowski对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Choi–Jamiołkowski同构是有限维量子信息论的基础工具，它将完全正映射与两体态联系起来。然而，对于具有无穷多个自由度的系统，其模型为III型von Neumann代数，此时既没有迹（trace）也没有密度矩阵（density matrix），因此需要重新表述这一同构关系。

Method: 作者在任意von Neumann代数M和N的设定下，建立了正规完全有界映射从M到N的前对偶的空间与M和N的反代数的空间张量积的前对偶之间的典范序同构，并在此同构下将完全正性对应为正值性。通过利用自然性（naturality）条件分析用N替代反代数的可行性，作者证明了需要反同构（anti-isomorphism）的存在，且此条件不仅是充分的而且是必要的。

Result: 证明了若要求映射识别对M具有自然性，则用N本身替代反代数所需的M与自身的反同构条件是充分且必要的。由于每个自反同构的M都存在平凡的同构，因此某些自然性要求是不可避免的。此外，作者表明所有正规映射在算子范数（operator norm）下的空间对该目的而言严格过大，且在此设定下完全正性并不蕴含完全有界性。

Conclusion: 研究得出对Connes构造的III型因子不存在Choi–Jamiołkowski对应，这揭示了无穷维系统与有限维量子信息论之间的根本差异，并为该同构在算子代数框架下的推广设定了明确的边界条件。

Abstract: The Choi--Jamiołkowski isomorphism identifies completely positive maps with bipartite states and underlies much of finite-dimensional quantum information theory. For systems with infinitely many degrees of freedom, modelled by von Neumann algebras of type III, neither traces nor density matrices are available, and the isomorphism has to be reformulated. We show that for arbitrary von Neumann algebras $\mathcal{M}$ and $\mathcal{N}$ there is a canonical order isomorphism between the space of normal completely bounded maps from $\mathcal{M}$ into the predual of $\mathcal{N}$ and the predual of the spatial tensor product of $\mathcal{M}$ with the \emph{opposite} algebra of $\mathcal{N}$; under this identification complete positivity corresponds to positivity. Replacing the opposite algebra by $\mathcal{N}$ itself requires an anti-isomorphism of $\mathcal{N}$ with itself, and we prove that this condition is not only sufficient but also necessary, provided the identification is required to be natural in $\mathcal{M}$. Some such requirement is unavoidable, since for every $\mathcal{M}$ anti-isomorphic to itself an isomorphism exists for trivial reasons. Consequently no Choi--Jamiołkowski correspondence exists for the type III factors constructed by Connes. Along the way we show that the space of all normal maps, taken with the operator norm, is strictly too large for this purpose, and that complete positivity does not force complete boundedness in this setting.

</details>
